# 🧬 Road to Self-Hosting — Pre-Flight Plan

> v0.5 Target: Morphic compiler written in Morphic
> Status: PLANNING — DO NOT EXECUTE WITHOUT REVIEW

---

## ⚠️ RISK ASSESSMENT

### What Can Go Wrong

| # | Risk | Severity | Likelihood | Impact |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | **Regression**: Hasil sintesis lebih buruk dari kode Rust asli — compiler jadi lebih lambat, lebih buggy, atau menghasilkan output yang salah | 🔴 Critical | Medium | Compiler rusak, semua downstream project terpengaruh |
| R2 | **Bootstrap paradox**: Menggunakan compiler yang belum terverifikasi untuk memverifikasi hasil sintesisnya sendiri — circular trust, error propagation | 🔴 Critical | High | Tidak ada kebenaran dasar (ground truth) |
| R3 | **Perf collapse**: Compiler hasil sintesis 10x lebih lambat dari Rust asli, bikin development cycle tidak usable | 🟠 High | High | Developer tidak bisa iterate, project mati |
| R4 | **Architecture mismatch**: Komponen hasil sintesis tidak compatible satu sama lain karena spec masing-masing tidak cukup ketat | 🟠 High | Medium | Komponen tidak bisa dirangkai, integrasi gagal |
| R5 | **Constraint explosion**: Spesifikasi formal untuk komponen sekompleks parser/type checker jadi terlalu besar buat MCTS + Z3, synthesis timeout atau memory overflow | 🔴 Critical | High | Synthesis gagal total, tidak ada output |
| R6 | **IR expressiveness gap**: Intermediate Representation tidak cukup kaya untuk mengekspresikan implementasi compiler-level (parsing, tree walking, code generation) | 🟠 High | Medium | Hasil sintesis tidak optimal, butuh workaround manual |
| R7 | **Test blindness**: Generated code lulus Z3 verification tapi gagal di real-world scenario yang tidak ter-encode di constraints | 🟡 Medium | High | Bug subtle yang hanya muncul di production |
| R8 | **Dependency entanglement**: Komponen baru bergantung pada komponen lama spesifik, bikin tidak bisa ganti komponen satu per satu | 🟡 Medium | Medium | Blocked progress, harus rewrite banyak komponen sekaligus |
| R9 | **LLM hallucination amplification**: LLM menghasilkan kode yang terlihat benar tapi punya bug — dan karena Z3 verifikasi hanya sebaik constraints-nya, bug lolos | 🟠 High | Medium | False confidence, bug tersembunyi |
| R10 | **Token budget explosion**: Synthesis compiler butuh konteks besar — MCTS tree + LLM prompt + Z3 queries bisa melebihi 1M token window | 🟡 Medium | Low | Synthesis gagal di tengah jalan |

---

## 🛡️ SAFETY NETS

### For Every Risk, A Mitigation

| Risk | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| R1 Regression | **Golden test suite**: Sebelum ganti komponen apapun, rekam output compiler Rust asli untuk 100+ input spec. Generated compiler HARUS menghasilkan output byte-identical dengan compiler asli untuk test suite yang sama. |
| R2 Bootstrap paradox | **Cross-validation**: Untuk setiap komponen hasil sintesis, jalankan test suite yang sama pada compiler Rust original vs compiler hasil sintesis. HASIL HARUS IDENTIK. Compiler hasil sintesis TIDAK BOLEH jadi satu-satunya source of truth. |
| R3 Perf collapse | **Benchmark gate**: Setiap komponen hasil sintesis harus di-benchmark. Batas toleransi: max 2x slower dari Rust original. Lebih dari itu → reject. |
| R4 Architecture mismatch | **Interface contracts**: Sebelum sintesis, tulis `.morph` interface specs yang mendefinisikan type signature + pre/postcondition untuk setiap komponen boundary. Synthesis hanya di-allow jika memenuhi interface contract. |
| R5 Constraint explosion | **Decompose**: Jangan sintesis komponen besar sekaligus. Pecah jadi sub-fungsi ≤200 lines Rust. Synthesis per sub-fungsi, lalu compose. |
| R6 IR expressiveness | **IR audit**: Sebelum synthesis, pastikan setiap target Rust pattern bisa di-represent di IR. Kalau ada pattern yang tidak bisa → tambahkan IR node type dulu. |
| R7 Test blindness | **Fuzzing**: Generate random `.morph` inputs, compile dengan compiler original vs compiler hasil sintesis, bandingkan output. Minimum 10,000 random inputs. |
| R8 Dependency entanglement | **Interface-first**: Definisikan trait/interface tiap komponen DULU sebelum implementasi. Generated code harus implement interface, bukan import konkrit. |
| R9 LLM hallucination | **Triple-check**: LLM candidate → MCTS scoring → Z3 verification → Golden test suite → Human review. Tidak ada satu checkpoint pun yang boleh skip. |
| R10 Token budget | **Multi-turn synthesis**: Jangan satu prompt besar. Sintesis per sub-komponen, dengan hasil sebelumnya sebagai context. |

## 🔄 ROLLBACK STRATEGY

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                 ROLLBACK PROTOCOL                            │
│                                                              │
│  Level 1 — Component rollback:                               │
│    Kalau satu komponen hasil sintesis gagal test:            │
│    → Revert file itu ke versi Rust asli                      │
│    → git checkout src/spec/parser.rs                         │
│    → Investigasi kenapa spec-nya kurang ketat                │
│                                                              │
│  Level 2 — Full rollback:                                    │
│    Kalau beberapa komponen gagal atau integrasi kacau:       │
│    → git revert ke commit sebelum self-hosting                │
│    → Semua komponen kembali ke Rust asli                     │
│    → Re-evaluate strategy                                    │
│                                                              │
│  Level 3 — Emergency stop:                                   │
│    Kalau synthesis corruption terdeteksi:                    │
│    → Lock branch master, tidak ada push baru                 │
│    → Audit semua generated code                              │
│    → Tidak ada generated code yang masuk ke main branch      │
│      sebelum lulus full review                               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Branch Strategy

```
master (stable, semua Rust asli)
  │
  ├── feat/self-host-tokenizer    ← tokenizer.morph → tokenizer.rs
  ├── feat/self-host-typeck       ← typeck.morph → typeck.rs
  ├── feat/self-host-codegen      ← codegen.morph → codegen.rs
  │      │
  │      └── Setiap branch punya:
  │          ├── spec/*.morph           (spec)
  │          ├── generated/*.rs         (output synthesis)
  │          ├── tests/*_verify.rs      (cross-validation tests)
  │          └── BENCHMARK.md           (perf comparison)
  │
  └── feat/self-host-integration   ← Semua komponen dirangkai
```

---

## 🧪 TESTING STRATEGY

### The 4-Layer Test Pyramid for Self-Hosting

```
        ┌──────────┐
        │  FUZZING │  10,000 random inputs, cross-compare
        │ 10K runs │  original vs synthesized output
        ├──────────┤
        │INTEGRATION│  Full pipeline: spec → parse → typeck
        │   TESTS   │  → synthesize → verify → codegen
        ├──────────┤
        │  GOLDEN   │  100+ known .morph specs, recorded
        │  OUTPUTS  │  outputs. Must be byte-identical.
        ├──────────┤
        │   Z3      │  Per-constraint formal verification.
        │VERIFY     │  UNSAT = proven correct.
        └──────────┘
```

### Test Harness Design

```morphic
// Example: test harness spec for tokenizer
spec test_tokenizer_equivalence {
    input: source: String
    output: Result<List<Token>, ParseError>

    constraint: output == original_rust_tokenizer(source)

    // Generated from 100+ recorded test cases
    test "keyword spec" { input: "spec"; expect: Ok([Spec]) }
    test "generic fn" { input: "fn<T>"; expect: Ok([Fn, Lt, Ident("T"), Gt]) }
    // ...
}
```

---

## 📊 SUCCESS CRITERIA

| # | Criteria | Threshold | Measurement |
|---|---|---|---|
| SC1 | **Z3 verification** | 100% constraints pass | `cargo test` in CI |
| SC2 | **Golden test equivalence** | 100% output match | Automated diff against recorded outputs |
| SC3 | **Performance** | ≤ 2x slower than Rust original | Benchmark suite (criterion.rs or similar) |
| SC4 | **Fuzzing pass rate** | ≥ 99.9% match across 10K inputs | Randomized input generation |
| SC5 | **Self-compile** | Morphic can compile its own specs | `morphic build tokenizer.morph` produces valid tokenizer |
| SC6 | **Bootstrap** | Generated compiler can compile a spec → generates correct code | Full roundtrip test |
| SC7 | **No regression** | Existing 25 tests still pass | `cargo test` unchanged |

## 🪜 EXECUTION MILESTONES

### M0: IR Audit (before writing any specs)
- [x] Review semua 32 IRNode variants — cukupkah untuk compiler-level code?
- [x] Identifikasi Rust patterns di compiler saat ini yang TIDAK bisa di-represent di IR
- [x] Tambahkan IR node types jika perlu
- [x] **Gate**: Semua pattern expressible di IR

### M1: Tokenizer (simplest component)
- [x] Tulis `tokenizer.morph` spec (~100 lines)
- [x] Tulis golden test suite (50+ recorded outputs)
- [x] LLM seed → MCTS → Z3 verify → codegen
- [ ] Cross-validate: generated tokenizer vs Rust original
- [ ] Benchmark: must be ≤ 2x slower
- [ ] **Gate**: 100% golden test pass + Z3 verified

> **Gate state (Phase 80 re-run, 2026-11-22):** the M1 formal gate stays OPEN — re-run in full and NOT passed; this note replaces the 2026-11-21 deferral note, whose conditions are now discharged. The deferral (owner decision, 2026-11-21) held the gate open until the language had (1) method calls in spec expressions, (2) a field-access node in the IR, and (3) record literals — plus, per the Phase 72 audit's sufficiency finding, (4) a Z3 Char scalar encoding — so the gate could pass in full (100% golden + Z3 verified) without compromising its standard. All four were built and ratified as D1a–D5a (Phases 73–79), and Phase 80 executed the checklist as written. **What passes:** the spec is written and checks whole (18/18 tests in-spec); the golden suite exists (thousands of recorded outputs); the pipeline stages all execute on the module; and the three Char classifiers pass the gate line in full — synthesis-crowned **Z3-Verified** winners, executed agreement **774/774** each, in-spec tests 3/3 each — the clause the deferral was written to protect, now met at the full standard, not by executed-agreement substitution. **What remains, named:** `skip_whitespace` is Verified-crowned but its golden half is not 100% (its spec states only two postconditions — a spec-strength fact, recorded since Phase 47); `peek` has a hand-verified body but no synthesis-crowned winner (no seed family admits its guard rows); `next_char`'s verification is blocked by the missing complementary-guard resolver denotation for `peek`; `tokenize_one` is **spec-inconsistent at end-of-input** (proven mechanically in Phase 80: its first row refutes the output its own golden test demands) and additionally carries a generation gap; `tokenize_all` has a verifying degenerate body but 0/8 golden agreement and no crowned winner; cross-validation against the Rust original is therefore partial, and the ≤ 2× benchmark was never run (no harness exists, and there is as yet no complete generated tokenizer to time). The three priced remainders — (i) the complementary-guard resolver builder, (ii) the `tokenize_one` amendment decision (options in the Phase 80 note; amending is owner territory and nothing was amended here), (iii) the `tokenize_one`/`tokenize_all` generation campaign, sequenced (ii) → (i) → (iii) — are recorded in the Phase 80 note below. The gate line stays unticked; no standard was lowered to tick it. **Phase 81 update (2026-11-22):** the owner ratified all three remainders the same day (amendment A for (ii)); remainder (i) + (i-b) are now built — `peek` denotes through the new complementary-guard builder and crowns a Verified winner, and `next_char` both verifies (hand body) and crowns a Verified winner through the record-completion seed; see the Phase 81 note below. Remainders (ii) and (iii) remain, sequenced as Phases 82 and 83–84, with the gate re-run as Phase 85. **Phase 82 update (2026-11-22):** remainder (ii) is now APPLIED — amendment A exactly as ratified (row 1 gains row 2's end-of-input disjunct; row 2 untouched); the Phase 80 contradiction is flipped to admission by the re-stated proof, and the mirror-body measurement states Phase 83's exact contract — the mirror's outputs are refuted at exactly two battery classes (the whitespace-only-remainder corner via row 1; unexpected characters via the still-unamended row 2), where the rows jointly admit only an Ok carrying exactly one more token. Remainder (iii) remains, as Phases 83–84; the gate re-run is Phase 85. **Phase 83 update (2026-11-22):** the round-2 amendment feasibility the Phase 82 contract called for is now MEASURED (nothing amended): candidate A′ (row 1's disjunct reads the OUTPUT state's pos) licenses the whitespace-only-remainder class, and candidate R2-α (row 2 gains a not-a-token-start Err license over the indexed char, in callee and direct forms) licenses the unexpected-character class — jointly, the mirror's outputs are admitted at all seven battery classes, with exactly one surviving subclass (whitespace-then-bad-char, e.g. `" 5"`, which no spec-side formulation reaches: the peek form over-licenses, the skip form is unencodable, and a skip denotation is underivable and would not inline past the While boundary anyway). A priced option set (A′ + R2-α recommended) is recorded in the Phase 83 note for owner ratification; the generation campaign follows whichever rows are ratified, and the gate re-run remains the closing phase. **Phase 84 update (2026-11-22):** remainder (ii) is now closed at round 2 — the owner ratified Phase 83's option 1 (A′ + R2-α, callee form) and it is APPLIED: row 1's disjunct reads the output state's pos, row 2 gains the not-a-token-start Err license; the re-stated proofs pin the mirror's outputs admitted at all seven battery classes with the teeth refuted and exactly one surviving deviation (the `" 5"` subclass, priced in Phase 83 as unreachable from the spec side). The instruments record one movement beyond expectation: `tokenize_one` now crowns a Verified winner (tpr 0.500; its pool holds 4 Verified and zero refuted candidates per run) where Phase 82 measured an Inconclusive crown and 12 refuted — executed agreement stays 0/10 on the standing emitter class. Remainder (iii) remains, as Phases 85–86; the gate re-run is Phase 87.

> **Phase 72 (2026-11-22, branch `docs/m1-features-audit`) — the three features audited and priced; owner chose to build them (2026-11-22).** The audit is a standalone document, [`M1_FEATURES_AUDIT.md`](M1_FEATURES_AUDIT.md) (the M0_IR_AUDIT precedent): per feature, the measured current state, the exact tokenizer expressions waiting on it, candidate designs, and five owner decisions — **D1** method calls (native node + closed builtin table recommended), **D2** IR field access (native node + record values in interpreter/Z3 recommended), **D3** record literals (named `Type { … }` construction recommended), **D4** the entangled token-column ruling (START normative recommended), **D5** the audit's headline finding: the three features are necessary but **not sufficient** for the gate as stated — the Char classifiers cannot be Z3-verified under any of them (Char is Opaque to the encoder), so a Z3 Char scalar encoding is priced as a fourth workstream (recommended) rather than the gate being quietly re-scoped. Implementation is planned as Phases 73–80 (AST/parser → typechecker → interpreter values → Z3 encodings → native emitters under a scoped unfreeze → Codegen-spec fallout + bootstrap cascade → tokenizer completion → M1 gate re-run). Docs-only phase; no production code changed, no spec re-authored. Decisions D1–D5 await the owner's letters.

> **Ratified (2026-11-22):** the owner accepted all five audit recommendations — **D1a** (native `Expr::MethodCall` + closed builtin table: `Result::{is_ok, is_err, unwrap}`, `List::len`; UFCS recorded as a future extension, not now), **D2a** (`IRNode::FieldAccess` + `Value::Record` + `Sym::Record`), **D3a** (named record construction `Type { field: value, … }`), **D4a** (token columns START normative; the hand-written tokenizer's `push_token` is corrected in Phase 79), **D5a** (the Z3 Char scalar encoding joins the campaign, landing in Phase 76). Phases 73–80 proceed as priced in the audit.

> **Phase 73 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-73-ast-parser`) — AST + parser for the three features; the phase's evidence is in.** Spec AST: `Expr::MethodCall { receiver, name, args }` and `Expr::RecordLit { type_name, fields }` appended at the **end** of `Expr` (SpecExpr kind codes 13/14 appended, nothing renumbered — CharLit's code-less F4 precedent noted in the variant docs). IR: `IRNode::FieldAccess { base, field }` (kind 28) and `IRNode::RecordLit { type_name, fields }` (kind 29) appended at the end of `IRNode`; kind glue appended in both composed-emitter assemblies. Parser: the postfix loop is now one shared chain after **any** primary — `.name(` parses as a method call, `.name` as a field, `[…]` as an index, composing after call results and parenthesized expressions (the acceptance shape `output.unwrap().tokens` parses; previously nothing after a call result could postfix and `output.is_err()` stranded on the `()`); `TypeName { field: value, … }` parses as a record literal in any expression position, including nested in lists and inside `Ok(...)` — the bare `{ … }` form stays a parse error (D3b was not ratified; no grammar ambiguity was found: in expression position an identifier followed by `{` has no other reading, blocks not being expressions and the spec/type/test braces being consumed by their own parsers). Lowerings: `expr_to_ir` now lowers `Field` → `FieldAccess` and `Index` → `Index` **for real** (both fell into the Hole arm before — the direct cause of Phase 48's 24/24 seedless record-spec runs) and `RecordLit` → `IRNode::RecordLit`; **`MethodCall` lowering stays Hole, pinned by a named test** — the IR kind table has no method-call kind, and method semantics land where the node is consumed (typechecker Phase 74, Z3 Phase 76), both working on the spec `Expr`. Every exhaustive match rustc enumerated gained a minimal honest arm naming its owning phase: typechecker (`RecordLit` types as its named type, field-set validation is Phase 74; `MethodCall` checks subexpressions, then errors naming Phase 74 — no sound coarse type exists to defer to), interpreter (`FieldAccess`/`RecordLit` are NotEvaluable until `Value::Record` lands in Phase 75), Z3 + the `encodable.rs` mirror in lockstep (unencodable until `Sym::Record` lands in Phase 76), the native Rust emitter (the loud `unsupported_expr` fallback — no existing byte moves; real rendering is Phase 77's scoped unfreeze), the composed emitters (`rust_unsupported_text`, the Alloc treatment, until the Phase 78 kind rows), and the spec-Expr fixed-text kind matches (13/14 route through the standing default, exactly as the native wildcard renders them). Structural walks recurse for real (mentions-var / calls-indexed / expr-same, selector child walkers, depth/count estimators, both literal-type searches, the LLM prompt formatter, the probes' local copies); body identity is Debug-derived and needed no change; the wildcard sites (free-var collection, substitution) fail conservatively on the new shapes, audited by hand. LANGUAGE_REFERENCE §11 now documents postfix chaining, method calls (the closed D1a table; UFCS recorded as a future extension) and record literals; §14 cross-references the construction form. **Tests first:** 6 parser tests (the dropped `tokenize_one`/`tokenize_all` constraints verbatim from `acda3f9`; postfix after calls/parens; the mirror-only test shapes re-authored in the D3a named form; a record literal in constraint position; the bare form rejected) + 4 lowering tests — all failed before (variants absent) and pass now. Scratch evidence (the example file untouched, per the audit): a fully restored tokenizer module in /tmp parses whole; its record-literal subset (peek/next_char/skip_whitespace + 5 tests) passes `morphic check` **in full**; the whole module's check stops at `tokenize_one`'s first method call with the Phase 74 deferral error — the recorded boundary, not a surprise. **Suites: 432/0/26 default, 432/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 359/0/19 no-Z3** — the +10 delta over the 422/422/349 baselines is exactly the new tests in every configuration; no pre-existing test moved. Next: Phase 74 (typechecker: the D1a table, the Field arm's declared-field resolution, RecordLit field-set checking).

> **Phase 74 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-74-typechecker`) — the typechecker for the three features; both Phase 73 deferral pins are closed.** `Expr::MethodCall` resolves against the D1a closed builtin table, keyed by the receiver's type after alias expansion (`StepResult` is seen as the `Result<...>` it aliases): `Result<T, E>` has `is_ok() -> Bool`, `is_err() -> Bool`, `unwrap() -> T`; `List<T>` has `len() -> Int`; all four are zero-argument. Unknown method names, wrong-family methods, methods on any other receiver type, and arguments to a builtin are each type errors naming the method and the receiver type; a receiver the checker cannot resolve (the `_` unknown marker, a generic parameter, an undeclared named type) keeps the standing coarse deferral and yields the unknown marker. No UFCS, no user-defined methods, no hooks for either — the table is consulted exactly as ratified. `Expr::RecordLit` validates against the declared record (D3a): the name must resolve to a `TypeKind::Record` declaration (alias/newtype names are followed); a duplicated field, an unknown field, and a missing field are three distinct errors each naming the field; every field value's type must match its declared field type at the checker's standing coarse precision (aliases expanded on both sides — `kind: 0` satisfies the declared `kind: TokenKind`); an undeclared type name and a name declared as a non-record are two further distinct errors. `Expr::Field` (D2a): on a base whose type resolves to a module-declared record the arm now returns the **declared field type** (before this phase it returned the base's type, unchecked) and rejects unknown fields naming the field and the record; a base provably not a record (primitive, container, name declared as a non-record) is an error; unresolvable bases keep the pre-Phase-74 deferral, pinned by a test. **The audit's named risk — the Field tightening rejecting a spec somewhere in the standing corpora — was measured and did not occur:** every corpus check in the suites stands (the self-host modules work over Int kind codes, not record fields; the tokenizer is the only record-field user). One adjunct the headline evidence forced, recorded here rather than smuggled: the restored tests are written with `Ok(...)` / `Err(...)`, which the checker's `Call` arm rejected as undefined functions — Phase 73's check stopped at `tokenize_one`'s constraints and never reached them. The `Call` arm now types `Ok(v) -> Result<v, _>` and `Err(e) -> Result<_, e>`, exactly parallel to the standing `Some` / `None` Option constructors. **Headline evidence:** the fully restored tokenizer module scratch (rebuilt in /tmp exactly as Phase 73 built it — the current module's types and constraints, `acda3f9`'s dropped `tokenize_one`/`tokenize_all` constraints verbatim, the 9 mirror tests in the D3a named form; `examples/functional_tokenizer.morph` untouched) passes `morphic check` **whole** — the method-call constraints that stopped Phase 73 included. Six mutated scratch variants are each rejected with the intended message: `output.len()` on a `Result` (wrong family), `output.is_err(1)` (argument to a zero-arg builtin), a `Token` literal missing `col`, one carrying an extra `bogus` field, one with `line` duplicated, and a test input naming the undeclared record `Nope`. **Tests first:** 19 new typeck tests (17 failed before the implementation; the undeclared-base deferral pin and the unknown-method pin held throughout). **Suites: 451/0/26 default, 451/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 378/0/19 no-Z3** — the +19 delta over the 432/432/359 baselines is exactly the new tests in every configuration; no pre-existing test moved. The pinned Phase 73 lowering test (`expr_to_ir_method_call_lowers_to_hole_pending_74_76`) is untouched — lowering was never this phase. Next: Phase 75 (interpreter values: `Value::Record`, `FieldAccess`/record-construction evaluation, record-literal binding).
> **Phase 75 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-75-interpreter`) — interpreter values for the new nodes; the record specs' tests bind and are judged in-process for the first time.** `Value` gains `Record { type_name, fields }` (D2a/D3a — the type name rides along, per the D3a self-describing literal; equality is the derived structural equality, so field written order is part of it, matching how the module writes its literals) and the constructor values `Ok` / `Err` / `Some` / `None` — **measured first: no Result/Option representation existed** (the enum was Int/Float/Bool/Str/Char/Unit/List), so nothing was duplicated. Evaluation: `IRNode::RecordLit` constructs a record (fields in written order; a `Return` inside a field value unwinds the whole evaluation, exactly as inside a `Collection`); `IRNode::FieldAccess` projects by name — an unknown field (naming the field and the record type), a non-record base (naming the field), and a base that itself fails (its own reason propagated) are each a named `NotEvaluable`, never a panic or a guessed value; `eval_call` builds the four constructor values with exact-arity named failures. **Method calls:** `eval_method_call` implements the D1a table on values — `is_ok`/`is_err`/`unwrap` on Result values, `len` on a List — with `unwrap` on an `Err` value as the named eval failure the interpreter's failure semantics require, and unknown/wrong-family/argument-carrying calls each naming the method. It is wired into `value_of_expr` — **the codebase's only spec-Expr evaluator for judging** (measured: constraints are never interpreter-judged; Z3 and the compiled runner own them) — as a direct arm on closed receivers, because `expr_to_ir` lowers `MethodCall` to the pinned Hole; a method call on a variable therefore still does not map, and one sitting *under* a field/index composite still routes through the fallback into that Hole (no restored test has that shape). **The pinned lowering test is untouched — it stands for Phase 76**, where the Z3 Result/Option forms land. **Test binding needed no change in `bind_test_env` itself** — it delegates to `value_of_expr`, whose `expr_to_ir` + `eval` fallback now carries record literals, records nested in lists, and `Ok(...)`-wrapped records; that is measured by the headline test, not assumed. **Residuals, recorded and deliberately not widened:** `TryPropagate` stays `NotEvaluable` (its reason is corrected — it no longer claims "no Result values"; `?` unwinding is control flow this evaluator does not model), and `Match` constructor patterns still never match in-process (`bind_pattern`'s doc now states this as the subset residual it is, its old "values are never constructors" premise being false since this phase) — no judging-coverage rule moved. **Headline evidence** (the restored module scratch of Phases 73/74, embedded verbatim in the test; `examples/functional_tokenizer.morph` untouched): all **9 record tests bind in-process** (Phase 48: "nothing to bind `state` to" — all five specs unbindable). Judged with hand-written bodies transcribing the mirror in `tests/tokenizer_proof.rs`: **peek 2/2 passed, next_char 1/1 passed**; **skip_whitespace binds 2/2** and its loop body is honestly `BodyNotEvaluable` on both (evaluable 2, passed 0 — the While stays outside the subset); **tokenize_one / tokenize_all bind 2/2** and are judged with partial bodies — `Ok(state)` and `Ok(state.tokens)` — each scoring **[Failed, Passed]** (the end-of-input/empty tests pass; the token-producing tests fail because the bodies do not tokenize — reported, not massaged). The three Char classifiers, read from the standing example file, pass **3/3 each** under mirror-transcribed bodies, and peek/next_char were cross-checked against the mirror on inputs the spec tests do not carry (peek at pos 1 of `"hi"` = `Some('i')`, at end = `None`; next_char over `'\n'` advances line and resets col) — exact agreement. **Tests first:** 11 new eval tests (all 11 failed against the stubbed implementation for the recorded reasons; the 24 pre-existing eval tests never moved). **Suites: 462/0/26 default, 462/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 389/0/19 no-Z3** — the +11 delta over the 451/451/378 baselines is exactly the new tests in every configuration; no pre-existing test moved. Next: Phase 76 (Z3 encodings: `Sym::Record`, the Result/Option tag+payload forms, the D1 method arms, the D5a Char scalar encoding, the `encodable.rs` mirror in lockstep).

> **Phase 76 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-76-z3`) — Z3 encodings for the new nodes + D5a; the classifiers are Z3-verified, and every remaining tokenizer Inconclusive has a named reason.** Plumbing first: the verifier previously saw only functions, so `SpecIndex` gains the module's type declarations (`SpecIndex::from_module`, `record_fields` following alias/newtype chains with a cycle guard, mirroring the typechecker's resolution) and `Verifier::with_module` judges against them; indexes built without declarations degrade to exactly the pre-phase behavior. **Records (D2a):** `Sym::Record { type_name, fields }` — the stored name is the RESOLVED declaration name on both construction paths, so an alias-written literal compares equal to a parameter-bound record; parameters bind per-field symbolic values by declared type (fresh constants named by dotted path, `state.pos`, `output.is_ok`, …) with a depth bound of 8 past which self-referential records bind Opaque. `Field` projects the named field's Sym and `RecordLit` constructs, in both `sym_eval` (candidate bodies) and `encode_expr` (constraints); an unknown field, a non-record base, or a record literal against an undeclared type is unencodable, never a guess. Record equality is fieldwise **by name**; different declared type names compare constantly false (the interpreter's structural equality includes the type name), same name with mismatched field sets is unencodable. **Result/Option:** `Sym::Result`/`Sym::Option` carry a `Tag` — literal `True`/`False` or `Free(Bool)` — plus payload slots that are absent ONLY under the corresponding literal tag (the invariant that makes literal-side equality need no payload term). `is_ok`/`is_err` encode as tag comparisons; `unwrap` encodes as the Ok payload — sound for the guarded shapes the tokenizer uses, and **the one named unencodable case is an unwrap whose receiver is a literal `Err(...)` construction** (no Ok payload exists to denote); `Ok`/`Err`/`Some`/`None` calls encode on both sides at exact arity; payload slots merge through conditionals via ite. **Lists bind as `Sym::ListLen(Int)` — length only**, uniformly (parameters, record fields, payloads): `len` reads the term, element access and list equality stay unencodable — a length is not the list, and pretending otherwise would be unsound. Measured exposure: `count_matching`'s verdicts are unchanged (its constraints are Forall, unencodable regardless), `is_sorted`'s length constraint becomes encodable while its Forall constraints keep every verdict Inconclusive, and the Codegen module declares no record types while the M3/M4 harnesses judge with declaration-free indexes — identical behavior there, all confirmed by the standing suites. **Char (D5a):** `Sym::Char(Int)` is the Unicode scalar value; parameters bind free Int constants, literals are scalar constants, and `verify_constraint` asserts the **range side-condition** (0 ≤ c ≤ 0x10FFFF ∧ ¬(0xD800 ≤ c ≤ 0xDFFF)) for every Char term reachable from the parameter values, walking record/Option/Result/List payloads. Char is sort-strict — it never compares or computes as Int; only the six comparisons on (Char, Char) encode. Decisiveness is pinned by probes: `output == (c <= '\u{10FFFF}')` and `output == (c <= '\u{D7FF}' || c >= '\u{E000}')` verify with body `true` ONLY via the side-condition, and an Int control (`n <= 1114111`) is refuted, proving the condition is Char-specific. (The parser itself rejects surrogate literals — `'\u{D800}'` is not writable Morphic — so the gap probe uses the representable boundary scalars.) **The string-index bridge (the audit's flagged top risk):** the safe z3 crate is 0.20.0 and exposes `String::at` but no `string_to_code`/`string_from_code`, and the raw context handle is not public — the Int-scalar bridge is **not reachable through the public API**, so the sound fragment is a dedicated form, `Sym::CharStr` = `s.at(i)`, a length-≤1 string. It supports the six comparisons against another CharStr (string order on length-≤1 strings IS scalar order) and against a **constant** Char (converted to its 1-char string); the out-of-bounds empty string sorts below every real char and equals none — a documented totalization of the same class as the codebase's accepted Z3 Div/Mod totalization versus interpreter failure. **Named boundary: an indexed char compared against a SYMBOLIC Char parameter is unencodable** — that is the comparison the sequence sort cannot make, and no tokenizer constraint needs it (peek compares indexed chars against literals and `None()`/`Some(...)` shapes only). A constant index into a literal string stays **exact** — the real scalar, or unencodable out-of-bounds, no totalization where the value is known; literal detection itself needed measuring: `as_string()` returns `Some("")` for a free constant and for a concatenation (all four term shapes are AstKind App), so it is not a literal test — the encoder uses term identity against a rebuilt literal, which measured exact on all four shapes. **`encodable.rs` moved in lockstep** (the W1 method): `Abs` gains `Char` (with numeral-ness tracked — literal/constant-index Known, parameter/ite Unknown), `CharStr`, `ListLen`, `Record`, and `Option`/`Result` with tag classes; `fresh_abs` mirrors `fresh_sym` including the depth bound; the cross-check corpus grew by the restored tokenizer module under `from_module`, classifier pools harvested via `synthesize_with_module`, and hand corners including the bridge boundary (indexed char vs Char parameter unencodable on BOTH sides) — exact agreement throughout, and no-Z3 verdicts stay honestly Inconclusive (only the predicate moved). **Evidence on the restored tokenizer module** (scratch, embedded verbatim in the verifier tests; `examples/functional_tokenizer.morph` untouched until Phase 79), judged with `Verifier::with_module` on mirror-transcribed bodies: **is_whitespace / is_ident_start / is_ident_continue — Verified** (constant-`true` bodies VerificationFailed); **peek — Verified** (the mirror body: record fields, the bridge, Option equality all encode); **skip_whitespace — Verified** (identity body against its two stated postconditions); **tokenize_all — Verified** (`Ok(state.tokens)` against its weak length postcondition); **tokenize_one — VerificationFailed for both partial bodies** (`Ok(state)`, `Err(_)`) — its is_err/is_ok/unwrap/len postconditions encode and refute them, and no verifying body exists short of a real tokenizing loop; **next_char — Inconclusive, named reason:** its body is fully encodable, but its postconditions call `peek(state)` and the resolver denotes **no body for peek** (`callee_body("peek")` is None — peek's postconditions are disjunctive guard rows over a computed Bool guard, neither an equation nor a key-indexed guard table), so the constraint side cannot inline the call. That is a resolver gap, not an encoder gap, and it is Phase 80's to weigh. Soundness spot-checks against Phase 75's executed outcomes: the verified classifier bodies are the same bodies the engine battery executes 774/774 against the hand mirrors (re-measured below), and peek's verified body is the transcription Phase 75 executed 2/2 in-process — no Verified claim is contradicted by an executed outcome. **Family re-statement, deliberate:** post-D5a the classifiers' inlined bodies are encodable, so by char_equation's own precedence rule (it admits only when the equation family cannot) the **equation family owns them** — their seeds are kind `equation`, Z3-Verified; char_equation's accepted trees are all Bool trees over param-vs-CharLit comparisons, hence all encodable now, so **the family's territory is empty by construction**; the builder and its precedence stay as the recorded fallback, and the four Phase 49 tests were re-stated (diagnosis flipped, seed kind + verdict moved, battery re-measured on the verified seed as the cross-check the audit's D5a terms require — re-measured at 774/774, not carried). **The pin is resolved by re-pinning:** `expr_to_ir_method_call_lowers_to_hole_pending_74_76` becomes `expr_to_ir_method_call_lowers_to_hole_no_ir_kind` — the deferral it named is complete (74's table, this phase's encoder, 75's evaluator all work on the spec `Expr`), and the lowering stays Hole as a design fact: the IR kind table has no method-call kind (the audit appended only 28/29), so a constraint-derived seed built from a method-call constraint carries a hole where the call was. **Recorded staleness, not fixed here:** the probe instruments (`tokenizer_synthesis_probe`, `tokenizer_cross_validation`) still narrate the pre-D5a "Char is Opaque to Z3" framing in their text; they assert executed agreement (unchanged) and carry no verdict assertions — Phase 79/80 own their re-instrumentation. **Tests:** 9 new z3-gated evidence/probe tests in the verifier (verdict table, side-condition decisiveness, bridge boundary + exactness, record probes, guarded-unwrap + literal-Err unwrap), the encodable cross-check extended, 4 Phase 49 tests re-stated, 1 pin re-pinned. **Suites: 471/0/26 default, 471/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 389/0/19 no-Z3** — the +9 delta over the 462/462/389 baselines is exactly the new evidence tests in the Z3 configurations and zero in the browser configuration by construction; no other pre-existing test moved. Next: Phase 77 (native emitters for the new nodes — opens only with its own scoped Rust-emitter unfreeze approval).
> **Phase 77 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-77-emitters`) — native emitters for the new nodes; the scoped Rust-emitter unfreeze is executed in its ratified form: NEW ARMS ONLY, no existing arm's output moved, and the gate re-runs below are the proof.** Spec-Expr → Rust (`expr_to_rust`): `MethodCall` renders the four D1a builtins as the identical Rust method syntax on the rendered receiver (`output.is_err()`, `output.unwrap().len()`); a name outside the closed table, or a table name carrying arguments (both Phase 74 type errors, unreachable from a checked module), renders the function's standing catch-all — never a guessed form. `RecordLit` renders the Rust struct literal, fields in written order, with one expectation the arm itself knows: a bare string-literal field value takes the owned form (`"spec".to_string()`), the defect-4d rule at the one position this arm controls, since a record field's declared type is the spec's (String → String) — measured by the compile fixture, whose first run failed E0308 on exactly this. `Expr::Field` deliberately keeps its standing catch-all rendering: the M3 corpus pins "field-fallback" byte-for-byte, so a real Field arm here would be drift on a pre-existing kind — its row belongs to the Phase 78 spec amendments, which move both sides together; the consequence is pinned in a golden (`state.tokens.len()` renders `??.len()` through the field receiver) rather than smoothed over. IR → Rust (`ir_to_rust_expr`): `FieldAccess` renders `.field` with the base through the condition-position discipline (block-like bases parenthesized; a record-literal base always parenthesized — a bare struct literal cannot head a projection in scrutinee/condition positions); `RecordLit` renders the same struct-literal form with the same owned-string rule; `ir_to_rust_tail` gains both kinds in its value-shaped list beside Index, so a tail-position projection or literal yields its value instead of a discarded `expr;`. **The other four emitters, per node, measured:** C renders both — member access, and a C99 compound literal with designated initializers (`(Token){ .kind = 0, .line = 1 }`) — because C's record concept is the struct and `generate_c` already carries the record-typedef rendering (field values ride the C expression emitter's standing per-kind coverage, a pre-existing limitation outside these nodes). Python renders both as the dict pair — literal `{"kind": 0, "lexeme": "spec"}` and subscript projection `state["pos"]` — because that emitter generates no class/dataclass definitions for record types (`ty_to_python` maps a named type to Any), making the dict the only declaration-free, self-consistent record model. JavaScript renders both natively — object literal `{ kind: 0, lexeme: 'spec' }` and property access — declaration-free and exact. **WAT renders neither**: the emitted WAT is a scalar model (i32/f64 params and results, no memory or struct types are ever declared), so both nodes keep the standing fallbacks (`i32.const 0` in expression position, the `;; node` comment in statement position) as explicit arms byte-identical to the catch-alls — the choice recorded at the site and pinned in the golden test, not an omission. **The record-type-declaration question (audit §4) is decided for this phase: declarations stay harness-supplied.** `generate` renders named types verbatim and its candidate API carries no module type declarations (`FunctionSpec::types()` is empty by design); promoting them into the emitter would need the module context Phase 76 built for the verifier's SpecIndex plumbed into codegen — the fixture therefore supplies the declarations through the Phase 48 runner prelude's shapes, exactly as the Phase 48 compiled runner did, and the promotion question is carried to Phase 78's cascade, which already re-opens the adapter. **Compile fixture** (the Phase 55/62 discipline; `examples/functional_tokenizer.morph` untouched): the restored tokenizer module (the Phases 73–76 scratch text, embedded verbatim) checks whole; generated Rust for next_char (a reduced transcription of the mirror body — its newline branch indexes a String, and the IR Index arm's standing rendering has no String form in Rust, a pre-existing limit outside this phase's two nodes; peek is excluded for the same reason, both already proved on the interpreter/Z3 routes), tokenize_one and tokenize_all (the Phase 75/76 partial bodies), plus ONE fixture-only spec `step_ok` carrying the MethodCall exercise — the module's own method-call constraints are postconditions (generate does not render those) over Field receivers (the standing catch-all above), so the exercise rides a precondition, the one constraint position generate_rust renders, with the precondition handed over in the engine-constructed `Constraint::Eq` form that the native renderer's Eq arm serves (a parsed `require: a == b` lands in `Constraint::Expr`, which that renderer carries through its standing "true" catch-all — measured on the fixture's first run, recorded here). The assembled file (prelude + one wrapped module per winner) compiles under `rustc --test` and runs **4 passed / 2 failed** — next_char 1/1 and step_ok 1/1 pass; each partial body passes its degenerate test and fails its token-producing one (keyword-spec, simple-spec), exactly the Phase 75/76 measurements. The fixture calls `generate_rust` directly in both configurations: the compiled adapter behind `bootstrap-codegen` gains its arms in Phase 78. **Unfreeze compliance:** M3 gate re-run **PASS — byte-identical over the 2,573-case corpus (mode B 2573/2573; mode A 2233 with divergences only the recorded F5 escaping class, reconciled)**; M4 gate re-run **143/143** dual render — zero drift on pre-existing cases in both. **Tests:** 5 new (per-emitter/per-node goldens incl. the Field boundary pin, the Phase 13 completeness list extended by the two kinds, the compile fixture); all 5 failed before the implementation. **Suites: 476/0/26 default, 476/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 394/0/19 no-Z3** — the +5 delta over the 471/471/389 baselines is exactly the new tests in every configuration; no pre-existing test moved. Next: Phase 78 (Codegen-spec fallout + bootstrap cascade: kind codes 13/14 + 28/29 rows, artifact regeneration, M3/M4 re-runs, M5 S1/S2 re-proofs, playground corpus).

> **Phase 78 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-78-cascade`) — the bootstrap cascade: the Codegen specs gain the new kinds' rows, and the composed + compiled emitters render them exactly as the native emitter does; the phase's headline is a measured spec-design correction, recorded below rather than smoothed over.** Spec amendments (`examples/self_host/codegen.morph`, 117 → **123 specs**, the Phase 55/62 discipline — families appended, never renumbered): `rust_expr_form` gains families **8** (Field, kind 5, moved out of the family-6 fallback), **9** (MethodCall, kind 13) and **10** (RecordLit, kind 14); `rust_expr_fixed_text` loses the kind-5 row and gains the kind-13 row (the MethodCall fallback routes through the table, as every fixed text does); six new specs — `rust_expr_field_text`, `rust_expr_method_form`, `rust_expr_method_text`, `rust_expr_record_text` (spec-Expr side) and `rust_field_access_text`, `rust_record_lit_text` (IR side), the five templates as `++` equations in the Phase 55 form (fields arrive pre-rendered/pre-joined by the walker; the per-field owned-string rule is applied by the walker as the native arm applies it; the IR field template's doc records the walker-side record-literal-base parenthesization exception); `rust_tail_form` gains rows 28/29 → the bare-expression family — an amendment the measurement showed to be REQUIRED, not cosmetic: the composed tail routed kinds 28/29 through its catch-all to the statement fallthrough, diverging from the Phase 77 native value list; `rust_stmt_form`'s constraints do not move (28/29 already land in its family-17 fallback — doc + tests only); the 30-kind totals are re-stated in the `rust_condition_wrap` / `rust_typed_form` / `rust_concat_right_form` docs. **The MethodCall dispatch finding:** the first draft keyed `rust_expr_method_form` by `(name, argc)` in the `rust_expr_call_form` guard shape — and the probe measured it seedless AND winnerless: 0 of 21 pool candidates Verified, the winner Inconclusive with a different junk root (Let/If/Block) in each of 3 runs, where call_form finds a deterministic Verified Match body (score 0.985, tpr 1.000) in the same runs. A scratch experiment (hand-built bodies through `verify_one`, removed after use) proved the spec itself fully verifiable — both the Match body and the if-chain body verify, all constraints holding — so the gap was generation, not encodability: the pool's winning Match bodies are test-table-shaped, and call_form's seven exemplar tests happen to state its whole classification while a two-test set leaves this table underdetermined (a body can pass both exemplars and still fail verification on the untested names, with no search gradient from there). The single-String-key draft (name alone) measured the same failure; the ratified shape therefore keys the classification by the **effective name** — the walker folds the argument count into the key (a call carrying arguments passes the empty name, itself off-table, so the fold is exact with no sentinel collision), mirroring the native arm, whose own decision is a match on the name under an `args.is_empty()` guard — the count is a structural precondition there, not table data — and the spec carries a **complete exemplar test set** (all four table names, an off-table name, a case near-miss, the empty key), as call_form's test set states its classification. Re-measured: seedless by design (String-equality guards, the recorded unadmitted seed family), pool winner deterministic Verified Match (0.985, tpr 1.000) in every run, joining the seedless set as its fourth member. **Native lockstep (the scoped unfreeze's Phase 78 half — both sides move together):** `expr_to_rust` gains the plain `Expr::Field` arm (`{base}.{rust_ident(field)}`; the condition-position discipline is the IR walker's only), and the Phase 77 golden's pin flips — `state.tokens` and `state.tokens.len()` now render whole, where Phase 77 pinned `??` / `??.len()`. **Re-synthesis + mirrors:** `tests/m3_proof.rs` mirrors amended and extended (+2 tests, 76 total; the local SExpr/IR types gain the new shapes and both assemblies the new arms); the seed fixture grows 63 → **69 bodies** (`rust_tail_form` joins by the Phase 37 precedent — a body a phase corrects joins the fixture — plus the five equation templates; the amended `rust_expr_form`/`rust_expr_fixed_text` bodies replaced in place, harvested verbatim from the resolver; `rust_expr_method_form` confirmed seedless). Probe ×3: **123 specs, seeds 119/123** (seedless quartet: `rust_ident_empty`, `rust_target_route`, `rust_expr_call_form`, `rust_expr_method_form`), **Verified 123/123** in every run; seed-kind tally equation 63 (58 + the five templates), guard_table 39, composition_guard 7, pair_guard_table 4, expr_pin_guard 3, guarded_identity 1, multi_guard 1, mixed_pair_guard_table 1. Cross-validation (3 randomized runs, winners executed): **golden 381/381, pinned 3,021/3,021** in all three runs; harness bookkeeping re-derived — module totals **3,939 cases / 3,021 pinned / 911 spec-silent** (the silent total falls by 2: kinds 13/14 were spec-silent in `rust_expr_form`'s band and are pinned by their rows now); the golden delta attributable to this phase is +25 by arm diff (the inherited 354 baseline was itself stale by 2 against the arm-level count, recorded here so the next re-derivation starts from 381). **Gate cascade, all re-run:** M3 gate **PASS** — corpus 2,573 → **2,639** (+18 proof cases: 9 spec-expr incl. the ONE re-stated case — `field-fallback` (expected `??`) is now `field` (expected `x.f`), counted and named here, not absorbed; 7 IR-expr incl. the record-literal-base and call-base projections; 2 tails — plus 6 winner files and 42 winner-tree renderings), mode B byte-identical over the whole corpus, mode A divergences only the recorded F5 class, reconciled; the composed walkers (`m3_gate::Composed`, `bootstrap_adapter`) gain the Field/MethodCall/RecordLit + FieldAccess/RecordLit arms transcribing the native discipline (condition-position base, record-literal base parenthesized, owned-string field values through their winner). M4 gate **149/149** byte-identical (corpus re-derived 143 → **149**: 123 Codegen + 7 validator + 7 printer + 8 tokenizer + 4 standalone). **Artifact regeneration** through the documented path only (`m5_s1_regen_artifact`, original pipeline): shipped artifact 111,558 → **121,245 B**; gate raw render 145,404 B; test-module strip count 59 → **60** (the new carrier is `rust_expr_method_form`'s complete test set); winner-set provenance re-recorded in the shipped header (119 seeds, equation 63, seedless quartet). **No new shim** — the mirror-completed composition needed none for these rows; the 9-shim definition stands unchanged. **S1 re-proofs:** byte identity PASS (fresh extraction + render reproduces the checked-in artifact byte-for-byte); adapter equivalence PASS — the compiled adapter is byte-identical to the original emitter over all 2,639 M3 cases and 149 M4 whole files, and equal to the interpreter composition on every case. **S2 re-proofs:** byte roundtrip 4/4 with `bootstrap-codegen` off and on; behavioral legs 5/5 builds / whole-file compiles / function compiles / tests in both configurations. **Playground fallout:** bundle rebuilt at **653,970 B against the 673,792 B budget** — within budget (the growth over W4's 611,728 B is the accumulated compiler change since v0.6.0, Phases 73–78; headroom 19,822 B; NO re-baseline — that remains an owner-visible act); corpus agreement re-run against the rebuilt pkg/: **13/13 check + 5/5 synthesis**. **The carried Phase 77 question is confirmed closed:** record-type declarations stay harness-supplied via the Phase 48 prelude shapes — `generate`'s candidate API carries no module declarations, unchanged through the cascade (no API surface moved in this phase; every gate above ran under it). One pre-existing engine unit test updated with its reason recorded in its doc comment: the pinned `rust_tail_form` guard-table seed body gains the amendment's two arms (28/29 → 1, in row order). **Suites: 478/0/26 default, 478/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 396/0/19 no-Z3** — the +2 delta over the 476/476/394 baselines is exactly the two new `tests/m3_proof.rs` tests in every configuration; no other pre-existing test moved. Next: Phase 79 (tokenizer completion: restore tokenize_one/tokenize_all constraints under D1a syntax, the 9 tests in D3a named form, D4a START columns in the hand-written tokenizer, S4 measurement re-runs).

> **Phase 79 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-79-tokenizer`) — tokenizer completion: the real file completed, D4a applied, both probe instruments re-instrumented, and the S4 measurement re-run against the Phase 48 baseline; the M1 gate verdict itself is Phase 80's and is NOT taken here.** **The file:** `examples/functional_tokenizer.morph` carries all 8 specs in full for the first time since the original — `tokenize_one` / `tokenize_all` restored with acda3f9's constraint text verbatim (spacing included: the one place the module's tight dialect is not applied, recorded in the spec comments, because the restoration is verbatim by design) and the 9 record tests restored in-spec in the D3a named form (peek ×2, next_char ×1, skip_whitespace ×2, tokenize_one ×2, tokenize_all ×2), so the module again states all 18 original tests and passes `morphic check` whole; the five carried specs' constraint texts are byte-unchanged (their comments now record the restorations). The file's header gains a Phase 79 completion record closing every item the Phase 47 record left open. Fallout, named: the playground's tokenizer snapshot is re-synced to the completed file (its byte-identity invariant), and the playground corpus agreement re-runs **13/13 check + 5/5 synthesis** against the Phase 78 pkg. **D4a:** the hand-written tokenizer's `push_token` (`tests/tokenizer_proof.rs`) takes the token's start state beside the advanced state and stamps START (through Phase 78 it read the already-advanced state and stamped END); the flipped pin was written failing first (proof columns [5, 6, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15] vs the mirrors' [1, 5, 6, 11, 12, 13, 14]) and `s4_tokenize_crosscheck_and_stamping_divergence` is now `..._agreement`, asserting full token equality — lexemes, kinds, lines, and columns — between the mirrors and the hand-written tokenizer, which now agree everywhere. **Re-instrumentation (Phase 76 flagged this as Phase 79's):** both instruments verify with `Verifier::with_module` (specs + type declarations — the functions-only index they used before degrades to the pre-Phase-76 opaque binding, so the old instruments would have kept measuring the pre-campaign world); the synthesis probe's narration is re-stated post-D5a and it now asserts the one deterministic verdict fact — each Char classifier's constraint seed reaches every verified pool and every such seed is Verified (it held in all 9 spec-runs); the cross-validation harness retires SMOKE-ONLY (the tokenize pair's verdicts are EXACT/PARTIAL on executed agreement with the winners' Z3 statuses reported), re-states its routes (interpreter record values exist since Phase 75 — the compiled route is kept because its object is the pipeline's generated code), and its mirror copies needed no change (they stamped START all along). **Measurement (probe + cross-validation, 3 runs each; Phase 48 baseline in parentheses):** the three classifiers — equation seeds in all 9 spec-runs, crowned, **Verified**, tpr 1.000 (Phase 49: char_equation seeds, Inconclusive by construction); executed agreement **774/774 per classifier per run** — 2,322/2,322 per run across the three — pool-exact bodies [1, 1, 1] each, winners deterministic: **EXACT, now with the Z3 backstop**. skip_whitespace — seedless (its two rows are field inequalities; no family admits them); Mutation winners **Verified in all 3 runs** (Phase 48: winners satisfied the postconditions but no verdict could be real); executed postconditions **12/12**, full mirror agreement **4/12** — Phase 48's exact totals, now with verified winners: the spec as stated is satisfied, and the stopping behavior beyond it remains the mirror's completion, as recorded since Phase 47. peek — seedless, **0 verified candidates in any pool**, Mutation winners Inconclusive, tpr 0.000; executed agreement **0/39** with the Phase 48 failure class intact (the crowned bodies' generated code fails rustc doing arithmetic on the state record itself, E0369): Phase 76 proved peek's mirror body verifiable by hand, so peek's blocker is purely generational — no seed family admits a guard-row record spec and the Mutation pools produce no field-reading body. next_char — seedless, winners Inconclusive, tpr 0.000; executed **0/39 full-state and 0/39 pinned** in the one run whose winner terminated (two draws non-terminating, killed at the 10 s limit; Phase 48 drew identity winners at the same 0/39). tokenize_one — seedless (its constraints' method calls lower to Hole for seed derivation — the standing no-IR-kind fact); the restored rows now REFUTE: **12 VerificationFailed candidates** across the 3 runs (Phase 48: vacuous Verified, nothing refutable), winners Inconclusive, executed agreement **0/10** (Phase 48's statistic, now measured against judging constraints). tokenize_all — seedless; **16 refuted candidates**; run 1's crowned winner is itself VerificationFailed (selection's documented fallback when every earlier-gate survivor is refuted), runs 2–3 Inconclusive; executed agreement **0/8**. **Phase 80 inputs, stated precisely:** (i) **next_char's Inconclusive is solely the resolver gap, unchanged since Phase 76.** Its body and constraints are fully encodable; the constraint side calls `peek(state)`, and `SpecIndex::callee_body("peek")` is None because every denotation builder keys on scalar shapes — `first_equation_body` needs an unconditional `output == …` equation (peek has none), `guard_table_body` needs a single Int/Bool parameter (peek's is a record), and the guarded-identity / composition-guard / multi-guard / expr-pin / pair-keyed builders are likewise keyed or arity-bound — while peek's two rows guard on the computed predicate `state.pos < len(state.source)` over that record's fields. The denotation Phase 80 needs is a complementary-guard builder (an ite over the shared predicate: `pos < len → Some(source[pos])`, else `None`) — a new resolver builder, not a one-line fix, and not inside this phase's measurement scope; Phase 80 builds it or records next_char as the gate's named residual. (ii) **tokenize_one carries a spec-consistency finding, recorded here, not amended** (the restoration is verbatim by design; amending a constraint is owner/spec territory): its first row, read literally, refutes the end-of-input behavior its own golden test demands — at `pos >= len(source)` the test expects `Ok(state)` with no new token, and that output gives `is_err() = false` and `len(output.unwrap().tokens) = len(state.tokens)`, i.e. `0 == 0 + 1` on the row; every output satisfying both rows at end-of-input must instead be `Err` or fabricate a token, and both fail the golden test. So no test-correct body can be Z3-Verified against tokenize_one as written, whatever synthesis finds — the gate run faces an amendment decision (e.g. an end-of-input disjunct in row 1) in addition to the generation gap, and Phase 76's "no verifying body exists short of a real tokenizing loop" is sharpened accordingly: a real loop body returning `Ok` at end-of-input is refuted too. tokenize_all has no such tension (its row is the length floor the degenerate `Ok(state.tokens)` body already verifies against, Phase 76). (iii) tokenize_one / tokenize_all still need real looping bodies for executed agreement: their constraints encode and refute, but no seed family builds from method-call constraints and the randomized pools contain no tokenizing loop — a generation gap, measured, not massaged. **Suites: 478/0/26 default, 478/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 396/0/19 no-Z3 — zero delta over the Phase 78 baselines** (the flipped pin is a rename within `tests/tokenizer_proof.rs`; the new assertions live inside the ignored instruments; no counted test was added or moved). Next: Phase 80 (the M1 gate re-run: the ROAD M1 checklist executed as written, the gate line ticked or not, with (i) and (ii) above as its named inputs).

> **Phase 80 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-80-gate`) — the M1 GATE RE-RUN: the checklist executed as written, every clause measured, the gate NOT passed, and the campaign (Phases 72–80) closed with its remainders priced. Nothing was amended, built, or re-scoped in this phase: the only code change is one proof test (instrumentation), and the deferral-era gate state note above is replaced by the outcome recorded here.** **Method:** this phase re-ran the instruments itself rather than inheriting Phase 79's numbers — `morphic check` on the completed module (✓ valid), the synthesis probe (3 runs × 8 specs), the cross-validation harness (3 runs), the Phase 76 verifier evidence tests (standing in the suite, green), and one new test, `phase80_tokenize_one_eof_contradiction`, which proves the tokenize_one finding at the point (below). Random tallies (which junk body a pool crowns, refuted-candidate counts) vary between instrument runs exactly as a randomized engine's should; every deterministic fact below held in both Phase 79's and this phase's runs. **The gate-line reading, stated once:** the checklist's artifact is the *generated* tokenizer (item 3 produces it, item 4 cross-validates the generated one), so the gate line's "Z3 verified" is judged on synthesis-crowned winners, and its "100% golden test pass" on the winners' executed agreement with the recorded outputs. A hand-transcribed body verified by hand (peek, tokenize_all — Phase 76) proves the *spec* verifiable; it is reported as such and does not discharge the gate. **Clause by clause:** (1) *Spec written* — **PASS, ticked**: `examples/functional_tokenizer.morph`, 8 specs, all 18 original tests in-spec, `morphic check` valid (re-run this phase). (2) *Golden suite (50+ recorded outputs)* — **PASS, ticked**: the 18 in-spec tests, the mirror goldens in `tests/tokenizer_proof.rs`, and the harness batteries (774 chars × 3 classifiers, 39 + 39 states, 12 skip states, 10 + 8 tokenize states — thousands of recorded outputs). (3) *LLM seed → MCTS → Z3 verify → codegen* — **PASS as executed, ticked**: the probe ran the full pipeline on every spec in all 3 runs (synthesise ok, verify_all module-aware, selection, `codegen=ok` on every winner); what the pipeline *produced* per spec is the gate line's business, below. (4) *Cross-validate generated vs Rust original* — **NOT-PASS, unticked**: agreement is per-spec (table below) — classifiers EXACT, everything else partial; the D4a stamping question is closed (mirrors and the hand-written tokenizer agree on full token equality, columns included), so what remains is winner quality, not a measurement dispute. (5) *Benchmark ≤ 2×* — **NOT-PASS, unticked**: never run. No tokenizer benchmark harness exists anywhere in the project (the only benchmark on record is M5's SC3 codegen-render measurement, a different component), the ROAD's infra list still carries "benchmark harness" as an open item, and there is as yet no complete generated tokenizer to time — the clause is unmet twice over, unmeasured and currently unmeasurable against a passing artifact. (6) *Gate line: 100% golden + Z3 verified* — **NOT-PASS, unticked**, per spec: **is_whitespace / is_ident_start / is_ident_continue — PASS both halves**: equation seeds reach every pool and crown deterministically (score 0.975, tpr 1.000, all 9 spec-runs), winners Z3-**Verified**, executed agreement **774/774** per classifier per run (2,322/2,322 across the three), in-spec tests 3/3 each, golden 3/3 each in the harness. The deferral's protected clause is met at full standard. **skip_whitespace — Z3 half PASS, golden half NOT-PASS**: Mutation winners **Verified in all 3 runs** (verified pool members 9–16 per run), and the winners satisfy every postcondition the spec states (executed postconditions 12/12 in the runs whose winner compiled); but the crowned winners stand at tpr **0.500** (1 of 2 in-spec tests — a body can satisfy the stated floor `output.pos >= state.pos` and the length row without actually skipping, and selection crowns such bodies) and full mirror agreement is **4/12**. The shortfall's nature is spec strength, recorded since Phase 47 and re-stated by the harness itself ("PARTIAL by design … a property of the spec as stated, not a machinery failure"): the spec under-determines the behavior its own test pins exactly. **peek — NOT-PASS both halves**: 0 verified candidates in any pool (0/40, 0/40, 0/28), winners Inconclusive, executed agreement **0/39** with the Phase 48 failure class intact (crowned bodies' generated code fails rustc, E0369 arithmetic on the state record). A verifying body EXISTS — Phase 76 verified the mirror body by hand (record fields, the string-index bridge, Option equality all encode) — but under the gate-line reading above, existence is not crowning: no seed family admits a guard-row record spec, so synthesis never proposes a field-reading body. **next_char — NOT-PASS both halves**: winners Inconclusive, executed **0/39** (full-state and pinned; draws this phase: one non-terminating winner killed at the 10 s limit, two rustc failures). Uniquely in the module, even a hand body cannot be verified: its postconditions call `peek(state)` and the resolver denotes no body for peek — the standing Phase 76 test asserts `callee_body("peek")` is None and this phase's suites re-confirm it. A resolver gap, not an encoder gap (the body itself is fully encodable, Phase 76). **tokenize_one — NOT-PASS both halves, and its Z3 half is impossible as written** (proof below): seedless, winners Inconclusive at tpr 0.000, the restored rows refute (12 VerificationFailed candidates across this phase's 3 runs — the constraints bite; Phase 48's vacuous verdicts refuted nothing), executed agreement **0/10**. **tokenize_all — NOT-PASS both halves**: seedless, winners Inconclusive (one draw this phase crowned a VerificationFailed body under selection's documented all-survivors-refuted fallback, as in Phase 79), executed agreement **0/8**; a verifying body exists for its stated row — `Ok(state.tokens)`, Verified in Phase 76 — but it is degenerate (it tokenizes nothing) and synthesis does not crown it. **The tokenize_one contradiction, proven (the phase's one code artifact):** `phase80_tokenize_one_eof_contradiction` instantiates row 1 at the spec's own "end-of-input" test point through the production encoder and solver — no hand-waved arithmetic. The test (read off the spec itself, structurally asserted) demands, at input `{source: "", pos: 0, tokens: []}`, the output `Ok` of that same state. At that point row 1 — `output.is_err() || len(output.unwrap().tokens) == len(state.tokens) + 1` — is REFUTED under the golden output (row 2 is Proven; the failure is row 1's alone): the golden output gives `is_err() = false` and `len(tokens) = 0`, i.e. **0 == 0 + 1**. The satisfying set at that input is then enumerated by tag case analysis over a free tag (row 1 under a literal Err tag is the encoder's named unencodable case — no Ok payload exists to project — so the Err branch is discharged through the free tag, where the left disjunct is the very `is_err()` term that is definitionally true under the literal tag): row 1 ∧ is_ok ∧ 0 tokens is UNSAT, row 1 ∧ is_err is SAT, row 1 ∧ is_ok ∧ 1 token is SAT, row 1 ∧ is_ok ∧ 2 tokens is UNSAT — at end-of-input the rows admit exactly an **Err** output (row 2 independently Proven under a literal Err: 0 >= 0) or an **Ok carrying exactly one fabricated token**, and both fail the golden test. Therefore no body can be simultaneously test-correct and Z3-Verified against tokenize_one as written — including a perfect tokenizing loop, whose EOF return is the golden output. Phase 76's "no verifying body exists short of a real tokenizing loop" is superseded: no verifying test-correct body exists at all, short of amending the row. **One adjacent observation, recorded at observation strength (the artifact proves only the EOF point):** row 2 licenses Err only at end-of-input (`output.is_ok() || state.pos >= len(state.source)`), while the mirror errors on unexpected characters mid-input (the battery's digit case) — a mirror-faithful body is refuted by row 2 on those inputs too; whether Err-on-unexpected-character belongs to the intended contract is part of the same owner decision as the amendment. **The three priced remainders** (sizes in the audit's calibration — S/M/L/XL; F4 was one phase for one variant, a seed family ≈ one phase by the G3 precedent, template wiring ≈ one phase by G2): **(i) The complementary-guard resolver builder — M, one phase.** A ninth builder in the `SpecIndex` chain (`src/synthesis/engine.rs`, beside the eight standing `*_body` builders) plus its seed wrapper, on the standing core+wrapper pattern: pattern = a spec with exactly two postcondition rows, each a disjunction `G || output == E`, where the second guard is the *structural* complement of the first (`Not(G)`, or a complementary comparison — Lt/Ge, Le/Gt, Eq/Neq — over `expr_same` operands); denotation = `If(G1, E2, E1)` lowered through `expr_to_ir`. Registered at the END of the `callee_body` chain, so it fires only where all eight predecessors return None and no existing denotation moves. It closes two things at once: the verifier's `sym_invoke` gains peek's denotation, so **next_char's mirror body becomes Z3-Verified** (its verification gap closes — body encodable since Phase 76, constraints provable once the call inlines); and peek's own pools gain the denotation as a constraint seed — the seed IS the mirror body, Verified (Phase 76) and executed 2/2 on peek's tests (Phase 75) — so **peek can crown a Verified winner** (its generation gap closes with the same builder). Evidence: `callee_body("peek")` returns the If body; the denoted body verifies against peek's own postconditions (the Phase 76 test shape, as a self-check); next_char's mirror body verifies; the probe shows peek `seed_in_pool=true` with a Verified crown. Risk, named: complement detection must stay syntactic and exact — a mispaired denotation would change what a call *means* in verification; the pattern is deliberately narrower than most standing builders, and the trust model is theirs (a denotation is derived from the callee's own postconditions), with the self-check as this builder's added evidence. What (i) does NOT close: next_char's *crowning* — its pools would still need a body worth verifying; a record-construction seed from partial field equations (unconditional field equations + guard rows over a by-then-denoted callee → RecordLit completion) is a further builder, **(i-b), M**, recorded here rather than smuggled into (i); after (i) alone, next_char stands exactly where peek stands today (verifiable, uncrowned). **(ii) The tokenize_one amendment — an owner decision (the edit itself is S once decided: one row, the module's header record extended, re-measurement); NOTHING is amended in this phase.** Options, recommendation first: **(A) [RECOMMENDED] an end-of-input disjunct in row 1, in row 2's own idiom** — `output.is_err() || len(output.unwrap().tokens) == len(state.tokens) + 1 || state.pos >= len(state.source)`. Consequences: the spec's tests and rows become jointly satisfiable (the golden EOF output satisfies every row — re-checkable mechanically by re-pointing this phase's proof test), and a real tokenizing body becomes verifiable in principle; encodability unchanged (the added disjunct is row 2's existing one). Residue, stated: the license is an input condition, so at EOF inputs row 1 constrains nothing further about the output (row 2 + the golden tests bound it); and the whitespace-only-remainder corner — the mirror skips to end-of-input from a non-EOF input and returns Ok without growth — is still not licensed, so a mirror-faithful body remains refutable there and full battery agreement for a Verified tokenize_one stays capped on that corner. **(B) Pin the EOF output exactly** — row 1 gains `|| output == Ok(state)`: at end-of-input the only licensed Ok is the identity output (under (A) a position-moved no-growth Ok is also licensed; fabrication stays licensed under both, via the growth disjunct). The semantically tightest restatement, but a new idiom for this module (output equality against a constructor), and its encodability — Result equality against a record-payload construction — is plausible yet UNMEASURED; a measurement is owed before adoption, and if it encodes, (B) is the stronger spec. **(C) Growth as a range** — `output.is_err() || (len(output.unwrap().tokens) >= len(state.tokens) && len(output.unwrap().tokens) <= len(state.tokens) + 1)`: consistent with the mirror everywhere, the whitespace corner included — but the progress guarantee is vacated, because the identity body `Ok(state)` then satisfies both rows at every input and tokenize_one's Z3 clause becomes passable by a body that tokenizes nothing (the Phase 48 vacuity failure in a new form); the golden tests become the only behavioral judge. Not recommended. **(D) A whitespace-aware disjunct** (license no-growth when only whitespace remains): NOT VIABLE — "only whitespace remains" is a range-quantified (Forall) statement, Forall constraints are unencodable in the current encoder (the count_matching precedent), and row 1 would become unencodable, leaving tokenize_one permanently Inconclusive; recorded so it need not be rediscovered. **(E) Amend the test instead** (expect Err at end-of-input): contradicts the component's semantics in every other artifact — the mirror, the hand-written tokenizer, and tokenize_all's "empty" test all treat end-of-input as Ok. Not recommended. The row-2 observation above belongs to this same decision round: if Err-on-unexpected-character is ruled part of the contract, row 2 needs its own amendment in the same pass (its natural guard mentions the post-skip character — the (D) expressibility wall); if not, the battery's digit corner stands as mirror behavior beyond the spec. **(iii) The tokenize_one / tokenize_all generation campaign — L–XL (2–3 phases by the G-campaign calibration), strictly AFTER (ii); against the unamended spec it is pointless by this phase's proof.** Why nothing crowns today: MethodCall lowers to Hole (no IR kind — the re-pinned design fact), so no constraint seed derives from the tokenize rows, and the randomized pools contain no tokenizing loop (0 verified, winners tpr 0.000, executed 0/10 and 0/8 in every run of Phases 79–80). What closing it takes, by precedent: a spec-keyed algorithmic template in the G2 shape (templates.rs, structural keying) whose body is the tokenizing step — skip_whitespace call → peek dispatch (classifier calls for the ident arm, single-char arms) → next_char consumption → RecordLit Token construction → list append → Ok/Err assembly, every composed callee denoted or directly encodable after (i) — plus a seed family admitting it (the G3 pattern, family #11) keyed on the amended rows' shape (the is_err/is_ok disjunction + unwrap projection), reaching tokenize_all's pools as well (its mirror body is the loop over tokenize_one; the same construction serves both). Honest calibration: G2+G3 closed count_matching's and sort's gaps across two phases and sort still needed G6 plus a language decision; a tokenizing loop is larger than any template yet wired (five composed callees, record construction, an error arm), and the G5/binary_search precedent stands — a named generation gap can survive a campaign. It may land as "verifying body exists, crowning unreliable"; that outcome would still move tokenize_one/tokenize_all to peek's post-(i) position. **CAMPAIGN SUMMARY (Phases 72–80) — what the language gained:** method calls as a native expression with the closed D1a builtin table (`Result::{is_ok, is_err, unwrap}`, `List::len`) resolved by the typechecker, evaluated by the interpreter, encoded by Z3, and rendered by every emitter; an IR field-access node (kind 28) and a record-construction node (kind 29) with named record literals (spec kind 14) validated against declared record types; interpreter record values plus the `Ok`/`Err`/`Some`/`None` constructor values, so record-spec tests bind and are judged in-process for the first time; Z3 symbolic records, Result/Option tag+payload forms, length-only list binding, the Char scalar encoding with its range side-condition (D5a), and the string-index→Char bridge with its one named boundary (an indexed char against a symbolic Char parameter); a module-aware verifier (type declarations in the index) with the pure encodability mirror in lockstep; the Codegen specs grown 117 → 123 with the new kinds' rows appended (13/14, 28/29 — nothing renumbered) and the synthesized bootstrap artifact regenerated (111,558 → 121,245 B) through a full cascade re-proof (M3 gate byte-identical over the extended 2,639-case corpus, M4 149/149, S1/S2 re-proved, playground corpus 13/13 + 5/5 at 653,970 B against the 673,792 B budget, no re-baseline); D4a's START-normative columns applied, closing the last spec-vs-implementation divergence (mirrors and the hand-written tokenizer now agree on full token equality); and the tokenizer module itself completed — 8 specs in full, 18/18 tests in-spec, checking whole. Headline movement, Phase 48 baseline → now: the classifiers went from *exact-but-Inconclusive-by-construction* to **Verified AND exact**; the record specs went from *unbindable, unencodable, seedless (24/24)* to bindable, encodable, and — for skip_whitespace — Verified-crowned; the tokenize pair went from *vacuously Verified against no constraints* to honestly refuting, with their remaining gaps named to the builder, the row, and the loop. **Suites: 479/0/26 default, 479/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 396/0/19 no-Z3** — the +1 delta over the 478/478/396 baselines is exactly this phase's proof test, Z3-gated so the browser configuration is unchanged by construction; no other test moved. **The M1 gate stays OPEN.** Its remaining distance is three priced items and one owner decision, all recorded above — not a standard anyone is invited to relax.

> **Phase 81 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-81-resolver`) — M1 remainders (i) + (i-b) built: the complementary-guard resolver builder and the record-completion seed. Both Phase 80 closures land: `peek` denotes and crowns Verified; `next_char` verifies by hand and crowns Verified.** **(i) The complementary-guard builder** — a ninth builder in the `SpecIndex::callee_body` chain, registered at the END so it fires only where all eight predecessors return None (no existing denotation moves — the regression evidence below), plus its seed wrapper (`complementary_guard`).
