The shape, exactly as priced: a spec with EXACTLY two postconditions, each an Or of exactly two leaves — one `output == E` pin (either orientation) and one guard leaf, neither mentioning `output` — whose guards are structural complements: `Not(G)` against `G`, or a complementary comparison (Lt/Ge, Le/Gt, Eq/Neq, either order) over `expr_same` operands IN THE SAME ORDER (swapped operands are declined — pinned by test). The denotation is `If(G1, E2, E1)` lowered through `expr_to_ir`, with one builder-local step: `len(x)` is rewritten to its IR call form before lowering (`Expr::Length` lowers to a Hole in the standing `expr_to_ir`; the call form is what the verifier's `len` arm and the Phase 76 mirror body use), and any remaining Hole declines the denotation. A third postcondition of any shape declines (the denotation claims the spec's whole meaning — the guard families' deviation discipline, tightened). Two measured dialect facts, recorded: a bare leading `!` in a constraint is hoisted by the parser to a constraint-level Not and `!(…)` does not parse in guard-leaf position, so the Not pair — admitted by the pricing and implemented — is reachable only from constructed ASTs and is pinned on constructed expressions; the reachable complement forms are the comparison pairs (peek's Lt/Ge, next_char's Eq/Neq). **Self-check, as priced:** the denoted body for `peek` verifies against peek's own postconditions under `Verifier::with_module` (the Phase 76 test shape) — held. **(i-b) The record-completion seed** — a seed-only family (`record_completion`; deliberately NOT a `callee_body` denotation, the char_equation/forall_fold precedent — what it closes is crowning, not other specs' constraint sides). The exact rule: the return type must resolve through the index's declarations to a record R with exactly one base parameter of the same record type; every postcondition must classify as an unconditional field equation `output.f == rhs` or a guard row with a single guard leaf `expr_same` to the family's one guard G or its structural complement (a row's pin holds where its leaf FAILS — an `expr_same` leaf pins on the G-false side, a complementary leaf on the G-true side); at least one of each is required. The body is `R { … }` in declared field order: a field's value is its unconditional rhs, an `If(G, …)` over its side pins, or the base record's field CARRIED on any side no equation pins — the rule the module's own spec comment states ("Fields no row pins … are carried over unchanged"). Declines (all pinned or by construction): no declarations in the index; non-unique base; an unclassifiable postcondition; a pin on an undeclared field; conflicting pins on one field/side; a field both unconditional and guarded; a second unrelated guard; any Hole in a lowered piece. For `next_char` it constructs exactly the mirror body: `pos` from the unconditional equation, `line` pinned on the newline side only (carried otherwise), `col` pinned on both sides, `source`/`tokens` carried. **Two enabling changes, recorded:** (1) the resolver's IR shape set (`inline_rec`, `substitute_rec`, `collect_free_vars`, `collect_pattern_binders`) is widened to the Phase 73–77 nodes (If was always an IR node but outside the set; FieldAccess/Index/RecordLit are the record-era shapes) — the widening only turns shape-refusals into resolutions, and (i-b)'s seed requires it (its guard calls a callee whose denotation is an If over an Index); (2) the engine gains a declarations-carrying constructor (`with_module_spec` / `synthesize_with_module_spec`, the verifier's Phase 76 asymmetry closed on the synthesis side), and the two tokenizer instruments now synthesize through it — the M3/M4 call sites are untouched (their modules declare no record types; the declarations are inert there). **Measured closures (probe + cross-validation, 3 runs each, this phase):** `peek` — the complementary_guard seed is in every pool, seed Verified in every pool, and the crowned winner IS the seed in all 3 runs: **Verified, tpr 1.000, score 0.975, deterministic** (Phase 79/80 baseline: seedless, 0 verified candidates, Inconclusive winners). `next_char` — the record_completion seed likewise: **Verified crown in all 3 runs, winner = the seed, tpr 1.000**; and the hand-body half: the full mirror body (line/col branching on a `peek(state)` call) verifies Verified with peek inlined through the denotation — the Phase 76 test that pinned the blocker is re-stated as this closure (its encodability half unchanged). **Executed agreement, reported separately and not conflated:** in-process, both winners pass their in-spec tests (tpr 1.000 above); in the cross-validation harness's COMPILED runners both still stand at **0/39** — their generated Rust does not compile in the runner (E0425: `len` not in scope; E0277: `str` cannot be indexed by `i64`; E0618: `None` called as a function). That is the standing emitter-side class Phase 77 recorded when it excluded peek from its compile fixture (the IR Index arm has no String form in generated Rust; builtin `len`/`None()` renderings in generated code), NOT the Phase 48 E0369 class and not a verification fact: the winners are Verified and interpreter-exact on their tests, and the compiled-execution half of the gate line remains open on this named emitter class for the Phase 85 re-run to weigh. Everything else in the harness is unmoved: classifiers EXACT 774/774 Verified; skip_whitespace Verified at 4/12 by design; the tokenize pair at their Phase 79/80 measurements (Phases 82–84 territory). **Regression discipline:** the Codegen module probe re-run ×3 — **123 specs, seeds 119/123, winners Verified 123/123 every run** — and cross-validation golden **381/381** + pinned **3,021/3,021** per run (summed 1143/1143, 9063/9063 over the 3 runs): exactly the Phase 78 totals; no denotation for any previously-denoted callee changed, and the M3/M4 gate tests stand green in both standing configurations. **Tests:** 4 new engine tests (peek denotation + seed; complement decline side incl. swapped operands and the Not pair at AST level; record completion's constructed shape + resolved seed; its decline side) and 1 verifier test re-stated (Phase 76's blocker pin → Phase 81's closure: self-check + next_char full-body verification). **Suites: 483/0/26 default, 483/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 400/0/19 no-Z3** — the +4 delta over the 479/479/396 baselines is exactly the new engine tests in every configuration (they are not Z3-gated; the verifier re-state is net-zero). `examples/functional_tokenizer.morph` untouched. Next: Phase 82 (amendment A for tokenize_one, owner-ratified), then the generation campaign (Phases 83–84), then the gate re-run (Phase 85).
> **Phase 82 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-82-amendment`) — M1 remainder (ii) applied: amendment A for `tokenize_one`, owner-ratified 2026-11-22. This phase changes a SPEC — one constraint row in `examples/functional_tokenizer.morph` is amended; it is the first deliberate deviation from the Phase 79 verbatim restoration, and it is marked as such in the module header (a Phase 82 amendment record) and at the spec itself.** **The amendment, exactly as ratified** (Phase 80's option A, word for word): tokenize_one's row 1 — `output.is_err() || len(output.unwrap().tokens) == len(state.tokens) + 1` — gains row 2's own end-of-input disjunct, becoming `output.is_err() || len(output.unwrap().tokens) == len(state.tokens) + 1 || state.pos >= len(state.source)`. Row 2 is NOT touched: Phase 80's adjacent observation (row 2 licenses Err only at end-of-input while the mirror errors mid-input) belongs to a future owner decision round; it is re-measured below, not amended. The module passes `morphic check` whole; the playground snapshot is re-synced byte-identical and corpus agreement re-runs **13/13 check + 5/5 synthesis**. **The proof, flipped:** Phase 80's `phase80_tokenize_one_eof_contradiction` was first run against the amended spec and failed at its row-1-refutation assertion — the flip is the amendment working, not a test bug — and is re-stated as `phase82_tokenize_one_eof_admitted_amendment_a` on the same point machinery. At the spec's own end-of-input test point the golden output is now ADMITTED: row 1 is Proven under it (the added disjunct is the point's own truth, 0 >= len("") = 0), row 2 Proven as before, and the free-tag case analysis is SAT in every case — including (is_ok ∧ 0 tokens), whose UNSAT was the contradiction, and (is_ok ∧ 2 tokens), whose flip to SAT is the amendment's named residue, measured rather than smoothed: **at end-of-input inputs the amended rows constrain nothing further about the output** (row 2's own disjunct is equally true there), and the golden tests are the only judge at EOF. The row's teeth are pinned at the keyword-spec point: a mid-input no-growth Ok is still REFUTED by row 1 (false || 0 == 0 + 1 || 0 >= 4) and the mirror's one-token output is Proven — the amendment did not vacate the row (the rejected option C's failure mode). **Phase 83's contract, measured** (new test `phase82_tokenize_one_mirror_body_vs_amended_rows`): the mirror-transcribed tokenize_one body, judged whole by `verify_one` under the module-aware verifier, returns **Inconclusive** — outside the encodable subset twice over, both blockers named: its ident arm is a While (an unencodable `sym_eval` arm — the standing loop boundary), and its first step calls `skip_whitespace`, for which the resolver denotes NO body (its two stated rows are field inequalities; pinned in the test: `callee_body("skip_whitespace")` is None while `callee_body("peek")` is Some since Phase 81) — so a verifying Phase 83 body must be an encodable formulation of the behavior, not this transcription. The mirror's own outputs, judged at the point per cross-validation battery class (input at pos 0, no tokens): ADMITTED (both rows Proven) at EOF (`""`), ident (`"spec"`), leading whitespace (`"  spec"` — carried by the growth disjunct; the input is not at EOF), newline run (`"\n\nx"`), and single char (`":"`); REFUTED at exactly two classes — the **whitespace-only-remainder corner** (`" "`: the mirror skips to end-of-input from a non-EOF input and returns Ok without growth; amended row 1 Refuted — the new disjunct reads the INPUT's pos, so it does not license the corner — and row 2 Proven) and the **unexpected-character class** (`"5"`: the mirror's Err; row 2 Refuted, unamended; row 1 satisfied via its is_err disjunct). At both refuted classes the rows jointly admit ONLY an Ok carrying exactly one more token (pinned: Ok/+1 proves against both rows at both points; at the corner, row 2 bars the Err that row 1 alone would have allowed) — so under the amended rows **no body is simultaneously Z3-Verified and mirror-exact on the full battery**: Phase 83's template either deviates at exactly these two classes, paying the executed-agreement cost there visibly, or the classes return to the owner. **Instruments re-run (probe + cross-validation, 3 runs each):** tokenize_one stays seedless, winners Inconclusive (tpr 0.000), executed agreement 0/10; its VerificationFailed candidate tally is **unmoved at 12 across the 3 runs** — the refuted pool members are the test-shaped Match bodies, refuted on mid-input behavior the amendment does not touch, exactly as the flipped proof's teeth section predicts. tokenize_all (spec unamended): winners Inconclusive in all 3 runs this time (Phase 79 drew one VerificationFailed crown under the all-survivors-refuted fallback), executed 0/8, refuted tally 16 → 12 — randomized variance on an untouched spec, reported as such. Everything else stands at Phase 81's measurements (classifiers EXACT 774/774 Verified; peek and next_char Verified seed crowns; skip_whitespace Verified at 4/12 by design). **Fixtures, recorded:** the Phase 76 scratch module copies (`RESTORED_TOKENIZER_MODULE` in eval.rs / verifier.rs) deliberately keep the pre-amendment row text — they are historical fixtures whose pinned assertions (both partial bodies refuted) hold under both texts, and the gate judges the examples file, as Phase 80 established. **Suites: 484/0/26 default, 484/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 400/0/19 no-Z3** — the +1 delta over Phase 81's 483/483/400 is exactly the new mirror-body test (Z3-gated; the proof flip is a re-statement, net-zero; the browser configuration is unchanged by construction). Next: the generation campaign (Phases 83–84), then the gate re-run (Phase 85).

> **Phase 83 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-83-amend-feasibility`) — amendment round-2 FEASIBILITY for `tokenize_one`: measured, nothing amended.** Phase 82's contract left the generation template a choice between deviating at exactly two battery classes or returning them to the owner: class W (whitespace-only remainder — the mirror skips to end-of-input from a non-EOF input and returns Ok without growth; amendment A's row-1 disjunct reads the INPUT's pos and refutes it) and class E (unexpected character — the mirror's mid-input Err; the unamended row 2 licenses Err only at end-of-input and refutes it). This phase measures whether spec-side round-2 formulations can license the mirror's behavior at both classes, before anyone builds a template (L–XL) against rows that would force it to fabricate a token at W and swallow the error at E. Method: candidate rows substituted into an in-memory copy of the examples module (the file on disk is never touched; the scratch builder asserts both current row lines occur exactly once and the scratch module typechecks whole), judged through the production encoder + verifier in the Phase 80/82 point style — five new Z3-gated tests in `src/verify/verifier.rs`. **Candidate A′ (row 1, output-pos disjunct) — ENCODES, closes W.** Row 1's third disjunct becomes `output.unwrap().pos >= len(output.unwrap().source)` — a statement about the OUTPUT state reaching end-of-input (the pure output-side form is the one measured; a mixed form reading `len(state.source)` was not needed, and the pure form is the faithful statement: the row should speak about the output it licenses). Case table (row 1 alone): the golden EOF output Proven (0 >= 0, as under A); the class-W mirror output Proven (output pos 1 >= len 1 — amendment A refutes the same point, re-measured in the same test so the contrast is one instrument's); the teeth hold (a mid-input no-growth Ok at a token start Refuted: false || 0 == 0 + 1 || 0 >= 4); the keyword-spec Ok/+1 Proven by growth. Two residues, measured and named: (1) A′ admits a no-growth Ok whose pos JUMPS to end-of-input — the row observes only (tag, token length, output pos, output source length), so it cannot distinguish "skipped whitespace to EOF" from "teleported to EOF" (amendment A refutes that output; the golden tests and executed agreement remain its judge, the division of labor A already accepts at EOF inputs); (2) at EOF inputs the row constrains nothing further (an Ok/+2 is admitted, exactly as under A). The literal-Err point stays the encoder's named unencodable case (the row routes through `unwrap`) — unchanged by A′. A′ also encodes against symbolic shapes (free state; point and fully free Ok payloads). **Candidate R2-α (row 2, pos-indexed Err license) — ENCODES in both forms, closes E except one subclass.** Row 2 gains a disjunct licensing Err when the char at the input's current position is not a token start: not ident-start, not whitespace, not one of the mirror's nine single-char punctuations {':','{','}','(',')',',','<','>','='}. Two formulations measured side by side — **callee** (the license in the module's own classifier vocabulary: `is_ident_start(state.source[state.pos]) == false && is_whitespace(state.source[state.pos]) == false &&` nine indexed-char disequalities; the callees inline through `sym_invoke` with the indexed char as argument) and **direct** (the classifiers spelled out as range/equality comparisons on the indexed char, no callees). Case table (row 2 alone, Err at pos 0), identical for both forms: `"5"` **Proven** (class E admitted); `"spec"` Refuted (teeth: Err at an identifier start stays unlicensed); `":"` Refuted (a punctuation token start); `" "` Refuted (whitespace at pos — the corner's output stays Ok-only); `"é"` Proven (a non-ASCII bad char — the mirror errors, α licenses); `""` **Unknown** — a literal-point artifact, not a formulation failure: the row now mentions `state.source[state.pos]`, and a constant out-of-bounds index into a literal string is exactly what `sym_index` refuses (it mirrors the interpreter's failure where the value is known). **The symbolic index, the brief's open question, is measured closed**: both forms encode under a fully free state (the index takes the CharStr bridge form, `str.at` of a symbolic index; the callee form inlines both classifiers over the CharStr argument), and under that path the EOF admission is PROVEN (with len(source) == 0 ∧ pos == 0 asserted, the row's negation has no model — the license is the row's own EOF disjunct) and the digit admission is proven symbolically too (source == "5" ∧ pos == 0: no countermodel — the license fires through the bridge, not only at literal points); a no-facts control is Refuted, so the row genuinely discriminates rather than merely encoding. **The approximation boundary, stated exactly:** α reads the char at the PRE-skip position, so at `" 5"` (leading whitespace, then a bad char) it judges the space, not the '5' — the mirror's Err there stays **Refuted**, and the joint rows admit only Ok/+1. α closes class E except its whitespace-then-bad-char subclass. **Candidate R2-β (formulations over the denoted machinery) — both fail, measured.** β-peek (license Err when `peek(state)` is none of the thirteen enumerable non-error chars) admits the class-E Err at `"5"` — and admits Err at `"spec"` too: an Option<Char> payload cannot be range-tested in the constraint dialect (the D1a table has no Option projection), so identifier starts are invisible to it and the row's teeth fail; an over-license is not a formulation. β-skip (the license stated at the SKIPPED position — the only form that would judge `" 5"` as the mirror does) encodes NOWHERE: `callee_body("skip_whitespace")` is None (re-pinned in this module), so any row mentioning `skip_whitespace(state)` is unencodable as a whole, at the very points it was written for. What a skip denotation would even mean, priced: it cannot be DERIVED — skip_whitespace's two rows (pos monotone; source length preserved) are satisfied by the identity and by every partial skip, so they underdetermine the function; the G-campaign precedent is a spec-keyed TEMPLATE (a hand-authored body keyed on the spec's identity), but that body is a While loop and `sym_eval` has no While arm, so inlining it into a constraint would STILL be unencodable. A skip denotation therefore does not serve R2 at any price short of an encoder extension (bounded loop unrolling or Forall — Phase 80's option-D boundary): encoder research, not a spec formulation. The whitespace-then-bad-char subclass is not closable from the spec side. **Joint satisfiability (A′ + R2-α):** the Phase 82 per-class table re-run against the joint rows — the mirror's outputs are admitted at ALL SEVEN classes (EOF: row 1 Proven at the point, row 2's admission proven on the symbolic path, the literal point carrying the named OOB artifact; ident, leading-whitespace, newline-run, single-char: Proven/Proven; the corner: Proven/Proven — class W closed; `"5"`: row 2 Proven at the point, row 1 discharged through the free tag as in Phases 80/82 — class E closed). The teeth cases stay refuted (no-growth Ok at a token start; Err at ident, punctuation, and whitespace positions). The one surviving residue is the `" 5"` subclass above: under the joint rows a template still deviates there or the subclass is accepted as a named executed-agreement gap. Disturbance: the scratch module differs from the real module in tokenize_one's rows ONLY (every other spec's postconditions, tokenize_all's included, Debug-identical — asserted in the joint test); the examples file is untouched, and both tokenizer instruments re-ran at their standing measurements (peek/next_char Verified seed crowns in all runs; tokenize pair seedless). **Suites: 489/0/26 default, 489/0/26 bootstrap-codegen, 400/0/19 no-Z3** — the +5 delta over Phase 82's 484/484/400 is exactly the five new measurement tests (Z3-gated; the browser configuration is unchanged by construction). **The priced option set for owner ratification** (applying any of options 1–4 is one phase in Phase 82's shape — amend, re-state the proofs, re-sync the playground snapshot, re-run the instruments; the generation campaign, L–XL, then builds against whichever rows are ratified): **(1) RECOMMENDED — A′ + R2-α (callee form).** Closes W and E; the template is built against rows the mirror satisfies at every battery class except the `" 5"` subclass. Residues: the whitespace-then-bad-char subclass (template deviates there, or it is carried as a named gap); A′'s pos-teleport admission (tests/executed agreement judge it); at EOF inputs the rows constrain nothing (as under A). The callee form is recommended over the direct form on spec-craft grounds — it states the license in the module's own classifier vocabulary, so the license cannot drift from the classifiers it names; the two forms measured identically everywhere. **(2) A′ + R2-α (direct form).** Same closures, same residues; the classifier logic is duplicated inline in row 2 and can drift from `is_ident_start`/`is_whitespace` if those ever change. **(3) A′ only.** Closes W; class E returns to the template as a deviation — it must return Ok/+1 where the mirror errors, i.e. a tokenizer that silently tokenizes garbage input (or the class stays a named gap and no body is Verified-and-exact). **(4) R2-α only (either form).** Closes E; class W deviates — the template must fabricate a token on a whitespace-only remainder, a token the mirror does not produce, which `tokenize_all` would then emit. **(5) Neither (Phase 82 status quo).** The template deviates at both classes, paying both semantic costs above. No option closes the `" 5"` subclass from the spec side; it is priced separately as encoder research, not as a sixth option. Next: owner ratification of the round-2 amendment, then the generation campaign against the ratified rows, then the gate re-run.

The one surviving deviation is pinned at its own point: at the whitespace-then-bad-char input the mirror's Err stays Refuted (α reads the pre-skip char, a space) and only the fabricated Ok/+1 is admitted. **Phase 83's scratch apparatus is retired:** the substitution builder (`phase83_scratch_module`) and the candidate-row constants are removed, and its five measurement tests are disposed of — the A′ and joint tests' surviving assertions are folded into the battery above (the A/A′ contrast assertion is dropped: its premise is what this phase removes); > **Phase 85 (2026-11-22, branch `feat/m1-features-85-generation`) — the tokenize generation campaign, first half (Phase 80 remainder (iii), leg 1 + leg 2): the missing IR renderings land in every Rust emitter, and seed family #11 (`tokenize_template`) puts mirror-shaped tokenize bodies into the pools. Two findings headline: peek and next_char go from executed 0/39 to executed 39/39; and the playground bundle crosses its budget — recorded below, NOT re-baselined (an owner-visible act).** **Leg 1 — the emitter leg (scoped unfreeze, Phase 29/39/77 form: new arms only; the corpus DID exercise the shapes, so the cascade ran in-phase by the Phase 78 discipline — the "owes rows" alternative did not arise).** The native IR walker (`ir_to_rust_expr` + the statement walker, through one shared helper `ir_call_text`) gains: **Call "List"** → `vec![...]` (the spec-Expr walker has rendered this since Phase 55; the IR walker never had it — the asymmetry was the E0425 in tokenize_one's winner); **Call "None" with no arguments** → the bare value `None` (the E0618 class); **Call "len" with exactly one argument**, keyed on the argument's shape — a field-access or string-literal argument (a String in every measured body) renders `({arg}).chars().count() as i64`, every other shape `({arg}).len() as i64` (the E0425 class; the keying's named boundary: a List-typed record field in the field-access position would take the char form and fail loudly at rustc — no measured body carries that shape, and `len` over the tokens field arrives as a MethodCall, which lowers to a Hole); **the Index arm on a string base** (field-access or string-literal) renders `({base}).chars().nth(({idx}) as usize).unwrap_or('\0')` — character semantics per the interpreter and the CharStr bridge (`chars().count()`, not bytes — the battery carries "é😀x"), out-of-bounds totalized to the NUL sentinel (the bridge's empty-string totalization read as a char; a genuine NUL in source text collides with the sentinel — recorded); other bases keep the corpus-pinned `({base})[{idx}]`; and **the RecordLit field-access value** renders `{base}.clone()` where Phase 77 pinned the bare field — a lurking E0382 the campaign's own bodies exposed (next_char's record reads the state after `next_char(state)` semantics move it in caller bodies), with Phase 77's golden re-stated in place, reason recorded in the test. **Cascade (the corpus exercised the shapes through exactly four M4 files — the tokenizer module's four record-spec winners):** six new Codegen specs (module **123 → 129**): `rust_call_form` (the IR Call dispatch — plain / List→vec / bare None / len — String-keyed by effective name with the arity folded into the key by the walker, seedless by design on the `rust_expr_method_form` precedent, its complete 7-test exemplar set stating the classification; the pool crowns a Verified Mutation Match, 0.985, tpr 1.000, deterministically), `rust_call_len_form` (the len argument-shape classification, guard_table-seeded), `rust_call_len_text` / `rust_call_len_char_text` (the two len texts as equations), `rust_index_form` (the index-base classification, kind 28 or string literal → char form; its fallthrough rows stand in leaf form — `pair_guard_table` refuses conjunction leaves), `rust_index_char_text` (the char-index text as an equation); the docs of `rust_call_text`, `rust_index_text`, `rust_record_lit_text`, `rust_stmt_call_text` and `rust_stmt_expr_text` amended to the dispatched reality (the statement-Call fallback template already existed and is reused — a seventh planned spec proved unnecessary). Proof corpus +13 cases (11 IR-expression, 2 statement); cross-validation bookkeeping re-derived — module totals **4,321 cases / 3,403 pinned / 911 spec-silent**, golden 381 standing with 19 new golden cases in the new specs' arms; probe ×3 deterministic: **129 specs, seeds 124/129** (seedless quintet: the standing quartet + `rust_call_form`), **Verified 129/129** in every run. **Gates:** M3 **PASS** — byte-identical over **2,706 cases** (2,639 + 13 proof + 6 winner files + 48 subtree renderings), mode A divergences only the recorded F5 class; M4 **155/155** byte-identical (the four tokenizer divergences closed); artifact regenerated through the documented path only, 121,245 → **130,142 B**, byte-identity PASS, the 9-shim composition unchanged (no new shim — the rows are winner-carried), strip count 60 → 64, winner provenance re-recorded (124 seeds: equation 66, guard_table 40, composition_guard 7, pair_guard_table 5, expr_pin_guard 3, guarded_identity 1, multi_guard 1, mixed_pair_guard_table 1; plus 5 seedless); S1 adapter equivalence PASS (byte-identical over all 2,706 M3 cases + 155 M4 files, equal to the interpreter composition everywhere); S2 roundtrip 4/4 and behavioral 5/5 in both configurations. **Leg 1.3 — the headline:** with the renderings in place, the Phase 81 seed winners' generated code compiles and runs in the cross-validation runner — **peek executed agreement 0/39 → 39/39 and next_char 0/39 → 39/39** (plus next_char's secondary full-state battery 39/39), in all 3 harness runs, winners unchanged (complementary_guard / record_completion seeds, Verified, tpr 1.000). tokenize_one's crowned winner still executes 0/10 — its failure class narrowed to a single E0308 inside the junk Mutation body; tokenize_all 0/8 likewise. **Leg 2 — the template leg (seed family #11).** `tokenize_template` registers after `record_completion`, before `char_equation`, keyed on the tokenize pair's shape (the Phase 80 (iii) form): the STEP spec (one record param declaring source: String, pos/line/col: Int, tokens: List of a token record declaring kind/lexeme/line/col; postconditions carrying an is_err/is_ok method call and an unwrap call — the amended rows' shape; the module denoting skip_whitespace (R→R), peek (R→Option<Char>), next_char (R→R), is_ident_start and is_ident_continue (Char→Bool) by name and signature) and the LOOP spec (a `Result<List<Token>, String>` return over the same state record; the step spec found in the index by the step keying itself, candidates sorted by name). The STEP body is the mirror's shape: Alloc the skipped state → Match on peek — None arm returns Ok(state); Some arm dispatches: the ident arm consumes through a While over peek/is_ident_continue appending chars and building the token RecordLit (kind 0, stamped from the post-skip state — D4a START), the nine punctuation arms build their single-char tokens (kinds 3–12 in the mirror's numbering) after one next_char, and the fallthrough returns Err. The LOOP body threads the state through a While (peek != None) of TryPropagate(step) and returns Ok(state.tokens). **Admission standard, stated in the builder: NO encodability gate** — the bodies carry While, which the encoder has no arm for; they are admitted on the char_equation precedent (Inconclusive by construction), and their evidence standard is executed agreement. **Measured** (the standing instrument `phase85_tokenize_template_measurement`): the family enters BOTH specs' pools in every run; Z3 verdict **Inconclusive, tpr 0.000** for both seeds (While, and the skip call, are NotEvaluable in-process — the prediction, confirmed not assumed); the template does NOT crown — tokenize_one still crowns its Verified Mutation (tpr 0.500), tokenize_all its junk; crowning was never the family's mechanism (an Inconclusive tpr-0 seed cannot outrank a Verified tpr-0.500 candidate under the standing selection). **Executed agreement** — the template bodies assembled with the production pools' own seed callees (peek = complementary_guard, next_char = record_completion, the classifiers = their equation seeds) plus a hand-supplied skip_whitespace mirror body (the Phase 76/81 hand-body precedent, labeled as such; skip_whitespace has no seed and no resolver denotation): **tokenize_one 9/10, tokenize_all 7/8** — and the ONLY disagreement in each battery is the error-message text: battery input `"5"` (resp. `"spec 5"`) — template `ERR "Unexpected character"` vs mirror `ERR "Unexpected character '5' at line 1"`. The constant message is a NAMED deviation, forced by the IR's expressiveness: the mirror's interpolated text needs char→String (available only as Append growth) and Int→String (the open finding F2) — no faithful rendering exists in IR today. Every other case agrees exactly: empty input, leading-whitespace skip, the whitespace-only remainder (the Phase 82 class W case `" "` — the template's Ok/+0 is the mirror's own output; the ROWS refute it, the body is faithful), newline line-tracking, the full punctuation run `"x{y}:(,)"`, multi-token streams. Two generation facts the measurement forced into the builder, recorded in its comments: generated callees take the state BY VALUE, so a call whose argument stays live passes `UnaryOp Clone` (E0382 otherwise — the peek/next_char seed bodies never hit this because their callees were inlined denotations, not calls); and Call arguments render untyped, so the Err message is bound in a typed Alloc first (E0308 otherwise). **The gate-line standing for the Phase 87 re-run, stated precisely:** after this phase, NO single body for either tokenize spec is both Z3-Verified and executed-exact. tokenize_one's CROWNED winner is Verified (Mutation, tpr 0.500 — the rows pin the Ok/Err split + growth, not exact outputs) but its executed agreement is 0/10 (its generated code still fails one E0308 in the runner). The template bodies are executed-near-exact (9/10, 7/8, the sole deviation the named message text) but Inconclusive. The exact technical reason is the **While/encoder boundary**: `sym_eval` has no While arm, so no body containing the tokenizing loop can verify — and skip_whitespace additionally has no resolver denotation (Phase 83: its two rows are satisfied by identity and every partial skip, so no denotation is derivable), meaning even a While-free step body could not inline the skip. What closing it takes is an ENCODER extension — bounded loop unrolling against the batteries' bounded inputs, or loop-invariant support in the verifier — priced **L–XL** in the audit's calibration (a new encoder subsystem with its own soundness evidence, larger than any single phase in this campaign), explicitly NOT built here and NOT attempted by seed or template machinery: no seed family can cross an encoder boundary. **Playground fallout — a budget crossing, flagged for the owner:** the bundle rebuilt at **700,674 B against the 673,792 B budget in `playground/budget.txt` — OVER by 26,882 B.** The growth is the accumulated compiler change since Phase 78's 653,970 B (the Phase 81 resolver builders, this phase's template builder, and the regenerated artifact's six new winners); corpus agreement against the rebuilt pkg/ re-runs **13/13 check + 5/5 synthesis** — the bundle is functionally sound; only its size crosses the recorded cap. The budget is NOT re-baselined in this phase: that is a deliberate owner-visible act (the W4 discipline), and it is owed a decision before the stack merges — either the owner re-baselines, or the merge carries a CI-red playground workflow. **Suites: 490/0/27 default, 490/0/27 bootstrap-codegen, 405/0/20 no-Z3** — the +5/+1 delta over the 485/485/400 baselines is exactly this phase's new tests (two codegen rendering tests, two engine template tests, one m3_proof golden test; +1 ignored = the measurement instrument); no pre-existing test moved except the one named Phase 77 golden re-state. Next: Phase 86 (the campaign's second half as measured), then the gate re-run (Phase 87) with the standing above as its tokenize input.

### M2: Pretty-Printer + Validator
- [x] Tulis `validator.morph` (type checker lite)
- [x] Tulis `printer.morph` (IR → text)
- [x] Same validation pipeline as M1
- [x] **Gate**: Both pass independently

> **Progress note (2026-11-20):** `examples/self_host/validator.morph` (7 specs: type-tag/node-kind domains, arity table, lite tag compatibility, depth guard) and `examples/self_host/printer.morph` (7 specs: type/kind/operator name lookups, precedence `needs_parens`, line-width rule) are written and are the first self-host specs verified end-to-end by the real pipeline: both pass `morphic check` (parser + typechecker in the compiled binary), enforced by `tests/m2_proof.rs` — which also carries pure-Rust mirror implementations with golden tests for every spec test case (the M0 Phase B pattern), plus negative controls proving `check` rejects invalid specs. Full suite: 59 passed / 0 failed.
>
> **Still open for the M2 gate:** the synthesis run has now been done (see the synthesis-run note below), but it did not produce a single compilable, test-passing implementation, and there is still no cross-validation of synthesized output against a Rust original — the Rust code in `tests/m2_proof.rs` is a hand-written mirror, not generated code. The gate therefore stays unchecked.
>
> **Baseline discovery while doing M2:** none of the pre-existing example specs (`sort.morph`, `binary_search.morph`, `functional_tokenizer.morph`, `self_host/is_sorted.morph`, `self_host/count_matching.morph`) currently pass `morphic check` — they use syntax the parser does not implement (no module wrapper, `if/then`, `implies`, record/list literals in tests, spaced operators; two files also trip a byte-boundary panic in the parser's error-snippet code at `src/spec/parser.rs:1094`). The M2 specs are written in the parser's actually-supported dialect, documented in their file headers. Wiring the older examples into `check` (or fixing the parser gaps) is future work, separate from M2.

> **Synthesis run (2026-11-20, branch `m2-synthesis-run`):** all 14 M2 specs were run through the real pipeline (parse → typecheck → synthesize → Z3 `verify_all` → `select_best` → codegen) via the test-only probe in `src/m2_synthesis_probe.rs` (`cargo test -- --ignored --nocapture m2_synthesis_probe`; generated winners are written to `target/m2-synthesis/`). Only opt level 1 can run: it is the sole LLM-free configuration. Opt levels 2–4 set `use_llm = true` and fail immediately in `seed_population` with `LlmError("No LLM backend enabled")` — the default build compiles neither LLM feature (`llm-local` / `llm-remote`), and no Ollama server exists in this environment (verified via CLI `build -O 2` / `-O 3` and locked in by the unit test `synthesis_opt3_blocked_without_llm_backend`). Candidate counts and times below are from one probe run; both vary between runs (randomized engine), while the Z3 verdicts and winner provenance were identical across 3 runs.
>
> | Spec | Synth (opt 1) | Candidates | Time | Z3 verdict (pipeline) | Winner | Holes in winner | Generated Rust compiles / passes spec tests |
> |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
> | validator `is_known_type_tag` | ok | 18 | 5 ms | 0/18 verified | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | validator `is_known_node_kind` | ok | 17 | 4 ms | 0/17 verified | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | validator `node_arity` | ok | 18 | 18 ms | **18/18 Verified** | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | validator `is_literal_kind` | ok | 19 | 5 ms | 0/19 verified | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | validator `tags_compatible` | ok | 11 | 202 ms | 0/11 verified | Mutation | 1–3 | no / n-a (no in-spec tests) |
> | validator `validate_kind_tag` | ok | 15 | 4 ms | 0/15 verified | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | validator `max_depth_ok` | ok | 19 | 4 ms | 0/19 verified | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | printer `type_tag_name` | ok | 17 | 215 ms | **17/17 Verified** | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | printer `node_kind_name` | ok | 19 | 227 ms | **19/19 Verified** | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | printer `binop_symbol` | ok | 18 | 183 ms | **18/18 Verified** | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | printer `needs_parens` | ok | 17 | 4 ms | 0/17 verified | Mutation | 0–1 | no / n-a (no in-spec tests) |
> | printer `is_infix_kind` | ok | 19 | 3 ms | 0/19 verified | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | printer `line_width_ok` | ok | 17 | 5 ms | 0/17 verified | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
> | printer `print_node_kind` | ok | 20 | 4 ms | 0/20 verified | TestInduced | 1 | no / no |
>
> **Why the run cannot close the gate — root causes, all verified in the code:**
> 1. **The Z3 stage is candidate-independent.** `Verifier::verify_one` (`src/verify/verifier.rs`) encodes only the spec's postconditions; the candidate body is never encoded (`is_well_formed` is a stub returning `true`). Its encoder maps boolean-valued subexpressions to int `0` (`encode_expr_int` fallback) and any `Or`-rooted formula to `true` (`encode_expr_bool` fallback), so the verdict is decided by constraint *syntax*: guard-style (`||`) lookup constraints always hold, `output == <bool expr>` equations never hold. The 4 "Verified" specs above are exactly the guard-style ones — a property of the spec text, identical for every candidate, not evidence that a synthesized implementation is correct.
> 2. **The search never executes tests.** `evaluate_candidate` (`src/synthesis/engine.rs`) returns `test_pass_ratio = 1.0` unconditionally and a constant `constraint_score = 0.7`; ranking is driven by AST-shape heuristics (depth/size) only.
> 3. **Winners keep their holes.** The test-induced seed's wildcard arm is a `Hole` and no pipeline stage fills it (the template builders also return bare holes); codegen renders holes as `panic!("Hole not filled")`. 13/14 winners carry ≥ 1 hole.
> 4. **Rust codegen defects (overlaps M3).** Spec test names are emitted verbatim as Rust fn identifiers — hyphens make 12/14 generated files unparseable; `Match` bodies are emitted as statements whose arm values are discarded (`value;`), which is E0308 once names are sanitized (proven on `is_literal_kind` and `node_kind_name`); unsupported IR in expression position becomes `/* UNSUPPORTED */` and unknown calls become `_()` (the two Mutation winners). Net: **0/14 generated files compile**, so 0/14 can be executed against their spec tests or cross-validated against the `tests/m2_proof.rs` mirrors.
>
> **Remaining for the M2 gate:** (a) an LLM backend (feature + server) or a scorer that actually executes spec tests, so the M1 pipeline shape (LLM seed → MCTS → Z3 → codegen) can run as described; (b) a verifier that encodes/evaluates the candidate itself, so "Verified" is a statement about the implementation; (c) the codegen fixes in (4) plus hole filling, so generated code compiles; only then (d) cross-validation against the mirrors. Until (a)–(c) land, "Same validation pipeline as M1" and the M2 gate stay unchecked — deliberately, per-spec evidence above.
>
> **Codegen fix follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `fix/codegen-output`):** defects (4a) and (4b) are fixed with regression tests — emitted Rust identifiers are sanitized (`rust_ident`: invalid chars → `_`, leading digit → `_` prefix, keywords → `_` suffix, applied at every Rust emission point), and match arms now yield their values as tail expressions (`ir_to_rust_tail`). Re-running the probe and compiling every winner with `rustc --edition 2021 --crate-type lib`: **8/14 compile** (was 0/14), **0/14 compile free of hole/unsupported markers** — every winner in this run still carries ≥ 1 hole (12× 1 hole, `needs_parens` 2, `tags_compatible` 3). The 6 remaining failures split into: 2 files (`needs_parens`, `tags_compatible`, the Mutation winners) still on defect (4c) — `_()` in an `if` condition does not parse; and 4 String-returning files (`binop_symbol`, `node_kind_name`, `print_node_kind`, `type_tag_name`) on a newly exposed defect (4d): `IRLiteral::String` is emitted as a `&str` literal while the spec return type is `String` (E0308) — previously masked by the `value;` discard. Fixing (4d), (4c), and hole filling (root cause 3) remains open, so item (c) above is only partially done.
>
> **Codegen fix follow-up 2 (2026-11-20, branch `fix/codegen-output-2`):** defect (4d) is FIXED with regression tests — codegen now threads the expected type to the emission points that know one (`ir_to_rust_expr_typed`: the spec return type for match arms and `Return` expressions, an `Alloc`'s declared type, a `Typed` node's own annotation), and only there is a string literal emitted owned (`"s".to_string()`); everywhere else it stays a `&str`, guarded by a negative test. Re-running the probe and compiling every winner as above: **12/14 compile** (was 8/14 — all 4 String-returning files now compile), **0/14 compile free of hole/unsupported markers** (unchanged: every winner in this run still carries exactly 1 hole, so the hole `panic!` marker remains in all 14; hole filling is root cause 3, still open). Defect (4c) is now diagnosed precisely and is NOT a codegen defect: the `_()` in the earlier run's `if` conditions is `IRNode::Call { function: "_", args: [] }`, emitted verbatim by codegen — the node itself is manufactured by the engine's random tree generator (`random_ir_tree`, `src/synthesis/engine.rs`, arm 3) as a placeholder leaf, a sibling of the real `Hole` leaves the same generator produces. It is an unfilled node in function position (engine territory, root cause 3 family); no function named `_` exists in any spec, template, or builtin, so there is nothing for codegen to emit — the fix belongs to the engine (stop generating placeholder calls, or fill them), and codegen was deliberately left unchanged for it. Variance note: winner shapes are randomized per run — in this run the two Mutation winners (`needs_parens`, `tags_compatible`) contained no `_()` at all and instead failed on E0425 (`return result;` after a `Hole` — the `Var("result")` leaf is another `random_ir_tree`/`random_filling` artifact). Item (c) above remains partially done: what is left is hole filling and the engine-side placeholder nodes.
>
> **Engine placeholder + hole-ranking follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `fix/engine-placeholders`):** defect (4c) is FIXED at the source — `random_ir_tree` arm 3 now emits an honest `Hole` instead of `Call { function: "_", args: [] }` (regression test: property sweep over generated trees asserting no `Call` to `"_"`; zero `_()` in every winner file since). Holes also cost something for the first time: `evaluate_candidate` decays correctness credit (`test_pass_ratio`, `constraint_score`) ×0.5 per hole in the body, and `select_best` hard-gates — if any hole-free candidate exists, a holed candidate cannot win; only when none exists does selection fall back to ranking the holed pool (unit tests cover both branches). Re-running the probe 3× and compiling every winner as above: **winner holes collapsed from 14× exactly 1 (every prior run) to 14× 0, 14× 0, and 13× 0 + 1× 1** — the single holed winner (`node_arity`, run 3) is the fallback case: no hole-free candidate reached its verified pool that run. **Compiles went DOWN: 0/14, 0/14, 1/14 (was 12/14); compiles free of hole/unsupported markers 0/14 (unchanged).** That is a change in *which* candidates win, not a codegen regression: the previously compiling winners were tidy trees carrying one hole, while the hole-free winners now crowned come from the random/Mutation family and fail on the next layer of generator artifacts — stray `Var("result")` leaves (E0425; the `random_ir_tree` arm 0 / `random_filling` family flagged in follow-up 2), unbound `a`/`b` vars (E0425), E0308 type mismatches, and `return` in statement position. Net: hole filling is no longer the binding constraint — the population produces hole-free candidates for nearly every spec — candidate *quality* is. The generator invents names that are never bound, and evaluation still cannot execute spec tests to tell a correct hole-free tree from a nonsensical one. Next blockers, in order: (1) stop the generator emitting free-variable leaves (`Var("result")`/`Var("output")` where nothing binds them) or bind them at codegen; (2) a scorer that actually executes spec tests (root cause (a) above), so ranking rewards correctness, not just completeness. Item (c) stays partially done. Suite after this branch: 89 passed / 0 failed / 3 ignored.
>
> **Engine test-execution follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `feat/engine-test-execution`):** blocker (2) above is FIXED — the engine now has a truth signal. New module `src/synthesis/eval.rs`: a total IR interpreter for the expression subset (Literal/Var/BinOp/UnaryOp with numeric promotion, If, Match over literal/variable/wildcard patterns, Let, Block, Return, `len` + the `List[...]` literal call, Collection/Index, Typed). Everything outside the subset — Hole, loops, Alloc/Assign, lambdas, Map/Filter/Fold/Sort, unknown calls (including template pseudo-calls like `first`/`rest`), guard patterns — returns `NotEvaluable` with a reason; the interpreter never panics and never guesses. No evaluator existed before (the only matches for "eval" in the tree were `evaluate_candidate` itself). Spec tests turned out to be directly usable: `Test.input`/`expected_output` are AST `Expr`s, mapped to evaluator values by `value_of_expr` (literals directly; composites lowered through the engine's own `expr_to_ir`, the same conversion `seed_from_tests` uses). Single-param specs bind the input value to the parameter; multi-param specs would need a List input of matching arity — in practice they carry no `.morph` tests at all, so nothing is lost today. `evaluate_candidate` now measures `test_pass_ratio = passed / evaluable` by actually running the candidate body (`run_spec_tests`), still ×0.5 per hole; a body that cannot be evaluated on a test FAILS that test, while a test that cannot be *mapped* is excluded from the denominator. Specs with tests but zero mappable ones keep the legacy structural score, marked `FALLBACK` in code; test-less specs keep the neutral 0.5. Decisive tests: a hand-built correct `is_literal_kind` candidate scores exactly 1.0, an always-false one exactly 2/3, an unbound-`Var("result")` body 0.0, and the TestInduced match shape (1 wildcard Hole) scores measured 1.0 × 0.5 hole decay = 0.5. Probe (3 runs, `winner_tpr` is the new probe field): all 42 winners Mutation, all hole-free; **measured winner tpr is 0.000 in 34/42** — the signal's first verdict is that the crowned winners are semantically wrong, including several Z3-verified ones (`node_arity`, `type_tag_name`, `node_kind_name`, `binop_symbol`: verified 13–20/13–20 yet tpr 0.000 in most runs — the Z3 constraint encoding and actual test behavior still tell different stories). Non-zero glimpses exist (`is_known_type_tag` 0.600, `line_width_ok` 0.667, `node_arity` 0.400, `is_infix_kind` 0.333 in individual runs) but no winner reached 1.0, and run-3 winners still compile 0/14. `tags_compatible`/`needs_parens` sit at the 0.500 no-tests neutral in every run, as designed. Why truth does not yet crown winners: the composite score still gives a flat placeholder `constraint_score` 0.7 to every hole-free body, and the GA's 10 generations can lose the TestInduced seed (which would measure 0.5 after hole decay) before final scoring — ranking now *can* tell correct from compiling-random, but selection pressure is not yet dominated by it. Next blockers, in order: (1) generator free-variable leaves (`Var("result")`/`Var("output")`) — still the compile killer; (2) make measured tpr dominate selection (revisit composite weights / the placeholder constraint score, and protect the TestInduced seed through the GA); (3) reconcile Z3 verification with executed behavior. Suite after this branch: 106 passed / 0 failed / 3 ignored.
>
> **Engine truth-selection follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `fix/engine-truth-selection`):** blockers (1) and (2) above are FIXED. (1) Generation is scope-aware: `random_ir_tree` threads a scope (spec parameters; its own `For` variable extends it in the loop body) and draws `Var` leaves only from it via `random_scoped_leaf` (literal fallback when empty); a new `scope_at_path` walks a hole's path collecting enclosing binders (`Let`/`For`/Lambda/Closure/match-pattern), and `random_filling` + `generate_hole_fillings` — which hardcoded `Var("result")`/`Var("output")` and `Var("x")` / `a + b` — now draw only from that scope. Property test: every `Var` in generated trees must be in scope (failed before: `Var("result")` with scope `[]`). (2) Measured truth dominates selection: `composite_score` weights are now 0.85 tpr / 0.05 constraint / 0.05 complexity / 0.05 quality, so a tpr gap of ≥0.25 (worth 0.2125) always beats the maximum combined swing of the other dimensions (0.15); `mcts_evaluate`'s private copy of the old weights now calls `composite_score`, so the invariant holds for the MCTS reward, GA fitness, `score_and_rank`, and `verify_all`'s re-scoring alike. And the TestInduced seed is protected: `run()` snapshots it after seeding and re-admits it just before final scoring if the search dropped it (structural presence via Debug fingerprint — `IRNode` has no `PartialEq`); it is re-scored on the same footing as everyone else, and winning is still earned (the completeness gate still bars the holed seed when a hole-free candidate exists). A unit test locks the seed's presence in the synthesized pool for `is_literal_kind` at its measured tpr 0.5. Probe (3 runs, same method as above): all 42 winners Mutation, all hole-free — unchanged. **Winner tpr 0.000: 29/42 (was 34/42); non-zero winners 13/42 (was 8/42); max still 0.667, none reached 1.0.** Directionally consistent with the mechanism (the pool is now sorted tpr-first, so criteria ties in `select_best` break toward measured truth), but modest — treat as movement, not proof. **Compiles: 0/14, 0/14, 0/14 — the count did not move, the failure class did: E0425 (free variables) now occurs in 0 of 42 winner files** (the class is gone from winners entirely); 40 files fail E0308 (type mismatch — e.g. a `vec![]` body for a Bool spec, `0;` as an i64 body) and 1 fails E0069. Generation is now scope-valid but still not type-directed. Why winners still measure 0 for most specs — the search itself, in order: (a) the MCTS explores a *single* root — the first node is initialized from one random population member and selection never starts another root, so the harvest is ~10–20 completions of one usually-random member, and completions of the TestInduced seed (whose wildcard hole any menu filling would complete into a test-passing, hole-free body) almost never enter the pool; (b) the variation operators are stubs — 4 of 6 mutation kinds are no-ops, `random_subtree` returns a whole-tree clone so crossover swaps nothing — at opt level 1 there is effectively no variation engine beyond MCTS hole filling; (c) with (a)+(b), the hole-free pool for most specs contains no candidate passing even one test, so tpr-dominant ranking has nothing true to crown, and the gate (by design) keeps the 0.5-tpr holed seed from winning. Next blockers, in order: (1) make the MCTS harvest completions of *every* population member (or at least every seed), not one random root; (2) real mutation operators (subtree replacement that actually selects a subtree; the no-op stubs filled in); (3) type-directed generation/selection so bodies are at least type-plausible for the spec's return type; (4) reconcile Z3 verification with executed behavior (carried over). Suite after this branch: 114 passed / 0 failed / 3 ignored.

> **Engine seed-harvest follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `feat/mcts-seed-harvest`):** blocker (1) above is FIXED — and fixing it exposed a deeper one underneath. Two changes. (a) `replace_at_path`, the walker under `replace_hole` (used by MCTS expansion AND rollout), implemented only the `Block` path step; every other `PathElem` (`MatchCase`, `IfThen`/`IfElse`, `LetBody`, `BinOp*`, …) fell into a no-op arm, so a hole anywhere except under pure `Block` nesting was silently left in place: expansion children were still-holed clones of their parent (never terminal, never harvested) and rollouts could not fill such holes either. It now mirrors `scope_at_path`'s traversal for every variant (unit test covers `MatchCase`/`IfElse`/`LetBody`/nested paths; failed before, at the match arm). This retroactively corrects the Phase-3/8 picture of the harvest: with the walker broken, MCTS contributed ~nothing and the crowned "Mutation" winners were effectively raw random seeds. (b) The MCTS now roots a tree at the TestInduced seed too: the per-tree loop/harvest was extracted to `mcts_harvest_tree(root, kind)`; `mcts_search` runs it at one random member (pass 1, as before) and once per holed TestInduced seed (pass 2, provenance `mutation_kind: "mcts_seed_harvest"`), so seed completions enter the final pool deterministically rather than when the seed happened to be the random root (~1 in 20 runs). Evidence chain for the new pipeline test (the pool returned by `synthesize(is_literal_kind, 1)` contains a hole-free seed completion measuring tpr 1.0): failed 5/5 before either fix, still failed 3/3 with (a) alone, passes 5/5 with both. Probe (3 runs, same method): **winner tpr 1.000 in 36/42 — every tested spec in every run; winner tpr 0.000: 0/42 (was 29/42); the remaining 6 are `tags_compatible`/`needs_parens` at the 0.500 no-tests neutral, as designed.** All 42 winners hole-free, all Mutation provenance. **Compiles: 1/14 in each run (was 0/14)** — `node_arity` in all three runs; the other 39 files fail E0308 (37×) or E0069 (2×), and E0425 remains 0/42. The new winners are visibly the seed completions (per-test match arms verbatim), and the compile blocker is now precisely located: the wildcard arm's filling is type-blind — `is_literal_kind` (returns Bool) gets `_ => 0`, `type_tag_name` (returns String) gets `_ => 0` — behaviorally unreachable by the spec's tests, so the interpreter honestly scores it 1.0, but rustc rejects the arm's type. The hole even carries `expected_type: Some(return_type)`; nothing reads it. Next blockers, in order: (1) type-directed fillings — `generate_hole_fillings`/`random_filling` must draw from the hole's expected type; this is now THE compile blocker for otherwise-correct winners, and fixing it should take most of the 12 tested specs from tpr-1.0-but-uncompilable to tpr-1.0-and-compiling; (2) real variation operators (carried over, re-verified this session and worse than reported: 5 of the 6 mutation helpers — `inline_random_call`, `swap_equivalent`, `optimize_loop`, `add_parallel_hint`, `random_rewrite` — are literally empty functions, and the sixth routes through `random_subtree`, which returns a whole-tree clone; `mutate` is also GA-only, so it never runs at opt level 1 at all) — matters for diversity beyond the seed, less urgent now that the seed harvest dominates; (3) reconcile Z3 verification with executed behavior (carried over; the disagreement has changed shape — in all 3 runs exactly 4 winners are Z3-verified and all 4 measure tpr 1.000, but the other 8 tpr-1.000 winners per run are verified=false, so Z3 still certifies a different, narrower property than the tests measure). Suite after this branch: 116 passed / 0 failed / 3 ignored.

> **Engine type-directed-fillings follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `feat/type-directed-fillings`):** blocker (1) above is FIXED. The hole's `expected_type` was recorded by seed construction but unreachable by the fillers — `HoleRef` carried only a path. `HoleRef` now captures `expected_type` where holes are found (`find_holes_rec`), and both filling entry points read it. `typed_literal_fillings(ty)` provides the literal menu per type (Bool → true/false; the integer family → 0/1; Float → 0.0; String → `""`; Char → `'a'`; Unit → `()`; List → empty list; Tuple composed recursively) and returns None for types with no honest literal menu — named/generic/function/Map/Set/Array, and Option (the IR has no Option literal node) — which keep the legacy menu untouched, as do all untyped holes (`expected_type: None`). For a typed hole, `generate_hole_fillings` returns the typed literals plus the spec tests' distinct expected outputs (return-typed by construction, admitted only through the conservative `node_matches_type` check, which recognizes only literal/collection shapes whose type is certain); `random_filling` draws from the typed menu only — no wrong-typed literal, no Return wrapper, no fresh Hole. Test-first: five new tests failed before the fix on exactly `Literal(Int(0))` evaluated for Bool/String/List holes and on a rollout-drawn unfilled Hole; a sixth guards that untyped holes keep the legacy menu. Probe (3 runs, same method): **winner tpr distribution unchanged — 36/42 at 1.000, 6/42 at the 0.500 no-tests neutral, 0/42 at 0.000; all 42 winners hole-free, all Mutation provenance. Compiles: 12/14 in every run (was 1/14)** — every tested spec now produces a compiling winner (wildcard arms read `_ => true`, `_ => "".to_string()` instead of `_ => 0`). Error classes across the 42 winner files collapse from 37× E0308 + 2× E0069 (Phase 9) to **9× E0308, 0× E0069, 0× E0425**. The only failing files are the two test-less specs (`tags_compatible`, `needs_parens`) in all 3 runs: with no tests there is no TestInduced seed and no truth signal, so their winners are still arbitrary random trees whose whole bodies are type-wrong at the top level (a `for` loop as the body of a Bool function; `vec![]` then `return op;` in a Bool function — the latter also trips a codegen statement-emission quirk, a missing `;` after the `vec![]` statement). Phase 9's 2× E0069 (a bare `return;` in a non-unit function) did not recur in 42 files: the Rust statement emitter always writes `return {expr};`, so those two were most likely random-tree winners whose `Return` wrapped an expression shape the expression emitter renders empty — that shape no longer wins anywhere, but with only two sightings ever, the class is dormant, not proven extinct. Next blockers, in order: (1) the test-less specs — nothing anywhere checks a candidate body's type against the spec's return type: generation is type-blind at the whole-body level, test-less scoring is the neutral 0.5, and selection cannot tell a Bool-bodied candidate from a loop-bodied one. A return-type check/filter for candidates (or tests for those two specs) is the remaining compile gap; it is a selection/generation discipline issue, not a filling issue. (2) Real variation operators (carried over, unchanged: 5 of the 6 mutation helpers are literally empty functions, the sixth routes through whole-tree-clone `random_subtree`, and `mutate` is GA-only so it never runs at opt level 1) — matters for diversity beyond the seed. (3) Reconcile Z3 verification with executed behavior (carried over, same shape: 12/42 winners are Z3-verified and all 12 measure tpr 1.000, but 24 of the 36 tpr-1.000 winners are verified=false, so Z3 still certifies a different, narrower property than the tests measure). Suite after this branch: 122 passed / 0 failed / 3 ignored.

> **Engine return-type-gate follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `fix/candidate-return-type-gate`):** blocker (1) above is FIXED on the selection side — and the measurement shows precisely why selection alone cannot finish the job. `selector::return_type_verdict` is a conservative type inference over IR candidate bodies, mirroring the interpreter's evaluable subset: a type is claimed only when certain (literals; collections whose elements are certain and agree, with a wildcard for unprovable positions; BinOp/UnaryOp under certain operand rules; If/Match whose branches all agree; Let/Block tail expressions; Vars resolved against the spec's parameter types; known pure builtins — `len` → Int, the parser's `List` literal; Typed nodes), and anything else is Unknown, which is neutral. A provably mistyped `return` anywhere in the body is also a mismatch (rustc checks every return against the signature); integer-family types unify; a Generic in any position proves nothing. `select_best` applies a return-type gate immediately after the completeness gate, mirroring its shape: provably mistyped candidates are excluded while a provably matching or Unknown candidate exists, and if EVERY candidate is provably mistyped the gate falls back to ranking that pool unchanged (documented in the gate comment). Composite weights, tpr semantics and `evaluate_candidate` are untouched, and a regression test proves the tpr-1.0 seed-completion winner on a tested spec still wins with the gate active. The precise composed guarantee — whenever the hole-free pool contains a non-mistyped candidate, the winner is not mistyped — is locked in by a probe-pipeline integration test over both test-less specs; because the engine is randomized and the premise is not structural (measured premise rate ≈ 0.7 per synthesis attempt per spec), the test retries synthesis up to 8 times per spec and was verified stable over 30 consecutive runs. Probe (3 runs, same method, gate only): **winner tpr distribution unchanged — 36/42 at 1.000, 6/42 at the 0.500 no-tests neutral, 0/42 at 0.000; all 42 winners hole-free, all Mutation provenance; Z3-verified winners 12/42, all measuring tpr 1.000. Compiles: 12/14, 13/14, 12/14 (was 12/14 in every run). Error classes across the 42 files: 8× E0308, 0× E0069, 0× E0425 (was 9× E0308), plus 2 files failing on a codegen syntax slip (below).** The gate demonstrably works — provably mistyped bodies no longer win when a sound alternative exists — but compiles barely moved, for two measured reasons. (a) In roughly half of raw draws, a test-less spec's hole-free pool is UNIFORMLY mistyped (every MCTS completion inherits a certainly-wrong root shape — arithmetic roots, Int literals, Int `return`s), so the documented fallback crowns a mistyped winner anyway (2 of the 5 failing files in the final measurement are exactly this: bodies containing `return actual;` / `return parent_op;` in Bool functions). A selection gate cannot crown what the pool does not contain; the discipline gap is now in generation, not selection. (b) The most common surviving shape is a For-rooted body (`for i in 0..10 { … }` as a Bool function body): loops are Unknown by design — the interpreter cannot evaluate them either — so the gate is blind to them, while the emitted Rust gives a `for` tail type `()`, hence E0308. The codegen slip named in the previous note DID still appear in winner files after the gate (2 of 3 gate-only runs: `vec![]` with no `;` before `return` — a syntax error, "expected `;`, found keyword `return`"), so it was fixed as its own change: `ir_to_rust` emitted Collections in statement position tail-shaped; now the Collection arm terminates with `;`, and genuine value positions route through the existing tail renderer — `generate_rust` renders the function body via `ir_to_rust_tail` (the body's value IS the return value; statement-shaped bodies fall through byte-identically, so all previously compiling winners emit identical code, and bare-expression bodies — the `(actual + expected);` shape — now keep their value), and `ir_to_rust`'s If branches and Let bodies, the value positions of those constructs, route via `ir_to_rust_tail` with the return type as expectation, mirroring match arms. Re-measured after the codegen fix (3 fresh runs): **compiles 12/14, 12/14, 13/14; tpr distribution unchanged (36/42 at 1.000); error instances 6× E0308, 0× E0069, 0× E0425, and zero syntax/`;` errors — the slip class is gone** (a `vec![]` inside a loop body now correctly emits `vec![];`, removing the second E0308 those files used to carry). Remaining failures are the two test-less specs in 5 of 6 spec-runs, decomposed above: For-rooted Unknown winners (3 files) and uniformly-mistyped-pool fallback winners (2 files, one of which also exposed a pre-existing expression-emitter gap — an If condition the emitter cannot render comes out as `if /* UNSUPPORTED */`, a parse error unrelated to the `;` family). Next blockers, in order: (1) generation-side type discipline for test-less specs — the pool itself must contain sound hole-free candidates (type-directed root shapes / whole-body generation), since selection gates have nothing to crown in ~half of draws; alternatively give the two specs executable tests so they get a seed and a truth signal like the other twelve. (2) Codegen expression-emitter gaps (carried, newly evidenced): the `/* UNSUPPORTED */` condition emission, and the sibling statement arms that still emit unterminated tail shapes in statement position (`Call`/`Map`/`Filter`/`Fold`/`Sort`) — same latent hazard as the fixed Collection arm, not yet seen in winner files. (3) Real variation operators (carried over, unchanged: 5 of the 6 mutation helpers are literally empty functions, the sixth routes through whole-tree-clone `random_subtree`, and `mutate` is GA-only so it never runs at opt level 1). (4) Reconcile Z3 verification with executed behavior (carried over, same shape: 12/42 winners are Z3-verified and all 12 measure tpr 1.000, but 24 of the 36 tpr-1.000 winners are verified=false). Suite after this branch: 137 passed / 0 failed / 3 ignored.
> **Engine type-directed-generation follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `feat/type-directed-generation`):** blocker (1) above is now FIXED on the generation side. `seed_random` — the only call site that creates fresh candidate bodies (grep-verified: GA mutation transforms existing bodies, MCTS fills holes in existing bodies) — now generates via `random_ir_tree_typed`, which threads the spec's declared return type through generation and reuses the verdict's own certainty rules (`selector::certainly_equal` / `int_family`, widened to `pub(crate)`; no second inference was written). The discipline: the root shape is generated in the return type's context (tail generator: plain typed expression, If/Match whose tails are generated in the same context, Let, Block ending in a typed tail, Return of a typed expression); a statement-only tree is never the entire body of a non-Unit function (loops appear only as statements inside a Block that ends in a typed tail, and statement generation never emits a Return); every Return wraps an expression generated for the return type (Returns come only from the tail generator, whose target is always the return type). Expression positions (operands, conditions, scrutinees, Let values, collection elements) use a separate expression generator, because the tail shapes are not renderable there. The shape menu is deliberately narrower than the verdict's typeable set — verdict-sound is not the same as renderable: no `Call` (codegen emits a call to a nonexistent free function, so the verdict's Int-typed `len(x)` would be E0425), no `Index`/`Head`/`Tail`/`Init` (unsupported in the expression emitter), no `Reverse` (emits `.reverse()`, which returns `()`), no `Concat` (emitted as `String + String`, which Rust rejects), no `Len` unary (emits `.len()` → usize vs the signature's i64); `Div`/`Mod` never take a constant-zero divisor (rustc's `unconditional_panic` lint rejects `x / 0`). For types with no literal menu (Named, Option, Map, …) the only leaf is an explicitly typed Hole — Unknown to the verdict, completable by the Phase 10 typed fillings. The legacy `random_ir_tree` / `random_ir_tree_scoped` are byte-for-byte unchanged for callers without a type context (regression-tested); the Phase 11 gate/verdict, composite weights, tpr semantics, the interpreter, Z3 and codegen are untouched. Measured baseline first (property test over the production seeding path, verdict-checked, 200 draws per M2 signature, written and run before the fix): seed bodies provably mistyped — is_known_type_tag 81/200, node_arity 27/200, type_tag_name 64/200, tags_compatible 69/200, needs_parens 73/200 — **314/1000 (31.4%) mistyped, only 13/1000 provably matching**. After the fix, the same test measures **0/1000 mistyped and 1000/1000 provably matching** (two runs): every shape the typed generator emits for these signatures is certainly typed under the verdict's rules, and typed generation emits no Holes for menufull types — so the sound-pool premise is now structural rather than sampled. Premise rate for the two test-less specs (fraction of synthesis attempts whose returned pool contains a hole-free non-mistyped candidate; ≈0.53–0.7 per attempt in the Phase 11 measurement): **30/30 attempts per spec in two independent batches (60/60)**. Probe (3 runs, same method): **winner tpr distribution unchanged — 36/42 at 1.000, 6/42 at the 0.500 no-tests neutral, 0/42 at 0.000; all 42 winners hole-free, all Mutation provenance; Z3-verified winners 12/42, all measuring tpr 1.000. Compiles: 14/14, 14/14, 14/14 (was 12/14, 12/14, 13/14). Error instances across the 42 winner files: 0× E0308 (was 6×), 0 syntax errors, 0× E0069, 0× E0425 — the first probe measurement with no failing winner file at all.** The two test-less winners are now typed-generation shapes (e.g. `return ((true || false) && (!false));` for tags_compatible; a Let + If/Match over the Int params with Bool arms for needs_parens): type-sound and compiling, but still semantically arbitrary — with no executable tests there is no truth signal for these two specs, and there cannot be one until the spec syntax can bind tuple test inputs, which is a language-design decision and deliberately not made here. Next blockers, in order: (1) Codegen expression-emitter gaps (carried): an unrenderable shape in expression position emits `/* UNSUPPORTED */` (seen in a Phase 11 fallback winner's If condition), and the `Call`/`Map`/`Filter`/`Fold`/`Sort` statement arms still emit unterminated tail shapes in statement position — the typed generator avoids these shapes in fresh bodies, but MCTS completions and GA mutations can still produce them. (2) Real variation operators (carried over, unchanged: 5 of the 6 mutation helpers are literally empty functions, the sixth routes through whole-tree-clone `random_subtree`, and `mutate` is GA-only so it never runs at opt level 1). (3) Reconcile Z3 verification with executed behavior (carried over, same shape: 12/42 winners are Z3-verified and all 12 measure tpr 1.000, but 24 of the 36 tpr-1.000 winners are verified=false). Suite after this branch: 139 passed / 0 failed / 3 ignored.
>
> **Codegen emitter-completeness follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `fix/codegen-emitter-completeness`):** blocker (1) above is FIXED. The audit of `ir_to_rust_expr` found the fallthrough was far broader than the one sighting suggested: **19 of the 28 `IRNode` variants** fell through to `/* UNSUPPORTED */` in expression position — Hole, Block, Let, While, For, Match, Return, Closure, Map, Filter, Fold, Sort, Alloc, Assign, Index, Loop, Break, StructDef, TryPropagate — and two *handled* variants degraded to unparseable operands: `BinOp` Xor emitted `UNKNOWN_OP`, and `UnaryOp` Head/Tail/Init/Last emitted `UNKNOWN_UNOP`. Any of these in an If condition, BinOp operand, Call argument or Let value made the whole generated file unparseable (the Phase 11 `if /* UNSUPPORTED */` winner was one instance, not the class). The expression match is now **exhaustive with no wildcard arm**, so rustc itself guards completeness from here on. Renderings: Hole → `panic!("Hole not filled")` (the codebase's existing Hole convention — type `!`, coerces anywhere, including conditions); Index → `(c)[i]`; TryPropagate → `(e)?`; Closure renders like Lambda; Map/Filter/Fold share their statement-arm expression text; Sort wraps its bind/sort/yield as a block expression; Block renders as a block expression (statements plus a value-keeping tail, mirroring `ir_to_rust_tail`); Let/While/For block-wrap their statement rendering in a neutral `Unit` return-type context (the only loss is the String-ownership rewrite in nested return positions, which the untyped emitter already forgoes elsewhere); Match and Loop render as native expressions; StructDef renders as a block holding the item definition plus its body; Assign → `(t = v)`; Return/Break are parenthesized because they greedily consume a following expression (`(return 5) + 1` must not become `return (5 + 1)`); Xor → `^` (the interpreter's Bool semantics); Head/Last index and Tail/Init slice (the selector verdict's types: element for Head/Last, the list for Tail/Init; the operand text is emitted twice for Last/Init, sound for the IR's pure expression forms); Alloc — which binds a name and has no value of its own — takes the loud `panic!` fallback naming the shape. Block-like expressions (match/if/loop/block/lambda/…) are parenthesized in condition and scrutinee positions across the statement and expression emitters (`if match … { } { }` does not parse); every other position renders byte-identically to before. One adjacent audit find fixed in passing: Set/Map collections in expression position now emit fully-qualified `std::collections::HashSet::from` / `HashMap::from` — no import is generated for winner files, so the bare paths failed E0433 whenever they appeared. Part 2: the `Call`/`Map`/`Filter`/`Fold`/`Sort` statement arms now terminate with `;` (the Collection arm's Phase 11 hazard, latent only because no winner contains those shapes in statement position), and value positions are preserved by routing those shapes — plus Index/Closure/TryPropagate, which in tail position previously fell through to a *discarded* `/* UNSUPPORTED */;` statement — through `ir_to_rust_tail`'s expression list, so bare bodies and match arms keep yielding their values (guard tests pin this; statement-shaped winners emit identical code). Test-first: 12 new tests failed before the fix on exactly these signatures (the 5 tail guards passed, pinning what part 2 must not break), including a rustc integration test over a synthetic body combining the broken shapes in both positions (Call + Fold + Collection statements, a Hole If-condition, an Index return, a bare Map body) that did not parse before and compiles now. Probe (3 runs, same method): **winner tpr distribution unchanged — 36/42 at 1.000, 6/42 at the 0.500 no-tests neutral, 0/42 at 0.000; all 42 winners hole-free, all Mutation provenance; Z3-verified winners 12/42, all measuring tpr 1.000. Compiles: 14/14, 14/14, 14/14. Marker sweep: 0 occurrences of `UNSUPPORTED` and 0 `UNKNOWN_OP`/`UNKNOWN_UNOP` across all 42 winner files; 0 error instances of any class.** Audit finds deliberately NOT fixed here (all parseable today, none reachable in current winners): `expr_to_rust` (the spec-AST emitter used for generated test code and precondition asserts) still falls back to `"??"` for unhandled `Expr` shapes; statement-position Set/Map collections emit `vec![…];`; `Filter`'s `.iter().filter(…).collect()` yields `Vec<&T>` rather than `Vec<T>`; `Reverse` emits `.reverse()`, which returns `()`. Next blockers, in order: (1) real variation operators (carried over, unchanged: 5 of the 6 mutation helpers are literally empty functions, the sixth routes through whole-tree-clone `random_subtree`, and `mutate` is GA-only so it never runs at opt level 1) — with generation, selection and codegen now disciplined, this is the only remaining source of candidate poverty beyond the seed shapes; (2) reconcile Z3 verification with executed behavior (carried over, same shape: 12/42 winners Z3-verified and all tpr 1.000, but 24 of the 36 tpr-1.000 winners are verified=false) — the verifier still encodes only the spec's postconditions, not the candidate body, which is also what the M2 gate's "same validation pipeline as M1" item is missing; (3) the gate-closing step itself: cross-validation of synthesized winners against the `tests/m2_proof.rs` mirrors. Suite after this branch: 157 passed / 0 failed / 3 ignored.

> **Verifier candidate-encoding follow-up (2026-11-20, branch `fix/verifier-candidate-encoding`):** gate item (b) from the "Remaining for the M2 gate" list above is FIXED — the verifier now judges the candidate, not the constraint syntax. `Verifier::verify_one` symbolically evaluates the candidate body once, with the spec's parameters as free Z3 constants, and checks every postcondition with `output` bound to that expression (∀ params: the negation must be UNSAT). The symbolic evaluator mirrors the Phase 7 interpreter's evaluable subset construct by construct (Literal/Var/BinOp/UnaryOp, If, Match over literal/variable/wildcard patterns folded to nested `ite`s, Let, Block, Return, `len` + `List` calls, Collection/Index, Typed) over the sorts Z3 can represent honestly — Int, Bool, String, plus literal lists of those (for `len` / constant `Index`). Floats and Chars are `Opaque` (bindable, discardable, unusable — IEEE floats are not Z3 reals); holes, loops, mutation, lambdas, higher-order combinators, unknown/spec-to-spec calls and guard patterns make a body unencodable. Both dishonest encoder fallbacks are gone: the spec-side encoders are `Option`-valued end to end (no more boolean-subexpression-as-int-0, no more Or-rooted-formula-as-`true`; negative int literals now encode via `from_i64`, not a wrapping `as u64` cast; `Constraint::Or` and `GreaterThanOrEqual` are encoded rather than falling through to `true`).
