> Totals per run, all 3 runs: winners **28/28 Z3-Verified (84/84 across runs** — 78 ConstraintInduced seeds + 6 pool-produced), tpr 1.000 for 27 of 28 specs (the exception is the test-less `rust_pattern_ctor_name` at its neutral 0.500), compiles **84/84**, golden agreement **94/94**, pinned agreement **301/301 with zero pinned disagreements in any run**. Spec-silent behavior of every seeded winner is the guard-table seed's typed-literal wildcard, deterministically (`""` for String tables, `0` for Int classifications) — including on rows slice 2 deliberately leaves to later slices (e.g. `rust_type_prefix` on scalar codes), spec-legal by the representation rule.
>
> **Phase 22 predictions, judged:** (a) **CONFIRMED exactly** — all 18 guard-table/equation slice-2 specs close like slice 1 (seed exists, Verified, wins, full pinned agreement), including the module's second equation spec, `rust_condition_wrap`: total over Int, its equation deciding out-of-range codes false, so the whole −2..=30 band is pinned (33/33) rather than silent past kind 27. (b) **Seedlessness CONFIRMED; the predicted outcome REFUTED, in the good direction** — `rust_literal_bool` and `rust_pattern_ctor_name` receive no constraint seed in any draw (`seeds=[]` in all 6 probe runs) and their winners are pool-produced (Mutation provenance), but both winners are Z3-Verified in every run of both instruments, never unverified or fallback. The mechanism: the builder gap does not extend to the verifier — both specs' constraints are fully encodable (Bool guard rows over a Bool parameter; an Or whose pin is String equality against a parameter), so pool candidates for them can be proven and refuted, and every pool held Verified candidates for selection to crown (verified pool counts — `rust_literal_bool`: 3 of 16, 3 of 26, 3 of 20; `rust_pattern_ctor_name`: 6 of 19, 5 of 16, 4 of 15). (c) **CONFIRMED with one refinement** — partial tables show large spec-silent counts (615 of 916 cases per run) whose winner values are the benign-by-design guard-table wildcard, never disagreements; the refinement is that the silent mass sits on partial tables of both output types (largest: `rust_type_midfix`, 32 of 33 cases silent) and that `rust_pattern_ctor_name`'s silent half is NOT a guard-table wildcard — seedless, its silent-branch behavior is draw-dependent (below).
>
> **The seedless pair, characterized (the evidence base for a builder-generalization phase):** `rust_literal_bool` — the winner is the SAME body in all 3 probe runs: the per-test match `match value { true => "true", false => "false", _ => "" }` carried by a Mutation-provenance candidate — the TestInduced shape, and here the per-test match IS the whole spec, because the two in-spec tests exhaust the finite Bool domain (the wildcard arm is unreachable). Verified, tpr 1.000, winner score 0.985 — outcome-indistinguishable from a constraint seed; executed agreement 2/2 golden and 2/2 pinned in every run. `rust_pattern_ctor_name` — no in-spec tests exist to induce from (tpr is the engine's neutral 0.500 in every draw; winner score 0.560), and each draw crowns a different Mutation body, but every one returns `name` on the pinned `has_subs == false` branch: across the 6 draws of both instruments, the silent `has_subs == true` branch returns `""` in half and the name itself (an identity body) in the other half — some of them carrying dead lets, dead statements, and redundant nested matches — spec-legal variance on a branch the spec deliberately does not state. Executed agreement: 2/2 golden, 7/7 pinned in every run; the 7 silent cases sample exactly those two values. So the refutation gate had real material for both specs — refutable constants and provable identities both arise in their pools — and what the missing seeds actually cost is not correctness but DETERMINISM: a seedless spec's winner is a draw, and its unpinned behavior varies by draw.
>
> **Builder diagnosis (exact predicates; diagnosis only — nothing was fixed this phase):** `guard_table_body` (src/synthesis/engine.rs) admits a spec only when (i) `spec.params.len() == 1` and (ii) `is_int_family(param.ty)`; per row, `guard_row` further requires (iii) every guard leaf to be a comparison of that parameter against an `IntLit` constant (`guard_leaf`), with the pinned value recovered by scanning an integer interval for the unique point falsifying all leaves, and (iv) the output pin to convert via `expr_to_ir` to an `IRNode::Literal` ("the pin must be a literal value"); `equation_rhs` requires (v) a top-level `output == <expr>` postcondition. `rust_literal_bool` fails (ii) — its parameter is Bool — and would fail (iii) (its guard constants are Bool literals) and (v) (its rows are Ors). `rust_pattern_ctor_name` fails (i) — two parameters — and would independently fail (iii) (its guard leaf `has_subs == true` is a Bool equality), (iv) (its pin is `output == name`, a Var, not a literal), and (v) (its constraint is an Or). **Judgment:** slice 2 is closed by measurement — every spec crowns a Verified, pinned-exact winner in every run — and neither seed gap blocks slice 3, whose work is speccing, not seed coverage. The two generalizations that would close the gaps when a future gate wants every winner to be a spec-derived body: a finite-domain guard table (admit a single Bool parameter; rows keyed by Bool constants over {false, true} instead of an integer-interval scan — it would emit exactly the match shape `rust_literal_bool`'s pool winner already takes), and a guarded-identity seed (admit a row whose output pin is a parameter Var when the remaining parameters are pinned by literal-guard rows — here `has_subs == false` → `output == name`, the complementary branch honestly unpinned). Both are builder-only changes; the verifier already judges both shapes today.
>
> **Seed-builder generalizations (2026-11-21, Phase 24, branch `feat/constraint-seeds-2`):** the two generalizations the Phase 23 judgment named are landed — builder-only (`src/synthesis/engine.rs` seed builders + the shared resolver); no verifier, selector, gate, spec, or emitter change. (1) **Finite-domain Bool guard tables:** `guard_table_body` now admits a single Bool parameter alongside the Int family. The row split (exactly one output pin + the remaining guard leaves) is factored out and shared (`guard_row_parts`); Bool guard leaves are Eq/Neq against BoolLit constants (`guard_leaf_bool`), and the pinned value is recovered by enumerating {false, true} for the unique value falsifying every leaf (`pin_bool_value`) instead of the integer-interval scan (whose probe scan is likewise factored out as `pin_int_value`, behavior unchanged). The emitted match keeps the family's shape — one arm per pinned row in spec order plus the trailing typed-literal wildcard: on a total Bool table the wildcard is unreachable and decides no domain value, and it is retained because the verifier's symbolic match requires a final unconditional arm — byte-for-byte the shape `rust_literal_bool`'s Phase 23 pool winner already took. (2) **Guarded-identity seeds** (new builder `guarded_identity_body`, seed kind `guarded_identity`, third fallback in `SpecIndex::callee_body` after equation and guard-table): a spec with exactly two parameters — one guard parameter (Int or Bool family) pinned by literal-guard rows reusing the same row recognition (`guard_identity_row`), and one identity target whose type is the return type, since the pinned arm returns it directly — whose rows pin the output to that target gets one match over the guard parameter returning the target on the pinned arms, with the typed-literal wildcard for every unpinned combination (the guard-table stance: the complementary branch is a documented guess). Conservatism, pinned by seven near-miss tests: every row-shaped postcondition must be an identity row for the SAME target — a row pinning a literal, a compound expression, or another parameter is a deviation and yields no seed at all rather than a body that silently drops the row; more than two parameters (a second parameter would have to be pinned too), two candidate identity targets, and a guard on the identity parameter itself are likewise not admitted. Admission is unchanged (`body_is_encodable_with` in module context). The standing diagnostic `xval3_seed_shapes_recognized_and_encodable` is flipped to assert recognition + encodability for all 28 specs, and a golden fixture (`tests/seed_body_golden.txt`, captured from the builders BEFORE the generalizations) pins the resolver-visible bodies of the 26 previously seeded Codegen specs and all 14 M2 specs byte-identical — no existing body moved. **Measurement (3 runs of both M3 instruments, identical in structure in every run):** ConstraintInduced winners **28/28 per run (84/84 across runs)** — 25 guard_table + 2 equation + 1 guarded_identity per run — all Z3-Verified, compiles **84/84**, golden agreement **94/94 per run**, pinned agreement **301/301 per run** with zero pinned disagreements. `rust_literal_bool`'s winner is now the guard-table seed, with the identical body and score (0.985) its Phase 23 pool winner had — structural instead of drawn. `rust_pattern_ctor_name`'s winner is the identity seed in every run (score 0.560; tpr the test-less neutral 0.500), and its spec-silent branch is now **deterministic**: the wildcard `""` on all 7 silent cases in all 3 runs, where Phase 23 sampled `""` in half the draws and the name itself in the other half. **M2 regression check (instruments re-run once):** probe per-run signature unchanged (12× tpr 1.000 / 2× 0.500, all winners ConstraintInduced + Verified, 0 refuted) and cross-validation golden **64/64** + pinned sweep **539/539** in every run. Suite: **231 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored** (225 + 6 new tests: the two positive seed tests, the partial-Bool-table and Int-guard identity variants, the near-miss set, and the byte-identity regression). Every Codegen spec's winner is now a spec-derived body — the seed-coverage precondition a future M3 gate will want is in place; no gate checkbox is ticked by this phase.
>
> **Slice 3 specs (2026-11-21, Phase 26, branch `feat/m3-slice3-specs`):** 16 specs added to `examples/self_host/codegen.morph` (44 specs, 118 in-spec tests total), one hand mirror per spec plus step/assembly mirrors in `tests/m3_proof.rs` (38 tests, +11), `morphic check` passes, full suite 252 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored. **Representation rule for this slice:** with F1 resolved, templates are pinned as EQUATIONS with `++` over already-rendered parts (the parts arrive pre-rendered, as in slice 2); classifications and fixed texts keep the slice-1/2 forms. The specs — rust_ident: `rust_ident_suffix` (the keyword-suffix step as a composition over a spec call — `rust_keyword(name)==false||output==name++"_"`, `rust_keyword(name)==true||output==name` — with the sanitized string as the input contract) and `rust_ident_empty` (the per-char step's empty fallback, its only pinnable case); the ownership rule: `rust_typed_form` (total dispatch classification over all 28 IRNode kinds: delegate / Literal-check / Typed-re-enter), `rust_string_ownership` (the leaf check over (literal kind, ExpectedTypeCode) — owned iff String literal under a String expectation; callers pass Some(String) only via fn-return Returns, String-typed Allocs, return-type-inheriting tail positions, and String-annotated Typed nodes), `rust_string_literal_text` + `rust_string_literal_owned_text` (the bare and owned templates as `++` equations — slice 2 could only classify the bare template; F1's resolution promotes it to a pinned equation); generate_rust pieces: `rust_doc_line`, `rust_param_text`, `rust_generics_text`, `rust_signature`, `rust_precondition_assert`, `rust_precondition_block`, `rust_test_name_form` (the named/default CHOICE — the default's text is F2), `rust_test_case`, `rust_test_module`, `rust_file_fixed_text` (the two standalone fixed texts). The assembly mirror composes the pieces through generate_rust's own presence branches and golden-tests the whole fixed structure (doc + block + signature + body + module, and the minimal form). **rust_ident decomposition verdict:** of the function's four rules — per-char keep/replace, leading-ASCII-digit prefix, empty fallback, keyword suffix — the last two are spec-pinned and the first two are mirror-only (finding F5, recorded with the F-series above). By input class: every already-valid identifier (the common case — plain spec/IR names) is FULLY spec-pinned, its output decided by the composition (identity or suffix); only names needing char surgery fall to the mirror, whose verbatim copy is cross-checked against the pinned composition (suffix ∘ sanitize == verbatim over the golden name battery, including `"type "` → `"type_"`, where sanitization — not the suffix step — supplies the underscore). **Seed-diagnostic note:** the standing diagnostic in `src/m3_cross_validation.rs` is scoped to the 28 slice-1+2 specs by name (the Phase 22 precedent; its assertions for those specs are unchanged), because several slice-3 shapes are expected seedless and whether the `++` equation templates seed is a measurement question, not an assertion to pre-judge. No synthesis has been run against the slice-3 specs.
>
> **Surprises found while speccing slice 3** (behavior as-is; mirrors pin today's bytes): (13) `generate_rust` emits the precondition block BEFORE the signature (src/codegen/mod.rs:96-104 vs 107-110) — a free-standing `#[cfg(debug_assertions)] { debug_assert!(…) }` block at module item level whose asserts name the function's parameters: not a valid item position and the names are out of scope there, so any candidate whose spec carries preconditions produces a file that cannot compile as placed; the block template and its position in the assembly mirror reproduce it exactly. (14) `rust_ident`'s doc comment claims anything outside `[A-Za-z0-9_]` becomes `_`, but the code keeps `char::is_alphanumeric` — Unicode letters and digits pass through untouched (`"café"` → `"café"`) — and the leading-digit prefix tests `is_ascii_digit` only, so a name starting with a non-ASCII digit (e.g. U+0663) is kept with no prefix at all. The mirrors copy the code, not the comment; goldens pin both cases.
>
> **Slice-3 measurement (2026-11-21, Phase 27, branch `feat/m3-slice3-specs`):** the M3 instruments are generalized to all 44 specs (`src/m3_synthesis_probe.rs` + `src/m3_cross_validation.rs`: String / multi-String (≤4) / Int-pair / Bool+String runner shapes; per-spec pinned sets from the constraints — 2,196 executed cases per run: 1,539 pinned, 650 spec-silent, 7 out of contract; a CAND3 pool dump for the four predicted-seedless specs; a COMPILE_EDGE classification, below). Suite 253 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored. **Per-spec results** (identical structure in all 3 runs of both instruments; "not measurable" = COMPILE_EDGE, the winner's emitted Rust does not compile at the rendering layer — never counted as disagreement): `rust_typed_form` — guard_table seed, Verified, tpr 1.000, pinned 33/33, compiles. `rust_test_name_form` — guard_table seed, Verified, pinned 2/2, compiles. `rust_file_fixed_text` — guard_table seed, Verified, pinned 2/2, compiles. The nine `++` templates — `rust_string_literal_text`, `rust_string_literal_owned_text`, `rust_doc_line`, `rust_param_text`, `rust_signature`, `rust_precondition_assert`, `rust_precondition_block`, `rust_test_case`, `rust_test_module` — ALL take equation seeds that verify and win in every run, at arities 1, 2 and 4 (multi-param seeding is not a gap; `rust_test_case`, the predicted likeliest surprise, seeds cleanly); interpreter tpr 1.000 wherever in-spec tests exist, the neutral 0.500 for the three test-less templates; NONE of the nine winners compiles as emitted (rendering layer, below), so their 42 pinned cases per run are not executable-measurable this phase. The four seedless specs: `rust_ident_empty` — no seed, but the pool produces the correct match shape and the verifier proves it (2 Verified per pool; the winner is identical in shape and score, 0.985, in all 3 runs; tpr 1.000; its execution is blocked only by the E0308 match rendering). `rust_ident_suffix` — no seed, no Verified candidate in any pool (73 candidates across 3 probe runs: 46 refuted, 27 inconclusive/malformed, 0 verified); winners are Inconclusive identity-shaped bodies (probe runs 1 and 3, all xval draws: pinned 12/63 — exact on the 12 non-keyword contract names, wrong on all 51 keywords, identity also on the 7 out-of-contract names, reported uncounted) or constant-`""` (probe run 2, tpr 0.000). `rust_string_ownership` — no seed, 0 Verified of 61 candidates; xval winners by draw: near-constant-false (pinned 1088/1089 — its ONLY disagreement is the domain's single true point (3,3)), non-terminating (10 s timeout, total disagreement by convention), constant-true (1/1089). `rust_generics_text` — no seed, 0 Verified of 55 candidates; winners: identity-on-`joined` (pinned 1/6), non-terminating, identity (1/6).
>
> **Phase 26 predictions, judged.** P1 CONFIRMED, strengthened: no builder fires for `rust_ident_suffix`; NO candidate in ANY watched pool contains a Call at all (0 of 246 candidates across the four seedless specs, all runs) — generation at opt 1 never proposes the composition; the refutations do flow through the Phase 18 inlining (a per-test match is refuted on untested keywords, which is only decidable because the constraint's `rust_keyword` call resolves); winners are never labeled Verified — but the pools hold zero Verified candidates, so selection crowns Inconclusive bodies whose pinned behavior can be 12/63 or 0/6-tested. One instrument divergence recorded: the xval identity winner's interpreter tpr reads 0.000 while its compiled execution agrees on the whole identity class — the interpreter fails a dead construct in the junk body (statements/a loop the emitter renders harmlessly); the probe's cleaner identity winners read the expected 0.500. P2 CONFIRMED in full (above). P3 CONFIRMED in full (above). The seeded tally is exactly the predicted 12/16. P4 REFUTED in its optimistic half, CONFIRMED in its warning: all four constraints are judged (refutations flow: 46/73, 39/61, 42/55, and 30/57 refuted for ident_empty), but Verified pool winners materialize for only one of the four; for the other three there is no determinism to speak of — behavior is a draw lottery spanning near-perfect-by-accident (1088/1089 via constant-false on a one-true-point domain) to non-terminating.
>
> **Builder diagnoses (exact predicates; builder-only, the verifier judges all four shapes today).** `rust_ident_suffix`: `equation_rhs` requires a top-level Eq — its constraints are Ors. `guard_table_body` rejects the String param at its type branch (Int family / Bool only); independently `guard_row` requires the output pin to convert to `IRNode::Literal` (its pins are the Var `name` and the Concat `name ++ "_"`) and `guard_leaf`/`guard_leaf_bool` read only Var-vs-literal comparisons (its guard leaf is a Call). `guarded_identity_body` requires exactly 2 params (it has 1). `rust_ident_empty`: Or-not-Eq; String param rejected at the same type branch, and its String-equality guard leaf is readable by neither literal family. `rust_string_ownership`: `guard_table_body` requires exactly 1 param (it has 2, and its rows' guard leaves span both — `guard_row`'s single-param recognition rejects them independently); `guarded_identity_body` tries both roles and `guard_identity_row` rejects the first row-shaped postcondition — the pins are Bool literals, not the identity Var (a deviation yields no seed); its rows are also region rows (each pins a half-plane, jointly a conjunction), not point rows. `rust_generics_text`: Or-not-Eq; 2 params for `guard_table_body`; for `guarded_identity_body` the `present == true` row pins a literal (`""`) — a deviation under the same-target rule — and the other row's pin is a Concat, not the bare Var.
>
> **The rendering layer (this phase's second headline).** 10 of 44 winners per run do not compile as emitted (probe standalone check, the identical set in all 3 runs; compiles 34/44 per run) — the nine template seeds plus `rust_ident_empty`'s winner — through THREE distinct defects in how the frozen emitter renders correct IR bodies: (i) E0369 Concat-operand ownership (Phase 25): `rust_doc_line`, `rust_signature`, `rust_test_case`, `rust_test_module`. (ii) E0308 string-boundary rendering: `rust_param_text` (`String + String` — the Concat RHS operand is never borrowed) and `rust_ident_empty` (a String-scrutinee match rendered without `.as_str()`, so its patterns are `&str` against `String`). (iii) Parse-level literal rendering: string literals are emitted RAW (contents unescaped), so any literal containing `"` breaks the file's syntax — `"""` for the quote templates (`rust_string_literal_text`, `rust_string_literal_owned_text`) and `", "Precondition violated");"` for the assert templates (`rust_precondition_assert`, and `rust_precondition_block` through its generated test module, which rustc parses despite `#[cfg(test)]`). The harness records these as COMPILE_EDGE (kind-labeled), never as disagreements; the affected specs' IR-level evidence (seed verdicts, interpreter tpr) is complete without execution. Roadmap consequence: byte-level validation of ANY assembly-shaped winner is currently blocked at the pipeline's last mile, and slices 4–6 are made of exactly these constructs (concatenations, quote-bearing literals, string matches) — the rendering layer is a precondition for the byte-identical gate, not an afterthought.
>
> **Module totals per run (×3):** ConstraintInduced winners 40/44 (28 slices 1–2 + 12 slice-3), Verified winners 41/44 (the 40 seeds + `rust_ident_empty`'s pool winner), Inconclusive winners 3/44, compiles 34/44. Cross-validation: slices 1–2 unchanged (golden 94/94, pinned 301/301, zero disagreements); executed slice-3 golden 65/71 · 11/71 · 13/71 and pinned 1138/1195 · 49/1195 · 51/1195 in runs 1–3 (all variance from the three Inconclusive winners); 19 golden + 43 pinned per run not measurable (compile-edge rows). **Next-step judgment (recorded, not decided here):** the smallest builder that seeds `rust_ident_suffix` is a composition-guard generalization — guard leaves that are Eq/Neq comparisons of a CALL to an indexed spec against a literal (the pinned quantity is the call's Bool result, recovered by the same falsify-every-leaf evaluation over {false, true}), output pins admitted as any hole-free output-free expression over the params (literal / Var / Concat), the body an If/Match over the call expression with the typed-literal wildcard for unpinned call values; admission unchanged (`body_is_encodable_with` — the verifier already encodes this shape, as the 46 refutations prove). It also seeds `rust_generics_text` (plain Bool guard + expression pins). `rust_string_ownership`'s region rows over two params are a larger, separate generalization. The recommendation recorded with the Phase 26 judgment stands and is now measured, not conjectured: land the composition-guard builder BEFORE slice-4 spec writing, because slice 4's walker specs compose by construction — without it they land in the ident_suffix regime (refutation works, proving never happens, winners are draw lotteries), and their measurement outcome is already known.
>
> **Composition-guard seeds (2026-11-21, Phase 28, branch `feat/composition-guard-seeds`):** the builder the Phase 27 note specified is landed, builder-only (`composition_guard_body` in src/synthesis/engine.rs, seed kind `composition_guard`). It generalizes the Phase 24 guarded-identity builder along both axes: the guard is ONE expression shared by every row, valued over {false, true} — a Bool parameter, or a call to an indexed spec returning Bool — with guard leaves Eq/Neq against BoolLit constants (`guard_leaf_on_expr`, the `guard_leaf_bool` normalization with the parameter variable replaced by an arbitrary expression) and the pinned value recovered by the same falsify-every-leaf enumeration, so a call guard is pinned without ever executing the call; and the output pin is any hole-free, output-free expression over the parameters calling only indexed specs (literal / Var / Concat / compositions), replacing the literal-only and bare-Var-only pin rules for this family. The body is one Match over the guard expression (a call guard is evaluated once, as the scrutinee), one arm per pinned guard value, the typed-literal wildcard for unpinned values; admission is the equation precedent (calls resolved via `SpecIndex::inline_calls`, then `body_is_encodable_with`). All-rows-or-none deviation semantics, as Phase 24; a territory rule (a variable guard whose pins are all literals or bare variables stays with `guard_table` / `guarded_identity`) keeps the families disjoint — no spec gains a second seed, and the golden fixture (extended 40 → 42 bodies with the two new resolver-visible bodies) proves every prior seed body byte-identical. `SpecIndex::callee_body` gains the composition body as its fourth fallback. Conservatism held, asserted by tests: `rust_string_ownership`'s region rows and `rust_ident_empty`'s String-equality leaves stay unadmitted (the seed-shape diagnostic now asserts both unrecognized, and its recognized set grows to 30), as do pins mentioning `output`, pins or guards calling unindexed specs, mixed guard expressions across rows, and call guards whose callee does not return Bool. **Per-spec before/after** (identical structure in all 3 runs of both instruments): `rust_ident_suffix` — no seed, 0 Verified of 73 candidates, Inconclusive winners at pinned 12/63 → composition-guard seed, Verified in every pool, winner in every draw, tpr 1.000, executed golden 6/6 and pinned **63/63** in every run, compiles as emitted (the 7 out-of-contract names pass through as identity, reported uncounted — they are non-keywords, so identity is the seed's own behavior there). `rust_generics_text` — no seed, 0 Verified of 55, winners at pinned 1/6 → composition-guard seed, Verified, winner in every draw; its executed rows move to COMPILE_EDGE (E0369 concat-ownership: the seed's literal-first `"<" ++ joined` hits the frozen emitter's Phase 25 rendering defect, the class of nine template winners), so its pinned domain is evidenced at IR level — seed verdict plus interpreter execution over the pinned cases in unit tests — until the rendering layer is addressed. **Module totals per run (×3):** ConstraintInduced winners **42/44** (ownership + ident_empty seedless by design), Verified winners **43/44** (the 42 seeds + `rust_ident_empty`'s deterministic pool winner; Phase 27: 41/44), Inconclusive winners 1/44 — `rust_string_ownership`'s lottery, unchanged (executed pinned 1/1089 · 1/1089 · 34/1089 across the runs; every other executed spec agrees fully). Cross-validation: slices 1–2 unchanged (golden 94/94, pinned 301/301, zero disagreements); executed slice-3 golden 15/68 · 15/68 · 24/68 and pinned 101/1189 · 101/1189 · 134/1189, all variance ownership's draw; not-measurable rows 22 golden + 49 pinned per run across 11 COMPILE_EDGE specs (Phase 27: 19 + 43 across 10 — the delta is exactly generics_text's 3 + 6). Compiles (probe) 33 · 32 · 33 of 44 (the systematic 11 rendering-edge winners; run 2's extra failure is ownership's E0689 junk draw). M2 instruments, one pass: probe 14/14 ConstraintInduced + Verified, cross-validation golden 64/64 and pinned sweep 539/539 in all runs — no regression. Suite 256 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored. **Consequence for slice 4:** the walker specs compose by construction — their guards are calls over rendered children and their pins are template expressions, exactly this builder's shapes — so they enter measurement with a seeding path instead of the ident_suffix regime. The remaining measured blocker for the byte-identical gate is now the rendering layer (11/44 winners unmeasurable as emitted), not seed coverage: 42/44 specs seed, and the two that do not are recorded non-goals.


> **Phase 29 re-pin inventory (2026-11-21, branch `fix/codegen-rendering`) — taken BEFORE any behavior change.** The user (project owner) approved unfreezing the Rust emitter for exactly the three rendering defects measured in Phases 25/27/28 — (D1) E0369 Concat-operand ownership, (D2) E0308 string-boundary rendering (Concat RHS never borrowed; String-scrutinee match without `.as_str()`), (D3) parse-level unescaped string literals — and nothing else. Every expectation in the repo that pins one of the defective renderings is listed below, per item: current pinned behavior → defect → what the fixed behavior must be. Everything NOT listed must remain byte-identical after the fix; that is the freeze's surviving scope. Defect sites in `src/codegen/mod.rs`: D1+D2a — the `IRBinOp::Concat` arm of `ir_to_rust_expr` (renders `({lhs} + {rhs})` with both operands bare); D2b — the two Match arms (`ir_to_rust` statement position, `ir_to_rust_expr` expression position), scrutinee via `expr_in_condition_position`, patterns via `ir_pattern_to_rust`; D3 — five raw-literal arms: `ir_to_rust_expr`'s String literal (line 609), `ir_to_rust_expr_typed`'s owned String literal (line 553), `ir_pattern_to_rust`'s String literal (line 820), `expr_to_rust`'s `Expr::StringLit` (line 1181 — the spec-Expr walker behind `generate_rust`'s test module and precondition asserts; the measured precondition_block parse failure flows through this site, not an IR literal), and — same defect class at an adjacent arm, included with justification — `ir_to_rust_expr`'s Char literal (line 610: a `'\''` / `'\\'` / control char renders raw and breaks parse identically; no expectation anywhere pins its special-char bytes, and escaping is the identity on every other char, so no existing byte moves).
>
> **(i) `src/codegen/mod.rs` unit tests — audited all; ZERO re-pins.** `rust_codegen_string_literals_owned_in_string_returning_match`, `rust_codegen_string_literal_owned_in_return`, `rust_codegen_string_literals_stay_str_without_string_expectation` pin the owned/bare literal texts for plain-word contents only (`Int`, `done`, …) — D3's escaping is the identity there, so their bytes do not move. `rust_expr_index_try_closure_match_render` (Match over a Var, Wildcard pattern only) and `rust_expr_block_like_conditions_and_scrutinees_are_parenthesized` (Int patterns) never trigger the D2b rule. `rust_expr_every_ir_variant_renders_without_markers` contains no Concat node, no String literal, no String pattern. No unit test pins Concat assembly text, a String-pattern match text, or a special-char literal text. The fixes therefore ADD tests (Step 2) instead of changing existing ones.
>
> **(ii) `src/synthesis/engine.rs` concat e2e — 2 re-pins (D1).** (E-1) The `concat_equation_seed_is_verified_and_wins` doc comment states the frozen rendering `(("Vec<" + inner) + ">")` is asserted byte-for-byte and that `exclaim` carries the compile leg → rewritten: wrap_vec's rendering becomes `(("Vec<".to_string() + &inner) + ">")` and wrap_vec gains its own rustc compile leg. (E-2) The byte assertion `code.contains(r#"(("Vec<" + inner) + ">")"#)` → the fixed rendering above. `exclaim`'s `(inner + "!")` is variable-first with a literal RHS, which stays bare under the fix — bytes unchanged, no re-pin.
>
> **(iii) `examples/self_host/codegen.morph` specs — 5 re-pins.** (S-1, D3) `rust_string_literal_text`'s doc pins "a quote, the contents RAW (no escaping of any kind … breaks the generated Rust; pinned as-is), a quote" → rewritten: the rendering is a quote + ESCAPE(contents) + a quote, where ESCAPE is the Rust per-character escaping (`\"`, `\\`, `\n`, `\r`, `\t`, `\u{…}` for other control chars); escaping is a per-character transformation (F5 family), so the escape rule itself is mirror-pinned (category iv) and the spec's equation pins the wrap — exact exactly on the identity subdomain (contents with no `"`, `\`, or control characters), silent elsewhere. (S-2, D3) the same spec's in-spec test `raw-quote` (`a"b` → `"a"b"`) pins the defect → replaced by an identity-subdomain test (`Vec<i64>`). The constraint equation itself does not change (it IS the wrap). (S-3, D3) `rust_string_literal_owned_text`'s doc describes the bare template + `.to_string()` → amended to reference the escaped base template; its constraint and its two tests (plain/empty — identity subdomain) do not change. (S-4, D3, pattern site) `rust_pattern_literal_form`'s doc calls family 2 a "quoted-raw template (String: a quote, the contents with no escaping, a quote)" → wording becomes "quoted template (a quote, the escaped contents, a quote)"; the classification constraint does not change. (S-5, D1, doc only) `rust_typed_form`'s doc names Concat operands as the standing example of literals that "stay bare even where a String is expected" → amended: the dispatch classification this spec pins is unchanged (the typed emitter still delegates every non-Literal/Typed kind), but Concat operands now receive ownership treatment inside the untyped emitter's Concat arm, so the example is dropped and the true remainder kept (literals nested in non-Concat composites stay bare). Checked, NO re-pin: `rust_string_ownership` (pins WHEN the owned form fires — the fixes do not move that decision), `rust_test_case`'s doc (its E0308 note is the generated-test PLACEMENT property — `expr_to_rust` inputs placed bare in call position against String parameters — a different property from D1–D3, deliberately not fixed), and the other 39 specs (their pinned texts are spec-level assemblies, not emitter renderings).
>
> **(iv) `tests/m3_proof.rs` mirrors + golden — 3 re-pins (all D3).** (P-1) The `rust_string_literal_text` mirror (quote + RAW contents + quote, doc comment "no escaping") → quote + escape(contents) + quote, with a hand-written escape helper in the proof file mirroring the emitter's rule — this is where the escaping rule is pinned. (P-2) The `rust_string_literal_owned_text` mirror (formats the raw template + `.to_string()` independently) → composes the fixed bare mirror + `.to_string()`, exactly as the emitter's typed arm composes the literal arm. (P-3) The `string_literal_templates` golden test asserts the RAW outputs for `a"b` and `a\b` → escaped expectations (`"a\"b"`, `"a\\b"` as rendered text), plus one control-char case (newline) pinning the escape rule; the owned == bare + `.to_string()` composition loop stays and now ranges over escaped text. Checked, NO re-pin: every other mirror (spec-level templates/classifications; none renders an IR literal or a Concat assembly).
>

(H-2, D3) The `STRING_CONTENTS` battery comment ("the contents render RAW, unescaped", "total: every case pinned") → the battery itself is unchanged (9 cases), but the three special cases (`a"b`, `a\b`, `line\nbreak`) become spec-silent for the two literal specs (the spec equation is exact only on the identity subdomain, per S-1); comment rewritten to say so. > **Explicitly out of scope (the freeze's surviving scope — none of these move):** the generated-test placement E0308 (`expr_to_rust` inputs placed bare against String parameters — ROAD Phase 21 note; driver-slice / language-owner territory); operand parenthesization for block-like BinOp operands (pre-existing for ALL operators, not a Concat defect); List-sort Concat compilability (`Vec + Vec` has no Rust `Add` impl before or after — the D1/D2a treatment is the String sort's, which is the measured defect; no expectation pins List-concat text); the other targets' literal rendering (Python `ir_to_python_expr` and JS `ir_to_js_expr` keep their raw forms — their languages' escaping rules are their own); `debug_ir_string` (debug text, not Rust output); and every rendering not reachable from D1–D3.


> **Rendering layer fixed (2026-11-21, Phase 29, branch `fix/codegen-rendering`) — the emitter freeze is amended: the emitter is frozen EXCEPT the three fixes below, which the user approved on 2026-11-21, scoped to exactly the defects measured in Phases 25/27/28.** Work order was inventory-first (the re-pin inventory above, committed before any behavior change), then one fix commit per defect with failing tests first, then the re-pins as their own commit, then re-measurement. **The fixes** (all in `src/codegen/mod.rs`, Rust-target paths only): (D1) the Concat arm of `ir_to_rust_expr` no longer renders both operands bare — the left operand goes through `concat_owned_operand` (a string literal in left position renders `"...".to_string()`, the same rewrite `ir_to_rust_expr_typed` applies in expectation positions; every other left shape already evaluates to a String and renders unchanged), so literal-first assemblies render `(("Vec<".to_string() + &inner) + ">")` instead of the E0369 `(("Vec<" + inner) + ">")`. (D2a) the right operand goes through `concat_borrowed_operand` — borrowed (`String + &String`); a string literal stays bare (already a `&str`; borrowing it would give `&&str`, which has no `Add` impl); a nested Concat is borrowed as a unit; a block-like operand is parenthesized first (via `is_block_like_expr`, the set extracted from `expr_in_condition_position`, whose behavior is unchanged). (D2b) both Match arms render the scrutinee through `match_scrutinee_to_rust`, which appends `.as_str()` when any case pattern is or contains a string literal (Constructor and Guard patterns included) — with one syntactic exception, forced by measurement: a scrutinee that is itself a string literal stays bare, because it is already a `&str` and `&str::as_str()` is unstable on this rustc (E0658, verified empirically before fixing). The rule is syntactic (the emitter carries no types); its recorded blind spot is a scrutinee variable let-bound to a bare literal, the same limitation class as the expectation-driven ownership treatment. (D3) a new `escape_rust_str` — `"` and `\` backslash-escaped, `\n`/`\r`/`\t` short forms, `\u{...}` for other control characters, everything else (including non-ASCII text) byte-identical — now feeds all five Rust string-literal sites: `ir_to_rust_expr`'s String arm, `ir_to_rust_expr_typed`'s owned arm, `concat_owned_operand`, `ir_pattern_to_rust`'s String arm, and `expr_to_rust`'s StringLit (the spec-Expr walker behind generated test modules and precondition asserts — the site the precondition_block parse failure flowed through); the adjacent Char arm takes `escape_rust_char` (same defect class; inventoried with justification). Other targets' literal rendering (Python, JS) is untouched. **Re-pins applied** (the inventory's items; each traceable to a defect): E-1/E-2 — the concat e2e's doc + byte assertion, wrap_vec gains its compile leg, exclaim's `(inner + "!")` unchanged; S-1/S-2 — `rust_string_literal_text`'s doc rewritten (the rendering is quote + ESCAPE(contents) + quote; the escape rule is per-character — F5 — and mirror-pinned; the spec's equation pins the wrap and is exact exactly on the identity subdomain, contents with no quote/backslash/control character) and its `raw-quote` test replaced by an identity-subdomain test; S-3 — the owned template's doc; S-4 — `rust_pattern_literal_form`'s doc wording (classification unchanged); S-5 — `rust_typed_form`'s doc (Concat dropped as the standing bare-literal example; the dispatch classification unchanged); P-1/P-2/P-3 — the proof mirrors escape via a hand-written mirror of the rule (this is where the rule is pinned), the owned mirror composes the bare one, the golden asserts the escaped outputs plus a newline case; H-1..H-6 — the harness mirrors escape and return spec-silent off the identity subdomain, the battery is unchanged but its three special cases are silent for both literal specs, the golden follows the amended spec tests, PINNED_TOTALS drop 9 → 6 for both literal specs (module: 1,539 → 1,533 pinned, 650 → 656 spec-silent, 2,196 cases unchanged), and the disagreement mechanics test is re-pinned to the new classification; H-7 — the harness/probe prose describing the edges as current fact moved to past tense, the COMPILE_EDGE classifier retained unchanged as a guard. **Inventory extensions found during the re-pin step** (same defect, wording-only, constraints unchanged; recorded here per the stop-and-extend rule): S-6 — `rust_literal_form`'s doc (family 3 "quoted-raw template … with NO escaping", family 4's char text) amended to the escaped wording; S-7 — `rust_expr_form`'s doc (family 3 "quoted-raw template (StringLit)" and its "bare quoted rendering is pinned as-is" sentence) amended likewise, its E0308 placement caveat preserved verbatim in substance. Two further notes: the inventory's category (i) held exactly — no pre-existing codegen unit test needed a re-pin (the owned-literal tests' contents are plain words, where escaping is the identity); and one incidental harness fix rode along in the D3 commit — `compile_rust_lib`'s temp dir was pid-only, so parallel test threads stomped on each other's files (surfaced by the added compile legs as a spurious archive error in an unrelated test); the dir is now unique per call and no expectation changed by it. **Re-measurement** (M3 probe + cross-validation, 3 runs each): COMPILE_EDGE specs per run **11 → 0**; not-measurable rows per run **22 golden + 49 pinned → 0**; probe compiles **33·32·33 → 44·44·44 of 44**; executed agreement is total outside `rust_string_ownership`'s draw — golden 130/130 and pinned 444/444 for the other 43 specs in every run (ownership: 0/54 golden + 0/1089 pinned on two non-terminating draws, 1/54 + 1/1089 on the third — its Phase 27/28 lottery, unchanged in character; it is a pool artifact, not rendering); `rust_generics_text` executes for the first time (golden 3/3, pinned 6/6) and `rust_ident_empty` executes (1/1, Verified winner, its E0308 gone); module winners unchanged (ConstraintInduced 42/44, Verified 43/44, Inconclusive 1/44 = ownership). M2 instruments, one pass: probe 14/14 ConstraintInduced + Verified, cross-validation golden 64/64 + pinned sweep 539/539 in all runs — no regression, and the M2 winners' plain-word literals prove the escaping fix moved no innocent output. Full suite **263 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored** (256 baseline + 7 new rendering tests, each rendering a previously failing shape and asserting the emitted Rust compiles AND behaves when executed). **Deliberately NOT fixed** (the freeze's surviving scope): the generated-test placement E0308 (`expr_to_rust` inputs placed bare against String parameters — driver-slice / language-owner territory); operand parenthesization for block-like BinOp operands (pre-existing for all operators); List-sort Concat compilability (`Vec + Vec` has no Rust `Add` impl before or after — the D1/D2a treatment is the String sort's, which is the measured defect); the other targets' raw literal rendering; and every rendering not reachable from D1–D3. **Consequence for slice 4:** the walker/driver slices' constructs — concatenations, quote-bearing literals, string matches — now render measurably; the byte-identical gate's last measured blocker is removed, and the emitter is frozen again outside the three fixes above.

> **Slice 4 specs (2026-11-21, Phase 30, branch `feat/m3-slice4-specs`):** 35 specs added to `examples/self_host/codegen.morph` (79 specs, 131 in-spec tests total), one hand mirror per spec plus the assembly mirror `ir_expr_assembled` in `tests/m3_proof.rs` (52 tests, +14), `morphic check` passes, full suite 277 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored. **Scoping decision: the FULL walker in one phase** — all four sub-slices, every arm of `ir_to_rust_expr` (src/codegen/mod.rs:742-971) — because the arms share one representation and the sub-slice boundaries fell out of the emitter's own order; no 4a/4b split was needed. Three arms need no new spec: Literal (slices 1–3's literal family), Var (slice 3's `rust_ident` composition), Typed (slice 3's `rust_typed_form` dispatch). **Representation rule (slices 2/3's, extended):** children arrive PRE-RENDERED; each spec pins its arm's template as a `++` equation over those parts, its classifications as total guard tables, and its fixed texts completely; where the walker consults a slice 1–3 classification, the spec composes it BY CALL — `rust_binop_infix_text`'s equation contains `rust_binop_symbol(op)` inside the concat chain (a call inside `++`: parses and typechecks, verified by `morphic check` this phase), and `rust_condition_text`'s rows guard on `rust_condition_wrap(kind)`. The specs — operators: `rust_expr_binop_form` (total branch classification: general / Concat / Append / Merge — the branch precedes the table, whose `.push`/`.extend` values stay dead), `rust_binop_infix_text`, `rust_binop_append_text`, `rust_binop_merge_text`, `rust_binop_concat_text` (over the Phase 29 operand treatments: `rust_concat_left_owned` — the (Literal, String) leaf check — `rust_concat_left_text`, `rust_concat_right_form` — total over 28 kinds × 6 literal kinds: bare / parenthesized borrow for the block-like set / plain borrow — `rust_concat_right_text`), `rust_unary_text` (all ten renderings as guard rows with expression pins, incl. Init/Last emitting the operand text twice and Reverse's `()`-returning call, pinned as-is); collections + iterators: `rust_collection_prefix` / `rust_collection_suffix` (expression position; Set/Map fully qualified — the statement-position collapse is slice 5's), `rust_map_text`, `rust_filter_text`, `rust_fold_text`, `rust_sort_text` (the block-expression form); control forms: `rust_call_text`, `rust_condition_text`, `rust_if_text`, `rust_lambda_text` (Lambda and Closure share one arm; the Closure's captured environment is not rendered and parameter types are dropped — pinned as-is), `rust_match_arm_text`, `rust_match_text`, the scrutinee trio `rust_match_scrutinee_bare` / `rust_match_scrutinee_form` / `rust_match_scrutinee_text` (the Phase 29 `.as_str()` rule as decision + rendering), `rust_block_text` (one wrap serving both the Block arm and `expr_block_stmt`, hence Let/While/For), `rust_loop_text`, `rust_struct_def_text`; leaves/misc: `rust_index_text`, `rust_try_text`, `rust_assign_text`, `rust_return_text`, `rust_break_text`, `rust_hole_text`, `rust_unsupported_text` (in this walker exactly one arm uses the fallback: Alloc). The assembly mirror performs the recursion over an `Expr` tree, composing the per-arm mirrors exactly as the walker composes the helpers, and golden-tests two-level nestings (BinOp in Call in BinOp; Match in a Match arm; Map over Filter), the parenthesization edges (a Match condition wraps, a BinOp condition does not re-wrap, `(return 5) + 1` stays outside the return), the Concat treatments (literal-left ownership, block-like-right parenthesized borrow, nested Concat borrowed as a unit), the scrutinee rule (string pattern nested in a Constructor still triggers `.as_str()`; a bare-literal scrutinee does not take it), and both loud fallbacks.
