> **Slice-4 representation gaps (G-series, recorded like the F-series):** **G1** — `pattern_has_string_literal` (src/codegen/mod.rs:697-706) is a recursion over the pattern TREE (through Constructor and Guard); patterns arrive pre-rendered in this representation, so `rust_match_scrutinee_form` takes `has_string_pat` as a precomputed Bool input and the recursion is mirror-only (the F5 family: tree recursion the flat inputs cannot carry). **G2** — `is_bare_string_literal` recurses through Typed wrappers; the flat (kind, lit) codes do not carry the inner node, so `rust_match_scrutinee_bare` deliberately leaves kind 22 (Typed) unpinned and the mirror decides it by recursion. **Not a gap, stated precisely:** this walker is expectation-free by construction — `ir_to_rust_expr` renders children untyped, so no expectation propagation exists to lose; the single expectation leak is the Typed arm delegating to `ir_to_rust_expr_typed` (slice 3 owns it), and the Block/Loop/Let arms' neutral Unit statement context loses only the String-ownership rewrite in nested return/tail positions, which belongs to the statement walker (slice 5). The `", "` joins (call arguments, collection elements, lambda parameters, match arms, struct fields) are iteration — mirror-only, as in slices 2/3. **Diagnostic scoping:** the seed-shape diagnostic's count moves 44 → 79 with its recognized set unchanged (30 names) and the sweep harness (ALL_SPECS / PINNED_TOTALS) stays scoped to slices 1–3 — which slice-4 shapes the builders recognize is the next phase's measurement question, not an assertion to pre-judge (the Phase 26 precedent). **Measurement expectations (recorded, not decided):** the pure `++` template equations should seed through the equation builder (the Phase 27 precedent: all nine slice-3 `++` templates seeded at arities 1, 2 and 4) and the Int guard tables with literal pins through the guard-table builder (slices 1–2 precedent); `rust_condition_text` is the composition-guard family's call-guarded shape exactly (Phase 28's `rust_ident_suffix` form: a call to an indexed Bool spec, pinned by falsify-every-leaf), and `rust_concat_left_text` / `rust_break_text` are its Bool-param-guarded shapes. The sharpest open shape is the **Int-keyed expression pins** — `rust_unary_text` (ten rows keyed by the Int op, pins are Concat expressions), `rust_concat_right_text` and `rust_match_scrutinee_text` (rows keyed by an Int form): no current builder obviously admits them — the guard-table builder requires literal pins, and the composition-guard builder's guard is ONE Bool-valued expression, not a per-row Int comparison — so these three are the measurement's test case for a possible fifth builder family (a guard table whose pins are expressions). The predicted lottery risks are the two-parameter leaf decisions whose rows span both parameters (`rust_concat_left_owned` over Int×Int codes, `rust_match_scrutinee_form` over Bool×Bool — `rust_string_ownership`'s region-row family) and the by-design partial `rust_match_scrutinee_bare` (kind 22 unpinned, G2). No synthesis has been run against the slice-4 specs.

> **Slice 4 measured (2026-11-21, Phase 31, branch `feat/m3-slice4-measurement`):** the cross-validation harness now covers all 79 specs — slice-4 mirrors copied from `tests/m3_proof.rs` (the one place the two mirror sets' stated domains differ: `rust_concat_right_form`'s catch-all row and `rust_match_scrutinee_bare`'s false rows decide codes outside the reachable kinds 0..=27, where the proof mirrors abstain; on every reachable input they agree exactly), new input shapes and runner arms for the multi-parameter templates, sweeps of 3,548 cases per run (2,761 pinned, 780 spec-silent, 7 out of contract) — the probe's CAND3 watch list covers the eight open shapes, and both instruments ran 3× with M2 one pass. **Module totals per run:** ConstraintInduced winners **69/79** (equation 32, guard_table 32, composition_guard 4, guarded_identity 1 — every seeded spec's seed wins in every run), Verified winners **71·70·70/79** (the 69 seeds + `rust_ident_empty`'s pool winner in every run, + `rust_match_scrutinee_form`'s one Verified pool candidate in run 1), Inconclusive winners 8·9·9/79, probe compiles **78/79 per run** — the one failure each run is the recorded E0689 `(- 0).abs()` junk-draw artifact rotating among the seedless String specs (execution-hazard backlog, not a rendering regression). Slices 1–3 preserved exactly: golden 130/130 + pinned 444/444 for the 43 non-ownership specs in every run, zero COMPILE_EDGE among them; `rust_string_ownership`'s lottery unchanged in character (32/1089 · 1023/1089 · one timeout). M2 one pass: probe 14/14 Verified ConstraintInduced winners, xval golden 64/64 + sweep 539/539 in all runs. **The Phase 30 predictions, scored.** (a) **Confirmed in full:** all 21 pure `++` template equations seed via the equation builder and win Verified in every run — including `rust_binop_infix_text`, whose equation composes a `rust_binop_symbol` call inside the chain (the admitted seed is the inlined guard-table match inside the concat tree; executed golden 4/4, pinned 5/5 — pinned exactly where the symbol table is, silent at ops 17/18/off-band as specced). (b) **Confirmed:** the four single-Int guard tables with literal pins seed via guard_table and agree completely — `rust_expr_binop_form` 19/19, `rust_collection_prefix` / `rust_collection_suffix` 5/5, `rust_hole_text` 1/1 pinned, every run. (c) **Confirmed for two of three:** `rust_condition_text` (the call guard) and `rust_break_text` (Bool guard, one compound pin) seed via composition_guard and agree completely (36/36, 4/4). `rust_concat_left_text` does **not** seed, refuting the third member on a precise predicate: its pins are bare variables under a plain Bool-parameter guard, so `composition_guard_body`'s territory rule refuses it — while `guard_table_body` (arity one) and `guarded_identity_body` (arity two) cannot take its three parameters either. The hole is BETWEEN the territory rule and the earlier families' arity caps, not inside any one builder. (d) **Confirmed seedless — the Int-keyed expression pins:** `rust_unary_text`, `rust_concat_right_text`, `rust_match_scrutinee_text` hold **zero Verified candidates in any pool in any run** (pools of 14–20, the rest refuted or unjudged — generation never proposes a Match over the Int key with expression arms, and the verifier can only refute what it is shown). Executed pinned: unary_text 0/10 · 0/10 · timeout; concat_right_text timeout · timeout · 1/4; scrutinee_text 0/3 in all runs. The rejecting predicates, per family: `guard_row` requires the pin to convert to `IRNode::Literal` (and `guard_table_body` admits exactly one parameter — these specs have two); `guarded_identity` requires the pin to be exactly the other parameter and treats the first compound row as a deviation; `composition_guard_row` requires every guard leaf to be an Eq/Neq against a BoolLit over ONE shared expression (`guard_leaf_on_expr`) and `composition_scrutinee` admits only a Bool parameter or a Bool-returning call — the Int domain is deliberately left to the guard-table family's interval machinery, which is exactly the half that refuses expression pins. (e) **The predicted lotteries, measured:** `rust_concat_left_owned` (363 pinned): timeout · 1/363 · 362/363 — the run-3 winner is the constant-false near-miss, wrong only at (9, 3); `rust_concat_right_form` (363): 2/363 · 32/363 · timeout; `rust_match_scrutinee_bare`'s **pinned half** (352 of 363; the kind-22 column is spec-silent by design, G2): 53/352 · 351/352 · 351/352 — near-constant-false winners missing only (9, 3) in two runs; `rust_match_scrutinee_form` (Bool×Bool, 4 pinned): each instrument's pools hold a Verified candidate in one run of three (the truth table is a small If body generation occasionally proposes and Z3 proves over the finite domain) — xval executed 3/4 · COMPILE_EDGE · 4/4, the middle row a junk Mutation winner `(has_string_pat + bare)` whose E0369 is Bool+Bool arithmetic in the winner BODY, not the Phase 29 Concat family (the body has no Concat node; the retained classifier keys on the error code and books it COMPILE_EDGE — a pool artifact, and the phase's one new COMPILE_EDGE row). `rust_concat_left_text`'s lottery likewise pays out once (xval run 3: Verified Mutation winner, 4/4) — small-body shapes pay out occasionally; region shapes effectively never do. Six timeout FAIL rows across the three xval runs (five slice-4 seedless specs + ownership once): non-terminating Mutation winners, contained by the 10-second deadline and booked as total disagreement per the standing rule. **Seed-shape diagnostic flipped to the measurement** (the Phase 24 pattern): RECOGNIZED 30 → 57; the eight unseeded slice-4 shapes are asserted unrecognized alongside the two standing non-goals, each on its measured predicate. Suite: **277 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored** (unchanged count — the harness mechanics were extended in place). **Next-step judgment (recorded, not decided here) — the fifth builder family, specified by (d)'s predicates:** an **expression-pin guard table**. Admission: the spec's rows are recognized exactly as the guard family's rows are (`guard_row_parts`: an Or of guard leaves plus one output pin), with the key recovered by the family's own machinery — one Int parameter's leaves parsed by `guard_leaf` and the pinned value recovered by `pin_int_value`'s falsify-every-leaf scan — while the pin is admitted the way `composition_pin` admits pins (any hole-free, output-free expression over the parameters, calling only indexed specs), replacing `guard_row`'s literal-only pin test. Body: one Match over the key parameter, one arm per pinned value returning the row's pin expression, the typed-literal wildcard off-table (the guard-table stance); admission unchanged (`SpecIndex::inline_calls`, then `body_is_encodable_with`); all-rows-or-none deviation semantics, as the other families. Territory: guard_table keeps specs whose pins all convert to literals (the pin test is what keeps the families disjoint — no spec gains a second seed), guarded_identity keeps bare-variable pins at arity two, composition_guard keeps the Bool domain (variable or call guards). The family seeds exactly the three specs of (d) — arity two, an Int key plus one text parameter whose text the pins interpolate. **Sibling shapes the same measurement exposes, in the order the evidence ranks them:** (i) **pair-keyed guard tables with literal pins** — `rust_concat_left_owned`, `rust_concat_right_form`, `rust_match_scrutinee_bare`: rows whose leaves constrain TWO Int parameters; the generalization is `guard_table_body`'s arity cap lifted with the pinned CELL recovered by falsify-every-leaf over the pair (the interval scan becomes a two-axis scan; rows pinning a whole row/column — scrutinee_bare's kind-only and lit-only rows — pin their cells by the same joint falsification, and its kind-22 column stays unpinned because no row decides it); literal pins only, so it stays disjoint from the fifth family by the same pin test. (ii) **Multi-guard truth tables** — `rust_match_scrutinee_form`: composition_guard with SEVERAL shared Bool guard expressions (here two Bool parameters), the pinned assignment recovered by falsify-every-leaf over the joint {false, true}ⁿ enumeration (n = 2 measured; the body is nested Matches or an If chain over the guard tuple). (iii) `rust_concat_left_text`'s territory hole — the cheapest single-spec fix on the board (composition_guard admitting a plain Bool-variable guard with bare-variable pins when no earlier family admits the spec's arity), recorded with its cost: it bends the disjointness rule, so it wants the same all-rows-or-none care and a re-audit of the 40-body golden fixture. `rust_string_ownership`'s two-parameter region rows remain the larger, separate generalization they were recorded as in Phase 27. Slice 5 (the statement walker) is unblocked by none of this — its specs will land in the same measured landscape, and the fifth family is the recorded prerequisite for walker-shaped specs whose rows key on Int codes with text pins.

> **Expression-pin guard table landed (2026-11-21, Phase 32, branch `feat/expr-pin-guard-seeds`):** the fifth builder family specified by Phase 31 is implemented in `src/synthesis/engine.rs` (`expr_pin_guard_body` / `expr_pin_guard_row` / `expr_pin_guard_seed_body`, seed kind `expr_pin_guard`). **Admission:** rows are recognized exactly as the guard family's rows are (`guard_row_parts`: an Or of guard leaves plus one output pin); the key is the one Int-family parameter nominated by the first row-shaped postcondition — the parameter under which all of its leaves parse via `guard_leaf` and pin a single value via `pin_int_value`'s falsify-every-leaf scan (a row's leaves name their parameter, so at most one parameter can qualify; zero — a row guarded on two keys, on a Bool or String parameter, or spanning two keys — is not this family, and two would be ambiguous: both yield no seed). Every later row-shaped postcondition must then be a row under THAT key, or the spec states a row this body would not honor — a deviation, so no seed at all (all rows or none, the guarded-identity rule); non-row postconditions are skipped as in the guard-table builder. The pin is admitted the way `composition_pin` admits pins — any hole-free, output-free expression over the parameters calling only indexed specs — replacing `guard_row`'s literal-only pin test for this family. **Body:** one Match over the key parameter, one arm per pinned value returning the row's pin expression (rows pinning an already-pinned value are skipped, first row wins), the typed-literal wildcard off-table (the guard-table stance); admission unchanged (`SpecIndex::inline_calls` → `body_is_encodable_with`), and the builder is the resolver's last fallback in `SpecIndex::callee_body`. **Territory — the mixed-pin-set decision:** at least one row's pin must be a COMPOUND expression (not a literal, not a bare variable — the composition builder's own pin-side test). Mixed pin sets are admitted WHOLESALE: a literal or bare-Var pin beside a compound one is just a pin, and both measured mixed specs (`rust_concat_right_text`, `rust_match_scrutinee_text`) carry a bare-Var row beside their Concat rows, so requiring every pin to be compound would refuse the measured shapes themselves. All-literal sets stay with the guard tables (and, at arities the guard table does not take, with the recorded pair-keyed generalization's open shape); all-bare-Var sets stay with guarded identity — pinned by a unit test asserting that shape yields exactly the `guarded_identity` seed and no other. **Measured (probe + cross-validation, 3 runs each; M2 one pass):** the trio is seeded in every run as each spec's ONLY constraint seed, the seed is Verified in every pool, wins in every run of both instruments, and compiles. Executed agreement at the full pinned counts in every xval run: `rust_unary_text` golden 10/10, pinned 10/10 (Phase 31: 0/10 · 0/10 · timeout); `rust_concat_right_text` golden 3/3, pinned 4/4 (was at best 1/4); `rust_match_scrutinee_text` golden 2/2, pinned 3/3 (was 0/3). **Module totals per run:** ConstraintInduced winners **72/79** in every run (equation 32, guard_table 32, composition_guard 4, guarded_identity 1, expr_pin_guard 3), Verified winners **74·73·73/79** (Phase 31: 71·70·70 — exactly +3, the trio), probe compiles **79/79** in all three runs (the rotating E0689 junk draw simply did not occur this time), xval COMPILE_EDGE **0**; the three exec FAIL rows sit inside the remaining seedless set (`rust_concat_left_text`'s E0689 junk draw in run 1, non-terminating winners for `rust_concat_right_form` and `rust_match_scrutinee_form` in run 2 — the standing lottery bookkeeping, contained by the 10-second deadline). The 73 deterministic specs (every seeded spec plus `rust_ident_empty`'s Verified pool winner) agree on every executed case in every run: golden **188/188**, pinned **586/586**. Slices 1–3 preserved exactly (golden 130/130 + pinned 444/444 for the 43 non-ownership specs); M2 one pass unchanged (probe 14/14 Verified ConstraintInduced winners; xval golden 64/64 + sweep 539/539 in all runs). **No double-seeding, proven two ways:** the probe shows `expr_pin_guard` in the seed list of exactly the trio — each as the sole seed — across all runs, and the golden fixture (extended 42 → 45 bodies with the trio's) asserts all 42 prior resolver-visible bodies byte-identical. Seed-shape diagnostic flipped to the new recognition (RECOGNIZED 57 → 60); the five remaining slice-4 shapes stay asserted unrecognized on their Phase 31 predicates. Suite: **281 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored** (277 + 4 new builder tests). **Deliberately not admitted:** rows guarded on two Int keys, or one row spanning two keys (the pair-keyed shape, even with expression pins); a row pinning a range rather than one value; pins mentioning `output`, converting through a Hole, or calling an unindexed spec; pin sets with no compound pin whose Vars differ per row (unmeasured and unclaimed). **Next steps (recorded, ranking unchanged from Phase 31):** (i) **pair-keyed guard tables with literal pins** for `rust_concat_left_owned` / `rust_concat_right_form` / `rust_match_scrutinee_bare` — `guard_table_body`'s arity cap lifted to two Int parameters, the pinned cell recovered by joint falsify-every-leaf (scrutinee_bare's kind-only and lit-only rows pin their cells by the same joint falsification; its kind-22 column stays unpinned because no row decides it, G2); disjoint from this family by the same pin test. (ii) **Multi-guard truth tables** for `rust_match_scrutinee_form` — composition_guard over several shared Bool guard expressions, the pinned assignment recovered by falsify-every-leaf over the joint {false, true}ⁿ enumeration, the body nested Matches over the guards. (iii) **`rust_concat_left_text`'s territory amendment** — composition_guard admitting a plain Bool-variable guard with bare-variable pins when no earlier family admits the spec's arity; the cheapest single-spec fix, recorded with its cost (it bends the disjointness rule, so it wants the same all-rows-or-none care and a golden-fixture re-audit). `rust_string_ownership`'s two-parameter region rows remain the larger, separate generalization recorded in Phase 27. Slice 5 (the statement walker) remains unblocked by all of this.

> **Pair-keyed guard table landed (2026-11-21, Phase 33, branch `feat/pair-guard-seeds`):** the sibling generalization Phase 31 ranked (i) is implemented in `src/synthesis/engine.rs` (`pair_guard_table_body` / `pair_guard_table_seed_body`, seed kind `pair_guard_table`). **Admission:** exactly two parameters, both Int-family; rows are recognized exactly as the guard family's rows are (`guard_row_parts`: an Or of guard leaves plus one output pin); every leaf must parse via `guard_leaf` against one of the two parameters — the keys — and the leaves must jointly range over BOTH (a one-key row set is the single-key families' shape, refused here on the two-key requirement, just as the guard table's arity refuses it there); every pin must convert to an `IRNode::Literal` — the guard table's own pin test. A row-shaped postcondition deviating on any of this (a leaf on neither key, a non-literal pin) yields no seed at all (all rows or none); non-row postconditions are skipped as in the guard-table builder. **Pinned-cell recovery — joint falsify-every-leaf:** for each key the candidate span is the leaf constants' range widened by two on each side (the probe-span derivation of `pin_int_value`); outside the outermost constants every leaf's truth is stable, so the span endpoints double as the two unbounded tail classes and the finite grid describes the whole infinite table (spans whose joint grid exceeds 100,000 cells, and constants at the i64 extremes where the widening has no room, are refused rather than scanned wrongly). A cell is pinned exactly where the rows jointly determine ONE output value — every row whose guard leaves all fail at the cell pins it there; rows pinning one cell to two values are jointly inconsistent (not a table, no seed); rows pinning whole rows or columns (scrutinee_bare's kind-only and lit-only rows) pin their cells by the same joint falsification. Cells NO row decides stay unpinned — scrutinee_bare's kind-22 column is the measured case (G2) — and fall to the wildcard: a partial table is admitted, never forced. **Body:** one nested Match — over the first key, then over the second inside any column whose pinned cells disagree; a column whose pinned cells all agree renders that literal directly, and a column whose pinned cells all equal the fall-through value needs no arm. The wildcards carry the value the pinned fall-through cells determine (the tail columns', and for an inner match the column's tail cells') — NOT blindly the typed-literal default: Bool's `typed_literal_fillings` menu starts at `true`, but both measured Bool tables fall through to a pinned `false`; only when no pinned cell falls through does a wildcard carry the typed-literal default, the guard-table stance. Admission is the guard-table seed's (`body_is_encodable_with`; no call-resolution step — the pins are literals, so the body contains no calls), and the builder is the resolver's last fallback in `SpecIndex::callee_body`. **Territory:** literal pins only — disjoint from the expression-pin family (which requires a compound pin) and from guarded identity (a bare-variable pin) by the same pin test, and from the single-key guard table by the two-key requirement; no already-seeded spec gains a second seed or changes body (the golden fixture, extended 45 → 49, asserts the 45 prior bodies byte-identical). **Measured (probe + cross-validation, 3 runs each; M2 one pass):** the three target specs are seeded in every run as each spec's ONLY constraint seed, Verified in every pool, winning in every run of both instruments, compiling. Executed agreement at the full pinned counts in every xval run: `rust_concat_left_owned` golden 4/4, pinned **363/363** (Phase 31: timeout · 1/363 · 362/363); `rust_concat_right_form` golden 4/4, pinned **363/363** (was 2/363 · 32/363 · timeout); `rust_match_scrutinee_bare` golden 3/3, pinned **352/352** with its kind-22 column (11 cases) spec-silent in every run, never a disagreement (was 53/352 · 351/352 · 351/352). **The side effect, recorded plainly:** the builder also claims `rust_string_ownership`, recorded since Phase 27 as the larger separate generalization ("two-parameter region rows; pinned-value recovery would be a coverage problem") and asserted seedless as a non-goal in two places. As WRITTEN its rows are a pair table — `lit<3||lit>3||expected<3||expected>3||output==true` pins the single cell (3, 3) and the two half-plane rows pin false elsewhere, rust_concat_left_owned's exact three-row pattern at a different constant — because the "coverage problem" was the SINGLE-key recovery problem (each row alone pins a half-plane, no single value), and joint falsification over the pair recovers the whole table. Its seed is Verified and executes golden 54/54, pinned **1089/1089** in every run (Phase 32: 32/1089 · 1023/1089 · timeout). Both non-goal assertions were updated to record this (the composition-guard near-miss test keeps `rust_ident_empty` only; the seed-shape diagnostic's unrecognized list drops ownership). A name-based carve-out was rejected: admission here is by stated predicate, and this spec satisfies it exactly. What remains unadmitted is the genuinely regional shape — a guard leaf comparing a parameter against another PARAMETER (`guard_leaf` parses only parameter-vs-constant leaves) — which no measured spec writes. **Module totals per run:** ConstraintInduced winners **76/79** in every run (equation 32, guard_table 32, composition_guard 4, guarded_identity 1, expr_pin_guard 3, pair_guard_table 4 — Phase 32's 72 plus exactly these four), Verified winners **78·78·77/79** (the 76 seeds + `rust_ident_empty`'s pool winner in every run, + one of the two remaining lotteries in runs 1–2), probe compiles **79·78·79/79** (run 2's one failure is `rust_match_scrutinee_form`'s seedless Mutation winner — pool quality, the standing bookkeeping), xval COMPILE_EDGE **0** and exec FAIL **0** in all runs. The 77 deterministic specs (every seeded spec plus `rust_ident_empty`) agree on every executed case in every run: golden **253/253**, pinned **2753/2753** — the arithmetic closes exactly over Phase 32's 188/586 plus the four new specs' 65 golden and 2,167 pinned, so slices 1–3 are preserved exactly; M2 one pass unchanged (probe 14/14 Verified ConstraintInduced winners; xval golden 64/64 + sweep 539/539 in all runs). **No double-seeding, proven two ways:** the probe shows `pair_guard_table` in the seed list of exactly the four specs — each as the sole seed — across all runs, and the golden fixture asserts all 45 prior resolver-visible bodies byte-identical. Seed-shape diagnostic flipped to the new recognition (RECOGNIZED 60 → 64); the three remaining unrecognized shapes are `rust_ident_empty`'s String-equality leaf family, `rust_match_scrutinee_form`'s two-guard Bool rows, and `rust_concat_left_text`'s territory hole. Suite: **287 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored** (281 + 6 new builder tests). **Deliberately not admitted:** specs with other than two parameters (a three-key row set, or pair rows inside a wider signature — the measured specs are all exactly two-parameter, and the conservative arity rule matches guarded identity's); rows over one key only; compound or bare-variable pins spanning two keys (no family states them — the expression-pin and guarded-identity key disciplines are one key each); conflicting-cell tables; pins mentioning `output` or converting through a Hole; parameter-vs-parameter guard leaves (the genuinely regional shape above). **Remaining (recorded, ranking unchanged):** (ii) **multi-guard truth tables** for `rust_match_scrutinee_form` — composition_guard over several shared Bool guard expressions, the pinned assignment recovered by falsify-every-leaf over the joint {false, true}ⁿ enumeration, the body nested Matches over the guards. (iii) **`rust_concat_left_text`'s territory amendment** — composition_guard admitting a plain Bool-variable guard with bare-variable pins when no earlier family admits the spec's arity; the cheapest single-spec fix, recorded with its cost (it bends the disjointness rule, so it wants the same all-rows-or-none care and a golden-fixture re-audit). `rust_string_ownership`'s region rows are CLOSED by this phase (see the side effect above) — the Phase 27 item survives only as the unadmitted parameter-vs-parameter shape, which no measured spec writes. Slice 5 (the statement walker) remains unblocked by all of this.

> **Multi-guard truth tables + the composition precedence landed (2026-11-21, Phase 34, branch `feat/multi-guard-seeds`):** the last two recorded items close. **(ii) Multi-guard truth tables** are implemented in `src/synthesis/engine.rs` (`multi_guard_body` / `multi_guard_row` / `multi_guard_seed_body`, seed kind `multi_guard`; the builder sits after composition in both `seed_from_constraints` and `SpecIndex::callee_body`). **Admission:** rows are recognized exactly as the guard family's rows are (`guard_row_parts`: an Or of guard leaves plus one output pin); the pin is admitted the way `composition_pin` admits pins (hole-free, output-free, over the parameters, calling only indexed specs); a row's leaves may compare ANY subset of the guard expressions against BoolLit constants (`guard_leaf_on_expr`), grouped per expression, each group pinning a single value by falsify-every-leaf (`pin_bool_value`) — the row then pins every cell of the joint {false, true}ⁿ table extending that partial assignment. The guard set is the distinct expressions across all rows, in first-appearance order (postcondition order, leaf order within a row); every guard must be a valid composition scrutinee (a Bool parameter, or a call to an indexed spec returning Bool). A row-shaped postcondition deviating on any of this yields no seed at all (all rows or none); non-row postconditions are skipped as in the guard-table builder. **Territory — the n=1 choice:** the family requires at least TWO distinct guard expressions, so single-guard specs keep producing EXACTLY the existing composition-guard bodies — the generalization does not reduce to the old body at n=1, it never fires there — and at most `MAX_MULTI_GUARD_EXPRESSIONS` = 4, because the joint table (and the nested match) doubles per guard. **Pinned cells** are recovered by joint falsify-every-leaf over the whole table — the pair-keyed family's joint falsification on the Bool domain: a cell is pinned exactly where every row firing there agrees on the value; two rows pinning one cell to different values are jointly inconsistent (not a truth table, no seed); cells NO row decides stay unpinned and fall to the wildcard (a partial table is admitted, never forced). **Body:** nested Matches over the guard expressions, outermost-first in guard order — along any single execution path each guard is evaluated at most once, as one match's scrutinee (a call scrutinee may repeat textually across sibling subtrees, but specs are pure, so the repetition is value-identical); a subtree whose cells' effective values (the pin where pinned, the typed-literal default where not) all agree renders that value directly, with no match at all; every match ends in the wildcard carrying the typed-literal default (the composition stance — the Bool domain is total, so with both literal arms present the wildcard is the verifier-required unconditional last arm). Admission is the composition seed's (`SpecIndex::inline_calls` → `body_is_encodable_with`). **(iii) The composition precedence:** `composition_guard_body`'s territory refusal is restated as a precedence with the refusal evaluated in the builder — a plain-variable guard whose pins are all literals or bare variables returns no body exactly when `guard_table_body(spec).is_some() || guarded_identity_body(spec).is_some()`, plus one guard of its own: every bare-variable pin must name a parameter of the return type (guarded identity's own condition on its identity parameter — the only way the pin can BE the output). Disjointness therefore holds BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by pin-shape inspection: whenever an earlier family admits the spec, the composition builder still refuses, so no spec gains a second seed. **Reclassifications, recorded:** three synthetic shapes asserted seedless since the guarded-identity phase — a literal row beside an identity row, a pin naming the guard parameter itself, a bare-variable pin at arity three — were recorded against GUARDED IDENTITY's predicate, and guarded identity still refuses all three on that predicate; under the precedence the composition family states them, and they are now asserted as composition-guard positives whose bodies honor their rows (`constraint_composition_precedence_both_directions`). A fourth shape, `bool_pin_not_literal`, stays a near miss on the precedence's own return-type condition (its pin names the Bool guard parameter, which cannot be the String output). The composition near-miss `comp_mixed_guard_expressions` also stays seedless, but its rationale changes: the two-guard shape is now the multi-guard family's, and that instance is refused on its conflicting (true, true) cell. **Measured (probe + cross-validation, 3 runs each; M2 instruments in full):** `rust_match_scrutinee_form` is seeded in every run as its ONLY constraint seed (kind `multi_guard`), Verified in every pool, winning in every run of both instruments, executing golden **4/4**, pinned **4/4** (Phase 31: 3/4 · a COMPILE_EDGE junk winner · 4/4, by lottery); `rust_concat_left_text` likewise (kind `composition_guard`, via the precedence), golden **2/2**, pinned **4/4** (Phase 31: 0/4 · 0/4 · 4/4, by lottery). **Module totals per run:** ConstraintInduced winners **78/79** in every run (equation 32, guard_table 32, composition_guard **5**, guarded_identity 1, expr_pin_guard 3, pair_guard_table 4, multi_guard **1** — Phase 33's 76 plus exactly these two), Verified winners **79/79** in every run (the 78 seeds plus `rust_ident_empty`'s deterministic Verified pool winner, tpr 1.000), probe compiles **79/79** in all runs, xval COMPILE_EDGE **0**, exec FAIL **0**, and **zero disagreements** in all runs: every winner in the module — the 78 seeds plus ident_empty's pool winner — agrees on every executed case in every run, golden **259/259**, pinned **2761/2761** (2,761 is the module's full pinned total; the arithmetic closes exactly over Phase 33's 253/2753 plus scrutinee_form's 4 golden + 4 pinned and left_text's 2 golden + 4 pinned, so slices 1–4 are preserved exactly). M2 unchanged in full (probe 14/14 Verified ConstraintInduced winners; xval golden 64/64 + sweep 539/539 in all three runs). **No double-seeding, proven two ways:** the probe shows `multi_guard` as the sole seed of exactly `rust_match_scrutinee_form` and `composition_guard` as the sole seed of exactly `rust_concat_left_text` across all runs, and the golden fixture — extended 49 → 51 bodies — asserts all 49 prior resolver-visible bodies byte-identical, which is also the proof that the precedence moved no earlier family's body and that the multi-guard family fires nowhere at n=1. Seed-shape diagnostic flipped to the new recognition (RECOGNIZED 64 → 66); the asserted-unrecognized list is down to `rust_ident_empty` alone. Suite: **292 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored** (287 + 5 new builder tests). **Deliberately not admitted:** more than four distinct guard expressions; a single guard expression (the composition family's, unchanged); conflicting-cell tables; pins mentioning `output`, converting through a Hole, or calling an unindexed spec; mixed Int-guard rows among Bool rows (the Int leaves stay with the Int families, whose builders see the same row set and deviate on the Bool rows symmetrically); guard leaves comparing two parameters; and String-equality guards — `rust_ident_empty`'s family, the module's one remaining seedless spec, BY DESIGN (it crowns a Verified pool winner in every run, so it is a recorded non-goal, not a lottery). **What remains between here and the M3 gate, stated precisely:** the seed work for slices 1–4 is DONE — every Codegen spec any recorded builder family can state is seeded (78/79, the exception being the by-design String-guard non-goal). What remains is not seeds but slices: **slice 5** (the statement walker `ir_to_rust` + `ir_to_rust_tail`, ~343 lines — specs + mirrors + measurement on the established pattern, with its own representation questions: statement sequences, indentation, the tail-position classification) and **slice 6** (the driver `generate_rust` + `generate` and the whole-file assembly, then the GATE itself: the generated codegen — the module's winners composed — producing byte-identical Rust output against the original emitter on the golden corpus; the recorded generated-test placement E0308 sits in the driver's territory and is the gate's business, not the seeds'). After the gate: M4 integration and M5 bootstrap, per the checkboxes below.
>
> **Slice 5 specs (2026-11-21, Phase 35, branch `feat/m3-slice5-specs`):** 27 specs added to `examples/self_host/codegen.morph` (106 specs, 157 in-spec tests total), one hand mirror per spec plus the assembly mirrors `ir_stmt_assembled` / `ir_tail_assembled` in `tests/m3_proof.rs` (63 tests, +11), `morphic check` passes, full suite 303 passed / 0 failed / 6 ignored. **Scoping decision: the FULL walker in one phase** — both functions, every arm of `ir_to_rust` (src/codegen/mod.rs:310-498) and `ir_to_rust_tail` (507-541) — because the arms share one representation (pre-rendered children, pad as an input) and the sub-slice boundaries fell out of the emitter's own structure; no 5a/5b split was needed. Two arms need no template of their own: Block (pure concatenation — its discipline is the sequence classification) and Typed in statement position (a transparent recursion; only the TAIL Typed arm decides anything, via the tail dispatch). **The slice's three representation questions, resolved explicitly:** (a) **Sequences** — a Block's rendering is the plain concatenation of its statements' renderings, no separator and no braces of its own (the output's braces belong to the If/While/For/Match arms); there is no sequence template to pin, the iteration is mirror-only (F5 family, like the `", "` joins), and the specs pin the per-statement DECISION: `rust_stmt_seq_form` (which renderer renders each statement, by position × last-ness) and `rust_tail_form`. (b) **Indentation** — the pad string (`"    "` × indent) is repetition by a count, hence iteration, hence mirror-only; the specs take pad (and pad_inner where an arm uses both) as pre-rendered String inputs and pin the ARITHMETIC as `rust_stmt_child_indent`, the total table of relative indents over the nine placement sites (Block statement / Let tail body / If branch / While body / For body / Match arm header / Match arm body at +2 / tail Block non-last / tail Block last). (c) **Tail classification** — `rust_tail_form` is the tail walker's dispatch as a total table over the 28 kinds (statement fallthrough / bare expression / Block split / Typed re-entry), `rust_stmt_form` is the statement walker's own dispatch likewise total (18 arm codes in source order, the fallback arm catching the eleven expression shapes), and `rust_expectation_source` pins which expectation every expectation-carrying site of the two walkers passes (Some(fn_ret) at the Let/If/Match value positions and the Return arm; Some(own type) at the Alloc initial and the tail Typed re-entry; inherited through a tail Block's last statement) — the provenance half of slice 3's ownership rule. **The specs** — classifications: `rust_stmt_form`, `rust_tail_form`, `rust_stmt_collection_prefix` / `rust_stmt_collection_suffix` (statement position: Set and Map collapse COMPLETELY to the List form, suffix included — contrast slice 4's fully qualified expression forms), `rust_expectation_source`, `rust_stmt_child_indent`, `rust_stmt_seq_form`; statement templates: `rust_stmt_hole_text` (two lines, TODO comment + panic, both padded — its own format site, not slice 4's expression Hole), `rust_stmt_let_text`, `rust_stmt_if_text`, `rust_stmt_while_text` (Bool-guarded rows: the invariant comment line present or not; the invariant text arrives raw from the IR), `rust_stmt_for_text`, the Match trio `rust_stmt_match_head_text` / `rust_stmt_match_arm_text` / `rust_stmt_match_close_text` (the statement Match wraps each arm body in its own braces — not slice 4's one-line expression arms), `rust_stmt_call_text`, `rust_stmt_return_text` (the typed operand — not slice 4's parenthesized untyped `(return x)`), `rust_stmt_alloc_text`, `rust_stmt_assign_text` (bare — not the expression form's parentheses), `rust_stmt_collection_text` (an equation composing the two affix classifications BY CALL inside the `++` chain, on the Phase 30 precedent), `rust_stmt_map_text` / `rust_stmt_filter_text` / `rust_stmt_fold_text` (the slice-4 iterator templates terminated), `rust_stmt_sort_text`, `rust_stmt_expr_text` (the fallback: the expression rendering terminated, block forms included); tail templates: `rust_tail_expr_text` (pad + typed rendering + newline, NO `;` — the E0308 fix this walker exists for) and `rust_tail_block_text` (the split's assembly as a plain concat equation; an empty tail Block renders the empty string). **Surprises in the real code:** the If arm's closing braces carry NO pad (its format string interpolates none — While/For/Match closings sit at pad; nested, an If's braces land at column 0); the Sort arm's second and third lines likewise carry no pad at any depth; While and For bodies render as STATEMENTS (their values are discarded by construction) while If branches and Let/Match bodies render as TAILS — the position discipline is per-arm, not per-shape; and a statement-position Loop/Break/StructDef routes through the EXPRESSION renderer plus `;` (so a statement Loop keeps its block form and takes a trailing `;`). The assembly mirrors perform the mutual recursion over a `Stmt` tree (expression children reuse the slice-4 `Expr` tree; the typed mirror carries the one ownership rewrite; the scrutinee assembly composes slice 4's trio), and the golden covers: both dispatch tables in full, the affix collapse, expectation provenance, the indent table, multi-statement sequences, depth-2 nesting (If in Block, Match arm bodies at +3, While with invariant), tail-vs-statement pairs for the same node (Collection List/Set, Call, Map, Sort, Block split, empty Block), and the ownership pairs (Return under String vs Int fn_ret, Let-body and If-branch tail literals, Alloc String vs Int declared type, tail Typed re-entry vs statement transparency). Diagnostic scoping on the Phase 30 precedent: the seed-shape diagnostic's module count moves 79 → 106, its RECOGNIZED set and the slices 1–4 sweep tables unchanged — slice-5 seed recognition is the measurement phase's question. **Measurement expectations, recorded for the next phase:** the 19 pure `++` templates should take equation seeds (including `rust_stmt_collection_text`, whose equation composes two guard-table calls inside the chain — `rust_binop_infix_text`'s precedent); the six total Int classifications should take guard-table seeds (literal pins, String pins for the affixes); `rust_stmt_while_text` is `rust_break_text`'s shape (a Bool parameter guard with compound pins) and should take a composition-guard seed; `rust_tail_block_text` is a two-part concat equation. The one shape no current family obviously admits is `rust_stmt_seq_form`: its decisive row guards jointly on an Int key (position) AND a Bool key (last) — guard tables take one key, the pair-keyed family takes two INT keys, the composition/multi-guard families are Bool-domain only — a mixed Int×Bool two-key table is the candidate next builder gap, if the measurement confirms it.

> **Slice 5 measured (2026-11-21, Phase 36, branch `feat/m3-slice5-measurement`):** the cross-validation harness now covers all 106 Codegen specs (slice-5 mirrors transcribed from `tests/m3_proof.rs`, with three stated-domain extensions under the pinned-by-constraint precedent — the two dispatch tables' catch-all rows and `rust_stmt_seq_form` are total in the xval mirrors, where the proof mirrors state only their pinned rows; on every input both mirror sets state, the values agree; `rust_stmt_collection_text` composed over the affix mirrors, pinned exactly on kinds 0..=4; new (Int, Bool) / (Bool, String×4) / (String×5) input shapes with runner arms; 3,807 sweep cases per run: 2,914 pinned, 886 spec-silent, 7 out of contract — slice 5 contributes 259 cases, 153 pinned), the probe's expectations cover 106 specs with a 15-name pool-dump watch list, and the seed-shape diagnostic is flipped to the measured recognition (RECOGNIZED 66 → 92; asserted-unrecognized now `rust_ident_empty` + `rust_stmt_seq_form`). Measured over 3 runs of each instrument, identical in structure every run (M2 one pass, unchanged: probe 14/14 Verified ConstraintInduced winners; cross-validation golden 64/64 + sweep 539/539 per configuration): **seeds for 104/106 specs** (equation 51, guard_table 38, composition_guard 6, expr_pin_guard 3, pair_guard_table 4, guarded_identity 1, multi_guard 1), **ConstraintInduced winners 101/106**, **Verified winners 102/106**, compiles 102/106; slices 1–4 preserved exactly (78/79 ConstraintInduced, Verified 79/79, and in the executed deterministic set golden 259/259 + pinned 2761/2761). **The Phase 35 predictions, scored:** (a) CONFIRMED in full — all 19 pure `++` templates take equation seeds, verify, and win, including `rust_stmt_collection_text` composing two guard-table calls inside its chain (executes golden 5/5, pinned 5/5) and `rust_tail_block_text`. (b) CONFIRMED at admission, REFUTED in part at verification — all six classifications take guard-table seeds; three verify and win (`rust_tail_form` — its Eq-leaf catch-all pins 0, the typed-literal default, the `rust_typed_form` luck a second time — and the two affixes), but three seeds are REFUTED by Z3 in every run (finding 1 below). (c) CONFIRMED — `rust_stmt_while_text` takes the composition-guard seed (`rust_break_text`'s exact shape), Verified, wins every run; its executed agreement is COMPILE_EDGE'd (finding 2). (d) CONFIRMED seedless — `rust_stmt_seq_form`, diagnosed per family below, with the eighth family specified. **Finding 1 — RANGE ROWS defeat the single-key guard table's recovery, and the failure is silent.** `pin_int_value` recovers a row's pinned key only when EXACTLY ONE value falsifies every guard leaf; slices 1–4 write one row per single value, so this never bit. Three slice-5 classifications write rows whose falsifying region is a SET: `rust_expectation_source`'s bands (`site<0||site>4||output==0` pins sites 0..=4 at once; `site<5||site>6||output==1` pins {5,6}), `rust_stmt_child_indent`'s bands ({0,1}→0, {2..5}→1, {7,8}→0), and `rust_stmt_form`'s Eq-leaf catch-all (17 leaves pinning the complement of {0..8, 15..22} → 17). For such a row `guard_row` returns None — the NON-row outcome, not a deviation — so `guard_table_body` silently SKIPS it, and the body's wildcard carries the typed-literal default (0), contradicting the skipped rows wherever they pin a non-default value. The seeds therefore measure tpr 0.833 (`rust_stmt_form`: the loop-fallback in-spec test, kind 24 → 17, fails) and 0.667 / 0.667, and Z3 REFUTES all three deterministically (seed status VerificationFailed in every run; their pools hold zero other Verified candidates — 0/27, 0/21, 0/20 in run 1). Selection crowns Inconclusive Mutation winners (probe tpr 0.000 in every run); executed, `rust_stmt_form`'s winner is non-terminating in all three runs (exec FAIL, golden 0/6, pinned 0/33), `rust_expectation_source`'s run-1 winner panics (FAIL 0/3, 0/8) and its other draws reach golden 1/3, pinned 5/8, and `rust_stmt_child_indent`'s draws reach golden 0/3 · 1/3 · 1/3, pinned 0/9 · 4/9 · 1/9. The two earlier catch-all rows escaped only because their pinned value coincides with the default (`rust_typed_form`'s 0, `rust_tail_form`'s 0); the pair-keyed family already recovers ranges correctly, by joint falsification over its grid. The recorded fix shape for a future builder phase: lift that cell recovery to one key — candidate span from the leaf constants (±2 widening, span endpoints as unbounded tail classes), a cell pinned iff every firing row agrees, the wildcard carrying the pinned fall-through value when the tails agree — materializing band/catch-all rows the current builder drops, while every existing single-value body stays byte-identical (the golden-fixture discipline: range rows currently contribute no arm, so only specs that HAVE them change). Until then the three specs are recognized-but-refuted: the diagnostic keeps them in RECOGNIZED because a body IS built and encodes — recognition and verification are different steps, and this finding is exactly the gap between them. **Finding 2 — a FOURTH rendering family: E0382, Concat moves a String variable.** Four Verified seed winners do not compile as emitted in any run — `rust_stmt_hole_text`, `rust_stmt_while_text`, `rust_stmt_for_text`, `rust_stmt_match_arm_text` — all with `error[E0382]: borrow of moved value: pad` (resp. `pad_inner`): Concat renders as Rust `+`, which MOVES a String value in left-operand position; Phase 29's treatment covers a string LITERAL there (`.to_string()`) and borrows right operands, but a String VARIABLE in left position still moves, and these four templates use one pad parameter two or three times in a single `++` chain, so the second use borrows a moved value. At IR level the seeds are right (the interpreter has no moves; `rust_stmt_hole_text`'s seed measures tpr 1.000; the other three are test-less or partially tested shapes whose 0.500 probe tpr is the neutral prior). The harness books them COMPILE_EDGE under a new classifier arm (kind `E0382 concat-operand move`; 6 golden + 10 pinned cases per run unmeasurable) — the IR has no moves, so an E0382 is only ever a rendering failure, never a behavioral disagreement. NOT fixed: the emitter stays frozen, and whether this family earns a second, separately scoped unfreeze (borrow/clone treatment for variable operands in Concat left position, mirroring the literal treatment) is the owner's decision, recorded here as gate business — the byte-identical gate assembles winners into a codegen that must itself compile. **Prediction (d) at predicate level:** `rust_stmt_seq_form` (params `position: Int`, `last: Bool`; rows R1 `position<1||position>1||last==false||output==1`, R2 `position==1||output==0`, R3 `last==true||output==0`) is refused by every family: guard_table — `params.len() != 1` (two params); pair_guard_table — `pair_guard_table_seed_body` requires `is_int_family` on BOTH key types and `last` is Bool (its row recognizer parses leaves via `guard_leaf`, IntLit constants only); composition_guard — its leaves must parse via `guard_leaf_on_expr` (Eq/Neq against BoolLit on ONE shared Bool expression), but R1/R2 carry Lt/Gt leaves against IntLit on `position`, R1 mixes two distinct guard targets, and an Int parameter is not a valid `composition_scrutinee`; multi_guard — the same leaf-parse failure (its guards are Bool expressions; `position`'s comparisons are not); expr_pin_guard — it nominates ONE Int key and parses every leaf via `guard_leaf(leaf, key)`, so R1's `last==false` leaf is a deviation, and all three pins are literals, which its ≥1-compound-pin territory rule refuses anyway; guarded_identity — the pins are Int literals, not the identity Var; equation — no unconditional `output == <expr>` postcondition exists. Its pools across the three runs (60 candidates: 57 Mutation + 3 Template) hold ZERO Verified candidates (39 VerificationFailed, 18 Inconclusive, 3 Malformed); winners are Inconclusive Mutations executing at golden 1/4 · 3/4 · 1/4, pinned 1/12 · 11/12 · 1/12 — the run-2 draw misses exactly one cell, so the pool can nearly reach the table but never provably. **The eighth family, specified: the mixed pair-keyed guard table** — the pair-keyed family with one Int key and one Bool key. Admission: exactly two parameters, one Int-family (Int or a named integer type) and one Bool; every postcondition row-shaped by `guard_row_parts`; every guard leaf parses via `guard_leaf` on the Int key or `guard_leaf_bool` on the Bool key, with at least one leaf on EACH key across the row set (a one-key row set belongs to the earlier families); output pins literal-only (Int / Bool / String literals), which keeps it disjoint from expr_pin_guard (compound pins over one Int key) and from multi_guard (Bool-EXPRESSION guards with composition pins). Pinned-cell recovery: joint falsification over the grid — Int candidates by the pair family's span discipline (the Int leaves' constants widened ±2, span endpoints doubling as the unbounded tail classes) × {false, true} — a cell pinned iff every row firing at it (all leaves false) agrees on the pin; a conflict refuses the seed; partial tables admitted, undecided cells unpinned. Body: nested Match in the pair family's column discipline — outer over the Int key (literal patterns, span order), inner over the Bool key (Bool literal patterns) — whose wildcards carry the pinned fall-through value when the undecided cells are value-uniform, else the typed-literal default (the pair family's wildcard rule). Territory is disjoint by key-type signature: (Int) guard_table, (Int, Int) pair_guard_table, (Int, Bool) this family, (Bool-expression set) multi_guard / composition_guard — no spec gains a second seed, and the golden-fixture discipline applies unchanged. Under it `rust_stmt_seq_form`'s table is fully pinned: (0, false) → 0, (0, true) → 0, (1, false) → 1, (1, true) → 0, (≥2, *) → 0. **Executed totals:** the deterministic set (slices 1–4 plus the 19 executed slice-5 specs) agrees on every case in every run — golden 290/290, pinned 2842/2842; raw executed sums (lottery draws included, FAIL/COMPILE_EDGE rows excluded) golden 291/297 · 295/300 · 293/300, pinned 2843/2863 · 2862/2871 · 2849/2871, the entire variance being the four lottery specs' draws. Slice-5-only probe state: seeded 26/27, seed winners 23/27, Verified winners 23/27, compiles 23/27. **What remains to the M3 gate:** slice 6 (the driver specs — `generate_rust` / `generate` whole-file assembly — and the byte-identical gate itself) plus three measured items now standing on the gate's path: the range-row recovery fix (finding 1 — three classifications have no Verified winner without it), the eighth family (without it `rust_stmt_seq_form` stays a lottery), and the E0382 unfreeze decision (finding 2 — four template winners do not compile as emitted, and the gate's assembled codegen must compile). > **Range-row recovery — Finding 1 CLOSED (2026-11-21, Phase 37, branch `fix/guard-range-rows`):** the single-key guard table now recovers rows JOINTLY, the pair family's cell recovery lifted to one key exactly as Phase 36 recorded the fix shape. Each row contributes its whole falsifying REGION over the candidate span (the leaf constants' range widened by ±2, span endpoints doubling as the unbounded tail classes, constants at the i64 extremes and spans over 100,000 cells refused); a cell is pinned iff every row firing there agrees on one value, and conflicting pins refuse the seed. The wildcard carries the pinned fall-through value when the tail classes agree on one — `rust_stmt_form`'s Eq-leaf catch-all pins both tails to 17, so its wildcard is now 17, not the typed default 0 — else the typed-literal default. Materialization keeps the builder's standing convention (arms in row order; a single-value row arms its cell unconditionally, even where its pin equals the wildcard's value — node_arity's 0 → 0 and 1 → 0 arms beside a 0 wildcard are the standing example), and a band row arms only its interior cells the wildcard does not already yield, its tail cells riding the wildcard. Byte-identity is proven two ways: a survey diff of every spec's resolver-visible body (all 120 specs across validator/printer/codegen, before vs after) shows EXACTLY the three corrections — `rust_stmt_form`'s 17 arms unchanged with only the wildcard 0 → 17, `rust_expectation_source` gaining arms 5 → 1 and 6 → 1, `rust_stmt_child_indent` gaining 2..=5 → 1 — and `rust_typed_form` / `rust_tail_form`, the catch-alls that escaped Finding 1 by pinning the default, reproduce byte-identically (their fall-through value is 0, their catch-all cells pin the wildcard's own value and materialize no arm); the golden seed-body fixture asserts its 51 prior bodies unchanged and gains the three corrected bodies (54 total — the three were recognized-but-refuted before, so their wrong bodies were never fixture material; the corrected ones are pinned from now on). The silent-skip failure mode is closed STRUCTURALLY, not just for these rows: a row-shaped postcondition the recovered table does not subsume — a non-literal pin, a guard leaf that does not parse on the parameter, a conflicting pin — is a DEVIATION (the whole builder returns None) in both the Int and Bool guard-table paths, never a skip; a genuinely non-row postcondition (node_arity's `output >= 0` floor is the measured case) is still skipped as before, and a row whose guards are jointly always true fires nowhere and is subsumed vacuously. **The audit of the other table builders:** pair_guard_table CLEARED (joint region falsification with deviation semantics already — no silent-skip path exists in its row handling); multi_guard CLEARED (same joint discipline on the Bool domain, conflicts refuse); guarded_identity CLEARED (row failures are deviations, and its duplicate-guard-value drop is subsumed by construction — every admitted row pins the same identity Var); expr_pin_guard CLEARED for band rows with a reason — it recovers rows through `pin_int_value`, so a band row is a parse FAILURE there, which its all-rows-or-none discipline turns into a deviation: it can decline to seed, but it can never emit a body that ignores a row's region, which is the Finding 1 disease; composition_guard likewise turns every row-shaped failure into a deviation. One miniature of the disease did survive in two builders — the first-wins dedup in expr_pin_guard and composition_guard dropped a second row pinning an already-pinned key/guard value even when its pin DIFFERED — and is HARDENED to the same rule: an agreeing duplicate pin is subsumed and skipped, a different one is a deviation (the Bool guard table's duplicate handling gets the same agree-or-conflict treatment in the joint recovery). `guard_row` / `guard_row_bool` are deleted; their recognition lives in the two new body builders, and `pin_int_value` remains for the families whose admission is single-value by design (expr_pin_guard, guarded_identity). **Measured** (M3 probe + cross-validation, 3 runs each, identical in structure; M2 one pass, unchanged: probe 14/14 Verified ConstraintInduced winners, xval golden 64/64 + sweep 539/539): the three specs' seeds are Verified and WIN every run at tpr 1.000 (was: refuted every run, Inconclusive Mutation winners, one non-terminating in all three runs), executing at their full pinned counts — `rust_stmt_form` golden 6/6 + pinned **33/33**, `rust_expectation_source` golden 3/3 + pinned **8/8**, `rust_stmt_child_indent` golden 3/3 + pinned **9/9**. Module totals per run: seeds for 104/106 (seedless unchanged: `rust_ident_empty` by design, `rust_stmt_seq_form` awaiting the eighth family), seed-kind tally unchanged (guard_table 38 — the three were already guard-table seeds; now they are correct ones), ConstraintInduced winners 101 → **104/106**, Verified winners 102 → **105/106**, compiles 102/106 — the four non-compiling winners are exactly Finding 2's E0382 four, untouched by this phase and still booked COMPILE_EDGE (6 golden + 10 pinned cases per run unmeasurable). The executed deterministic set grows by exactly the three specs: golden **302/302** + pinned **2892/2892** in every run (was 290/2842), slices 1–4 preserved exactly (golden 259/259 + pinned 2761/2761); the only remaining executed variance is `rust_stmt_seq_form`'s lottery (golden 0/4 · 2/4 with a non-terminating draw in run 1, COMPILE_EDGE in run 3). The seed-shape diagnostic needs no flip — recognition is unchanged (the three were RECOGNIZED before; recognition and verification were the two steps Finding 1 was the gap between, and the gap is now closed). **What remains to the M3 gate:** slice 6 (driver specs + the byte-identical gate itself), the eighth family for `rust_stmt_seq_form`, and the E0382 unfreeze decision (Finding 2). > **Phase 39 re-pin inventory (2026-11-21, branch `fix/codegen-e0382`) — taken BEFORE any behavior change.** The user (project owner) approved a SECOND scoped unfreeze of the Rust emitter on 2026-11-21, for exactly the fourth rendering family measured in Phase 36 (Finding 2) — **E0382, Concat-operand move**: IR Concat renders as Rust `+`, which MOVES a String variable in left-operand position; Phase 29's treatment owns a string LITERAL there (`.to_string()`) and borrows right operands, but a plain VARIABLE used two or more times on one execution path (the statement templates' `pad` / `pad_inner` parameters, used 2–3× in a single `++` chain) fails `error[E0382]: borrow of moved value`. Exactly four Verified seed winners do not compile as emitted in any run: `rust_stmt_hole_text`, `rust_stmt_while_text`, `rust_stmt_for_text`, `rust_stmt_match_arm_text` (probe compiles 102/106; the cross-validation harness books their 6 golden + 10 pinned cases per run as COMPILE_EDGE). At IR level the seeds are right (the IR has no moves); the defect is purely the Rust rendering layer, and it stands on the M3 gate's path (slice 6's assembled codegen must itself compile). Every expectation in the repo that pins the defective rendering is listed below, per item: current pinned behavior → what the fixed behavior must be. Everything NOT listed must remain byte-identical after the fix; that is the freeze's surviving scope.
>
> **(i) Rendering-path trace.** A winner's file is produced by `generate_rust` (src/codegen/mod.rs:66): the signature renders each parameter's type via `ty_to_rust` — the ONLY place a parameter's String type is ever materialized — and the body renders via `ir_to_rust_tail` (mod.rs:122) with `fn_ret` = the return type, falling through the statement/tail walkers to `ir_to_rust_expr_typed` (mod.rs:549) with the return expectation. The typed walker rewrites ONLY a bare string literal under a String expectation; every other node — Concat included — delegates to the UNTYPED `ir_to_rust_expr` (mod.rs:742), whose Concat arm (mod.rs:773) renders `({} + {})` over `concat_owned_operand` (mod.rs:622) and `concat_borrowed_operand` (mod.rs:640). So at the Concat site the emitter holds NO type environment: `pad`'s String-ness exists in the emitted signature text and nowhere in the rendering machinery. The four winners' seed bodies are single Concat chains (equation seeds — the specs' own `output == …` expressions; `rust_stmt_hole_text`'s emitted body, verbatim: `(((pad + "// TODO: synthesis incomplete\n") + &pad) + "panic!(\"Hole not filled\");\n")` — the first `+` moves `pad`, the `&pad` then borrows the moved value). Any fix at this site is therefore necessarily SHAPE-based, exactly as Phase 29's was. Two candidate treatments were evaluated against rustc 1.100.0 BEFORE this inventory was finalized: **borrow-based left forms are DEAD** — `&String + &str` and `&String + &String` have no `Add` impl (both E0369, verified empirically; Rust's string concatenation requires an owned `String` on the left), so no borrow rule can fix the shape at all. The surviving treatment is the literal treatment's own analogue: an owned copy — `.clone()` for a variable, as `.to_string()` is for a literal (`.clone()` is also the emitter's own Clone-op rendering, slice 4's `rust_unary_text` op 9). **The fixed behavior:** in `concat_owned_operand`, a direct `IRNode::Var(name)` renders `{name}.clone()`; the literal arm and every other shape are unchanged (temporaries — Call results, nested Concats, block expressions — move freely and correctly). **Scope flag, stated plainly:** this changes the bytes of SINGLE-USE variable-left renderings that compile today (e.g. `(inner + "!")` → `(inner.clone() + "!")`). No compositional rule can avoid that: whether a bare move is legal depends on uses ANYWHERE later on the execution path — later in the chain, in later statements of a Block, in a Let's body after its value — information an operand renderer does not hold; the only alternatives are clone-on-every-Var-left (this fix) or a whole-body liveness analysis threaded through every walker, which is not a rendering-layer fix and would make one body's rendering depend on global context, breaking the compositional spec/mirror structure slices 4–5 are built on. The clone is value-transparent (the IR is pure), so every currently-compiling winner keeps its behavior; only its bytes move, and every moved byte is inventoried below. For a List-typed variable the rendering still fails E0369 (`Vec + &Vec` has no `Add` impl before or after — the Phase 29 not-fixed item, unchanged); for a `&str`-typed variable (let-bound to a bare literal) it still fails E0369 as today. Examined and EXCLUDED from the fix: a `Typed`-wrapped variable in left position (it delegates through the typed walker to the same bare Var rendering — no measured winner has this shape; seed bodies contain no Typed nodes; and the decision spec S-1 pins the DIRECT node only, the stance its doc already records for the literal rule) and an `Index` into a String collection in left position (moving out of an index is E0508, a different family, no measured case).
>
> **(ii) codegen unit tests in src/codegen/mod.rs — 2 re-pins.** (C-1) `rust_concat_literal_pair_and_variable_first_compile`: the variable-first byte assertion `(inner + "!")`, whose comment records it as byte-identical to pre-fix → `(inner.clone() + "!")`, comment rewritten to the Phase 39 treatment; the literal-pair assertion `("a".to_string() + "b")` in the same test is unchanged. (C-2) `rust_concat_string_rhs_is_borrowed_and_runs`: the byte assertion `((name + ": ") + &ty)` → `((name.clone() + ": ") + &ty)`; its compile-and-run leg is unchanged in behavior (the clone is value-transparent) and stays as the leg proving it. NOT re-pins, verified by grep over the whole test module: the wrap_vec assertions `(("Vec<".to_string() + &inner) + ">")` at both sites (the variable sits on the RIGHT, borrowed, unchanged), the nested-right assertion `("x".to_string() + &("y".to_string() + &s))` (ditto), the direct `concat_owned_operand` literal test (the literal arm, unchanged), and every Phase 29 compile leg.
>
> **(iii) engine e2e (src/synthesis/engine.rs) — 1 prose-only.** (E-1) The `concat_equation_seed_is_verified_and_wins` doc comment's Phase 29 update paragraph states exclaim's rendering is unchanged (`(inner + "!")`) → prose amended with a Phase 39 update paragraph (exclaim renders `(inner.clone() + "!")`; both compile legs unchanged — the test asserts bytes only for wrap_vec, whose variable is right-side, and compilation for exclaim, which the clone preserves). No assertion changes.
>
> **(iv) codegen.morph specs — 2 re-pins.** (S-1) `rust_concat_left_owned`: the decision "the left operand takes the ownership treatment" is pinned true EXACTLY at (kind 9, lit 3) → the Var kind (10) joins the owned set: owned ⇔ (kind==9 ∧ lit==3) ∨ kind==10 (a variable's clone is its owned form, as the literal's `.to_string()` is its); constraints amended to the rows pinning exactly this (total over Int×Int, as now); doc rewritten to the two-shape treatment. Its seed (pair_guard_table over (kind, lit)) changes accordingly — see F-1. (S-2) `rust_concat_left_text`: doc only — its "otherwise the operand's own rendering, unchanged" stays true of the owned==false branch, but the owned_text input's provenance is restated: it is the operand's OWNED FORM, formed per shape — the literal's owned template (`rust_string_literal_owned_text`) or the variable's clone text (the name under slice 4's Clone template, `rust_unary_text` op 9) — both arriving pre-rendered, as the representation rule provides; the spec's constraints (a pure selector over the two pre-rendered texts) are UNCHANGED. NOT re-pins: the four statement-template specs themselves (they pin Morphic-level `++` equations — IR semantics, which have no moves; the defect is below them), `rust_binop_concat_text` (its equation is over the two treated texts; the treatments are S-1/S-2's), `rust_unary_text` (op 9 already pins the clone template the fix reuses), and every other spec (grep-verified: no other spec states the left-operand treatment).

> **E0382 fixed (2026-11-21, Phase 39, branch `fix/codegen-e0382`) — the emitter freeze is amended a second time: the emitter is frozen EXCEPT the Phase 29 three fixes AND this E0382 treatment, which the user approved on 2026-11-21, scoped to exactly the defect measured in Phase 36 (Finding 2).** Work order mirrored Phase 29: the re-pin inventory above (with its one recorded extension, F-2) committed before any behavior change; the fix test-first in one commit; the re-pins as their own commit; re-measurement; this record. **Rendering-path trace (inventory step (i)):** a winner file is rendered by `generate_rust` — the signature renders parameter types via `ty_to_rust` (the only place a parameter's String type materializes), the body via the statement/tail walkers down to `ir_to_rust_tail`, whose expression positions route through `ir_to_rust_expr_typed` — which rewrites ONLY a bare string literal under a String expectation — and otherwise delegate to the UNTYPED `ir_to_rust_expr`, whose Concat arm renders `({} + {})` over `concat_owned_operand` + `concat_borrowed_operand`. No type environment exists at the Concat site, so any fix there is necessarily shape-based; the four failing bodies are statement-walker templates whose `++` chains repeat one String parameter (`pad` 2× in hole_text, 3× in while_text with `inv`, `pad` 2× in for_text, `pad_inner` 2× in match_arm_text), and the first `+` in each chain moves the variable the rest of the chain (and, in while_text, the invariant argument after it) still borrows. **The fix** (one arm in `concat_owned_operand`, src/codegen/mod.rs): a variable in Concat left position renders `<name>.clone()` — the owned-copy treatment, the literal treatment's own analogue (`.clone()` is also the emitter's Clone-op rendering); the literal arm and every other shape are unchanged. **Evidence, measured against rustc 1.100.0 before choosing:** the borrow-based alternatives are dead — `&String + &str` and `&String + &String` have NO `Add` impl (E0369; string concatenation requires an owned String on the left), so no borrow rule can fix the shape; and move legality depends on uses ANYWHERE later on the execution path, which no operand-local rendering rule can see, so single-use variable operands take the clone too — a byte change to renderings that compiled before, recorded in the inventory as the unfreeze's explicit scope flag (the alternative, whole-body liveness threaded through every walker, is not a rendering-layer fix and would break the compositional spec/mirror structure; clone is value-transparent because the IR is pure, so no executed behavior changes anywhere — the M2/M3 executed agreement below is the proof). > **Slice 6 specs (2026-11-21, Phase 40, branch `feat/m3-slice6-specs`) — the driver, specced in full in one phase (no 6a/6b split):** `generate` (mod.rs:12–22) and `generate_rust` (mod.rs:66–155) were read end to end; the driver is small and closed, so the whole assembly layer is specced now — 6 specs appended to codegen.morph (module now **112 specs, 174 in-spec tests**), mirrors + golden in tests/m3_proof.rs (71 tests, +8), including `generate_rust_from_spec_assembled`, a whole-file assembly over a raw spec tree (the slice-3 parts-level assembly stays as the parts-level witness). **The assembly, enumerated:** one candidate produces exactly ONE file holding exactly ONE function — there is no multi-function or module-level assembly anywhere in the driver. Sections, in emission order: (1) the doc header, present iff `spec.doc` is Some — each line of the doc (split by `str::lines`) rendered by the doc-line piece, then ONE blank line; (2) the precondition block, present iff the precondition list is non-empty, emitted BEFORE the signature as a free-standing module-level block (the placement slice 3 recorded as surprise 13, now pinned as a file-order fact); (3) the signature; (4) the body, rendered by the tail walker at indent 1; (5) the function's closing brace; (6) the test module, present iff the test list is non-empty. Joins: generics, params, precondition asserts and test cases concatenate with NO separator beyond what the pieces carry; the ", " inside generics/params is the pieces' own business (slice 3). **No import is ever generated at file level** — the only `use` in any output file is the test module's inner `use super::*;` (a test-module piece). **The six specs:** `rust_target_route` (String → Int: `generate`'s dispatch as String-equality guard rows — 0 rust, 1 c, 2 wasm, 3 python, 4 javascript with "javascript" and "js" as two rows onto one code, 5 ir, 6 the unsupported fallback as the catch-all row; the match is exact and case-sensitive, so "Rust", "rust " and "" are all unsupported); `rust_target_result_form` (Int → Int: routes 0..=5 → 0 Ok, 6 → 1 Err; route codes outside 0..=6 unpinned — they never occur); `rust_unsupported_target_text` (String → String: one `++` equation, the Display text of the module's only error, the name carried verbatim); `rust_doc_header_text` (String → String: one `++` equation — the pre-joined doc lines plus the blank line, which is rust_file_fixed_text piece 1); `rust_driver_section_text` (Bool × String → String: the driver's three presence branches as one guarded selection — present → the section text, absent → ""; one spec serves all three sites, the rust_block_text precedent; what "present" means differs per site and is stated in the doc: Some-ness for the doc, non-emptiness for preconditions and tests); `rust_file_text` (String ×5 → String: the whole file as one `++` equation over the five pre-rendered sections, the closing brace inline as the literal "}\n" with rust_file_fixed_text piece 0 cited — a literal over a literal-arg call, because a spec call's argument must be a parameter or an indexed spec's output). **Surprises in the real driver:** (i) `generate_rust` has NO error path — it returns Ok unconditionally; the module's only error is dispatch-level (an unknown target name in `generate`), so route 0 needs no error handling in an assembled codegen. (ii) Presence is Option-ness, not non-emptiness: `doc = Some("")` splits into ZERO lines and the file still gains the blank line — an assembled file can start with a bare "\n" (pinned end-to-end in golden). (iii) The driver seeds the tail walker's expectation itself: the body call passes the function's return type as fn_ret AND as the Some expectation — the one expectation site NOT in `rust_expectation_source`'s nine-site table (that table covers the walkers' internal recursion); pinned in rust_file_text's doc, since the body arrives pre-rendered. (iv) The generated tests' inputs are SPEC Exprs rendered by `expr_to_rust` — the weaker spec-AST emitter (slice 2), not the IR walker — so the test module never benefits from the IR side's ownership treatment; that is the structural root of the recorded E0308 placement. (v) `debug_ir_string` (the "ir" route's renderer) is the one driver-adjacent helper whose internals no spec can state: its Hole arm renders the hole's id in decimal (F2), its Literal arm is Rust's `{:?}` Debug formatting of IR values, and its fallback arm is a discriminant's Debug text — only the route is pinned; the helper stays mirror-only in full. **Mirror-only, stated:** all iteration (doc lines, the generics/params/precondition/test joins), the default test name's text (F2, slice 3), and debug_ir_string. The other targets' generators stay out of scope (the ROAD's one-target rule) — only their routes are pinned. **Facts the gate phase will need:** the test-placement E0308 does NOT affect byte-identity — the gate compares TEXT against the original emitter, which shares this driver and produces the same placement; and the assembled codegen needs only LIB compilation (the probe's `rustc --crate-type lib` check), not `rustc --test` — the cfg(test) module is part of the compared byte stream but is never compiled by the gate. What the gate must still decide is corpus + assembly mechanics (which candidates exercise the driver, and how the 112 winners compose into a codegen), not any driver behavior — the driver's behavior is now fully pinned. **Seed predictions for the measurement phase:** the three pure `++` equations (rust_file_text at arity 5, rust_doc_header_text, rust_unsupported_target_text) → the equation family, on the slice-3/5 evidence at arities 1–4 (arity 5 is new but the shape is identical); rust_target_result_form → guard_table (a single-Int-key literal-pin table, the commonest shape in the module); rust_driver_section_text → the composition precedence (a Bool-parameter guard with a bare-Var pin and a literal pin at arity 2 — guarded identity refuses it, its guards are literal Int rows; the rust_break_text / Phase 34 shape); **rust_target_route → seedless BY DESIGN** — its guards are String equalities, the rust_ident_empty family, recorded as unadmitted since Phase 24; it is the expected "next gap" only in the sense that it completes the pair of String-guard specs, and on the rust_keyword evidence (a 51-way String-equality disjunction encodes and verifies) it should still crown a deterministic Verified pool winner the way rust_ident_empty does. Diagnostic scoping follows the Phase 30/35 precedent: the seed-shape diagnostic counts 112 with the RECOGNIZED set (93) and the slices 1–5 sweep tables untouched. `morphic check` passes on the extended file.
