> **M3 GATE CLOSED (2026-11-21, Phase 42, branch `feat/m3-gate`) — the synthesized codegen produces byte-identical Rust output to the original emitter over the gate corpus, and the M3 checkboxes above are ticked on this measurement.** **The gate harness** (`src/codegen/m3_gate.rs`, test-only, declared inside the codegen module so the original side of every comparison is the emitter's own private functions, not a copy): the fixed winner set is extracted through the real pipeline (`synthesize_with_module` → `Verifier::with_specs` → `select_best` → `generate`) TWICE and asserted identical body-for-body and provenance-for-provenance across the two passes — **110 constraint seeds** (equation 54, guard_table 39, composition_guard 7, pair_guard_table 4, expr_pin_guard 3, guarded_identity 1, multi_guard 1, mixed_pair_guard_table 1) **plus the two by-design seedless pool winners** `rust_ident_empty` and `rust_target_route` (String-equality guards, the unadmitted family recorded since Phase 24; Mutation provenance, Z3-Verified, tpr 1.000), reproducing the Phase 41 pre-gate state exactly; the two pool winners are pinned by the same determinism the measurements established — route's winner is the unique Verified candidate in its pool, and the harness asserts the extracted set's composition (110 + exactly those two names) before judging anything. The winners compose through the `tests/m3_proof.rs` assembly structure with every spec call resolving to the WINNER body, executed on the real IR interpreter over call-resolved bodies (a gate-local resolver with the engine resolver's capture-refusal discipline, cycle guard and depth cap 8 — the engine's own resolver targets seed bodies, so it cannot be reused for winners); the census it reports is itself a finding: **the 112 winner bodies contain ZERO spec calls and resolution changes no body** — every winner is self-contained, the same fact Phase 41's 112/112 standalone compiles implied. The only glue is what the assemblies themselves carry: the recursion, the ", " joins, `rust_ident`, numeric Display, `escape_rust_char`, pad repetition, the indexed test-name default, and the constraint catch-all's constant `"true"`. **The artifact:** all 112 winners rendered through the original `generate`, each wrapped in its own module (so the per-file `mod tests` cannot collide), form one 123,741-byte Rust file that compiles as a lib crate (`rustc --edition 2021 --crate-type lib`) — the artifact whose behavior the gate judges. **The corpus — 2,527 byte-exact comparisons:** (i) the proof assemblies' golden trees transcribed into native types — 121 cases (types 16, patterns 14, spec exprs 14, constraints 14, expressions 36, statements 19, tails 8), each first asserted to reproduce its proof golden ON THE ORIGINAL SIDE so a transcription slip stops the gate loudly (0 transcription failures; the one untranscribable case, the proof's opaque `BlockText("let x = 1; x")`, has no faithful IRNode preimage and is excluded by name); (ii) the 4 slice-6 DriverSpec cases end-to-end as whole files through the composed driver vs `generate`; (iii) a 17-name target-dispatch battery (route + result form + the verbatim `Unsupported target language: …` error text; the `"ir"` route compared for Ok-ness only — its renderer is unstatable, Phase 40); (iv) all 126 winner files end-to-end (112 M3 + the 14 M2 validator/printer winners); (v) every winner body and its unique subtrees — 753 trees, each rendered three ways (expression / statement / tail position), 2,259 cases: the real candidates this emitter exists to render. **Two composition modes**, differing only at the two string-literal call sites: strict composition (the winners receive raw literal contents) matches 2,190/2,527; mirror-completed composition — the mirror layer supplies exactly the one transformation finding F5 records the spec language cannot state, `escape_rust_str` on literal contents, while the winners still decide every wrap/ownership byte — matches **2,527/2,527**, and every one of the 337 strict-mode divergences is mechanically reconciled by the completion (0 unreconciled, 0 execution failures): the strict run proves the divergences are exactly the recorded F5 class and nothing else. **One glue correction during the run, recorded:** the first gate run measured 2,525/2,527, failing only the Order GreaterThan/GreaterThanOrEqual cases — the harness's constraint glue consulted `rust_constraint_fixed_text` under the Order kind, whose table has no Order row (it is keyed by the catch-all constraint kinds); the assembly itself renders the catch-all constant `"true"` as glue (the original's wildcard arm), the glue now follows the assembly verbatim, and a mechanics test pins it. The owning layer was the harness's glue — not a winner body, not the corpus, not the emitter. **Scope, honestly:** byte-identity is proven over this corpus — the proof trees, the whole driver and dispatch surface, and every tree the 126 real winners contain — with the mirrors' executed totals (golden 337/337 + pinned 2962/2962, Phase 41) as the wider value-level evidence beneath it; it is not a proof over all possible IR trees, and the composed codegen inherits the recorded mirror-layer completions (F5's per-character transformations incl. escaping, the ", " joins, numeric Display) exactly where the specs declare themselves silent. **The freeze's final state:** the emitter stands frozen except the two user-approved scoped unfreezes (Phase 29's three rendering fixes; Phase 39's E0382 treatment) — the gate needed no third.
