> **M5-S2 landed (2026-11-21, Phase 52, branch `feat/m5-s2-switch`) — the switch is wired and checkbox 2 is ticked; the roundtrip's byte leg holds (4/4, both configurations) and its behavioral leg fails on the original compiler's own control draws, so checkbox 3 is NOT ticked.** **(1) The switch.** `generate`'s "rust" arm now calls `generate_rust_route` (`src/codegen/mod.rs`): under `#[cfg(feature = "bootstrap-codegen")]` it renders through `bootstrap_adapter::render_rust_synthesized`; otherwise through the original `generate_rust`. Every other route (c / wasm / python / javascript / js / ir / unsupported) is the pre-M5 code in both configurations, and the frozen emitter's functions are unmodified — the dispatch gains the route function, and `generate_rust` gains one `#[cfg_attr(feature = "bootstrap-codegen", allow(dead_code))]`: in the bootstrap configuration the original emitter is kept-but-unselected by design (the ROAD anti-pattern rule), and the annotation roots its call chain so that configuration gains no new dead-code warnings. This resolves the S1 deviation exactly as recorded there — the cfg lives at the `generate` dispatch. A mechanics test (`m5_s2_dispatch_routing`, normal suite) pins the routing in BOTH configurations (feature off: `generate`'s rust output is the original's; feature on: the adapter's) and pins the other routes plus the unsupported-target error as configuration-independent. **Suites: default 368 passed / 0 failed / 16 ignored (367 baseline + the mechanics test; +2 ignored roundtrip instruments); `--features bootstrap-codegen` IDENTICAL — 368 / 0 / 16.** No test needed a cfg-gated assertion under the flag: every pinned rust rendering in the suite lies inside the composition's proven behavior, so the feature-on suite passes through the adapter unchanged. **(2) Roundtrip (a) — the byte leg HOLDS** (`m5_s2_roundtrip_extract_once`, ignored, report at `target/m5-s2/roundtrip-report.md`, run in BOTH configurations): sort, binary_search, is_sorted, count_matching — one pipeline extraction each (WinnerCache discipline; pipeline runs exactly 4), the single winner rendered by `generate_rust`, by `bootstrap_adapter::generate_synthesized` (exactly what the flag-on dispatch calls), and by `generate` itself, asserted to follow the compiled configuration's route: **4/4 byte-identical in both configurations**, and the two runs drew genuinely different winners (distinct body hashes per file per run), so the identity holds per drawn winner, not per fixed set. **(3) Roundtrip (b) — the behavioral leg FAILS its every-draw criteria, on the control identically** (`m5_s2_roundtrip_behavioral`, ignored, report at `target/m5-s2/behavioral-report.md`): both binaries built from this tree (nested `cargo build`, the e2e CLI precedent; the default binary restored afterwards), each building `sort.morph` at `-O 1`, five draws. **End-to-end builds 5/5 in both configurations; distinct outputs 5/5 in both; whole-file rustc compile 0/5 in BOTH; winner-function compile 4/5 flag-off and 5/5 flag-on; executed in-spec tests 0/5 in BOTH.** The failures are configuration-independent, and both causes are pre-existing properties of the ORIGINAL compiler, sitting entirely outside the stage M5 switches: **(i) sort's winners are not sorts.** `evaluate_candidate` (`src/synthesis/engine.rs`) computes test_pass_ratio by executing spec tests through the IR interpreter, but for multi-param specs the tuple test inputs cannot be bound, so `run.evaluable == 0` and its recorded FALLBACK scores EVERY candidate tpr 1.0 ("tests assumed passed"); selection then ranks on complexity/quality and crowns trivial bodies. Every sort draw measured this phase (16 across a manual probe and the gate run's 2×5) returns the input unchanged or `vec![]`, and 0 pass the six in-spec tests executed through a hand-written runner (the cross-validation harness pattern). **(ii) the produced file does not parse.** `generate_rust`'s generated test module renders sort's tuple input as `__tuple__(...)` and the fn-typed cmp argument as `??` (the `expr_to_rust` "??" fallback, a recorded backlog item) — a parse-level error no cfg strips; the winner function alone parses and compiles, modulo the draw-dependent E0689 junk class (one flag-off draw in the gate run carried `1.abs().abs()` on an ambiguous numeric literal — the recorded execution-hazard family; a flag-off draw in an earlier aborted run drew the same class). The adapter inherits both behaviors byte-identically — that inheritance is exactly what (a) and S1 prove — so the flag-on binary's draws are the flag-off binary's draws in character: **the bootstrap claim (the synthesized codegen changes nothing) holds; the audit's leg-(3) absolute criteria (every drawn output compiles and passes sort's tests) do not hold for the original compiler itself and cannot be discharged by codegen-stage work.** Checkbox 3 therefore stays open, with its remaining blockers recorded precisely: a truth signal for multi-param specs in candidate evaluation (binding tuple test inputs in `eval`, or an equivalent), and test-value rendering for tuple/fn-typed test inputs in the emitter — each its own project, outside M5's switch. A binary-level flag-on/flag-off byte comparison on a fixed winner remains impossible (each binary synthesizes its own winner) and was not faked: (a) covers the byte claim at the dispatch level, (b) the binary level behaviorally. **Checkbox 2 is ticked** under the audit's definition and owner decision D2 (accepted 2026-11-21): the flag-on build IS the new binary — the original compiler with the checked-in synthesized Codegen artifact (S1) seated in the Rust emitter's place through the compiled adapter, the original kept and switchable (D4). **Blast radius:** M2 probe + cross-validation one pass unchanged (every golden/pinned fraction full — 64/64 + 539/539 per run); M3 probe one pass unchanged (seeds 110/112, ConstraintInduced winners 110/112, Verified winners 112/112, compiles 112/112 per run); M3 cross-validation one pass unchanged (golden 337/337 + pinned 2962/2962 per run, 0 exec failures); the M5-S1 proofs re-run green (artifact byte identity PASS; adapter equivalence 2,527/2,527 three-way + 138/138 three-way). The seed-body golden fixture is untouched; no change to synthesis, verifier, or parser. **Next: M5-S3** (the held-out component, checkbox 1) and **M5-S4** (the SC3 measurement + gate closing, checkbox 4); checkbox 3's blockers are recorded above for the owner.

> **Phase 53 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/eval-tuple-binding`) — roundtrip blocker 1 FIXED: tuple test inputs bind elementwise in candidate evaluation; checkbox 3 stays open on blocker 2 and a measured generation gap.** The owner chose to fix the blockers (2026-11-21). **Diagnosis (code-cited):** the site is `bind_test_env` (`src/synthesis/eval.rs`), the only binding path under `run_spec_tests`, whose only production caller is `evaluate_candidate` (`src/synthesis/engine.rs`). It mapped a test's input as ONE value via `value_of_expr`, then bound a single parameter directly or, for multiple parameters, required a `Value::List` of exactly the parameter count. The parser lowers a tuple input `(v1, v2, ...)` to `Expr::Call("__tuple__", [v1, ...])` and a list literal to `Call("List", ...)` (parser.rs); `value_of_expr`'s catch-all lowers the tuple through `expr_to_ir` to `IRNode::Call { function: "__tuple__" }` (any lambda element to a Hole), and the interpreter's `eval_call` knows only `"List"` and `"len"` — so a tuple input NEVER mapped, every tuple test was excluded, `evaluable == 0`, and the FALLBACK scored every candidate tpr 1.0. **Inventory of test-input shapes (the blast radius):** tuple-form in-spec tests exist in exactly three specs — `count_matching` (2 params, 3 tests `([..], 5)`, all elements first-order), `sort` (2 params, 6 tests `([..], |a, b| a < b)`), `binary_search` (3 params, 7 tests `([..], n, |a, b| a - b)`); no nested tuples or list-of-tuples anywhere; every multi-param M2/M3 spec carries NO in-spec tests (golden in the proof files, precisely because tuple inputs could not bind), and all other tested specs are single-param. **The fix (minimal, in `bind_test_env` only; no evaluator redesign, no selection/verifier change):** before the whole-value mapping, a tuple input whose arity equals the parameter count binds elementwise — each element through the same `value_of_expr` (literals and composite literals alike) — and any element that cannot map excludes the test, exactly as before (no partial binding, no guessing). **Admitted:** flat tuples of first-order values. **Still excluded, recorded by shape:** tuples carrying a lambda for a fn-typed parameter (the evaluator's `Value` has no function variant — sort's and binary_search's tests therefore STAY unbindable and in the FALLBACK's domain), nested tuples (no tuple Value), arity mismatches. The FALLBACK's legitimate domain is now exactly that residue plus other unmappable encodings; specs with no tests keep the neutral 0.5; single-param and List-form bindings are byte-identical. **Tests first:** the discrimination pins FAILED pre-fix and pass now — at `run_spec_tests` level (tuple spec: correct body evaluable 4/passed 4, trivial body 4/1; pre-fix both evaluable 0) and at `evaluate_candidate` level (correct tpr 1.0 earned, trivial 1/3; pre-fix both 1.0 via FALLBACK); the preservation pins (lambda tuple excluded, arity mismatch excluded, nested tuple excluded, List-form binding unchanged, unmappable-tests spec keeps FALLBACK 1.0) passed before and after. **Measured consequence** (`src/phase53_measurement.rs`, ignored, report at `target/phase53/measurement-report.md`; vehicles: the interpreter where tests bind, compiled runners — winner function + hand-written runner main — as the behavioral judge): **count_matching** (6 pipeline draws) — pool tpr now SPREADS 0.000–0.333 in every draw (was uniform FALLBACK 1.0), every crown sits at the measured pool ceiling (tpr 0.333, interpreter passed 1/3), and the interpreter and compiled vehicles agree on every crown's pass count; but **0/6 crowns pass 3/3**, and pool scans (2 draws, 44 distinct bodies, 31 function-compiling) find **no body passing 3/3 under the compiled vehicle either** — the remaining count_matching problem is a generation gap at opt 1, not a signal gap. **Sort** (12 draws) — unchanged by design (evaluable 0 asserted per draw): crowns all Mutation random-seed at FALLBACK tpr 1.000, function compiles 10/12, best crown 3/6 executed, **0/12 pass 6/6**; pool scans (3 draws, 44 distinct bodies, 31 compiling) find **0 bodies passing 6/6** — so even a hypothetical binding for lambda-carrying inputs would have no correct sort body to reward in these pools: generation at opt 1 does not produce one (cf. the recorded generation limits), and the interpreter's expression subset could not execute an iterative/higher-order sort body in any case. **Checkbox 3 status:** blocker 1 is discharged as a signal defect — where tests can bind, tpr is now measured truth. The checkbox itself stays open: its behavioral leg still requires blocker 2 (test-value rendering for tuple/fn-typed inputs in the emitter, untouched here) and, for sort specifically, a generator that produces a passing body and an evaluation path for fn-typed test inputs — both recorded as separate projects, not forced in this phase (no selection changes, no template additions). **Blast radius:** full suite **376 passed / 0 failed / 17 ignored** (368 baseline + 8 new tests; the measurement is the 17th ignored); M2 probe one pass unchanged (14/14 Verified ConstraintInduced winners; the two test-less specs at their neutral 0.5); M2 cross-validation one pass unchanged (golden **64/64** + pinned **539/539** per run, all rows full agreement); M3 probe one pass unchanged (seeds **110/112**, Verified winners **112/112**, compiles **112/112** per run); M3 cross-validation one pass unchanged (golden **337/337** + pinned **2962/2962** per run, 0 exec failures); M5-S2 byte roundtrip (a) re-run PASS (4/4, render-level as expected); `--features bootstrap-codegen` builds and its dispatch test passes; the seed-body golden fixture is untouched; no change to selection criteria, the refutation gate, the verifier, or the parser.

> **Phase 54 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/test-value-rendering`) — roundtrip blocker 2 DIAGNOSED and STOPPED at the territory gate: the broken test-value rendering is inside the Codegen specs' synthesized domain, so no unilateral emitter fix was made; blocker 2 is reclassified as a spec-amendment + re-synthesis project (path recorded below). Checkbox 3 stays open. No production code changed this phase (diagnosis instruments were temporary and removed; the suite stands at the Phase 53 baseline, 376 passed / 0 failed / 17 ignored, and the `bootstrap-codegen` dispatch test passes under the feature).** **Diagnosis (code-cited).** The generated test module is emitted by `generate_rust`'s test loop (`src/codegen/mod.rs:156-179`), which renders `test.input` and `test.expected_output` through `expr_to_rust` (`src/codegen/mod.rs:1382-1408`): its Call arm formats `name(args)` verbatim, its BinOp arm maps only Add/Sub/Mul/Div/Eq/Neq/And/Or and renders every other operator as `?`, and its catch-all renders `??`. The parser lowers a tuple input to `Call("__tuple__", ...)` and a list literal to `Call("List", ...)` (parser.rs:700), so today's module for sort reads `sort(__tuple__(List(5), ??))` (real binary, `-O 1`). **Full inventory (mechanical walk of every in-spec test, input + expected, all 8 example files; 245 tests):** the broken shapes present are exactly Lambda ×13 (sort 6, binary_search 7 — rendered `??`, a parse error), BinOp(Lt) ×6 (sort's lambda bodies — rendered `(a ? b)`, a parse error), `Call("__tuple__")` ×16 (E0425 + E0061: the tuple reaches the call as one argument), `Call("List")` ×27 (E0425: rendered `List(...)` for a `vec!` value), and `Call("None")` ×4 (binary_search's expects — rendered `None()`, E0618); every other shape renders correctly (IntLit 371, StringLit 141, BoolLit 50, CharLit 9, `Call("Some")` ×3, BinOp(Sub) ×7), and FloatLit / Field / Index / UnaryOp / Length / Comprehension / nested tuples occur NOWHERE in test position. Affected specs: exactly sort, binary_search, count_matching, is_sorted (21 tests). Per-file rustc catalog (one `-O 1` draw each): sort 18 errors (6 parse `?`, 6 E0425 `List`, 6 E0061), binary_search 1 parse error (below), count_matching 9 (3× E0425 `__tuple__`, 3× E0425 `List`, 3× E0061), is_sorted 5 (E0425 `List`). **Failing pins first** (temporary instrument, run and removed): `expr_to_rust` of sort's lambda returns `"??"` against the desired `"|a, b| (a < b)"`; `expr_to_rust` of `a < b` returns `"(a ? b)"`; sort's generated test module contains `__tuple__` — all three pins failed as designed. **Territory verdict: INSIDE the synthesized domain — there is no shared hand-written site to fix once.** The test module is produced by three parallel implementations, byte-equal by gate proof, not by shared code: the original (`generate_rust` + `expr_to_rust`), the proof assembly (`expr_to_rust_assembled` + `generate_rust_from_spec_assembled`, tests/m3_proof.rs:896-921, 2328-2401), and the compiled adapter (`driver_file`'s test-module section + `sexpr_text`, bootstrap_adapter.rs:1071-1096, 417-458; the interpreter composition in m3_gate.rs:1045, 1061 calls the same winners by name). Per shape: **(1) Lambda → `??` is winner-pinned twice** — `rust_expr_form` (codegen.morph:597) classifies kind 10 (Lambda) to family 6 (fallback), and `rust_expr_fixed_text` (codegen.morph:625) pins kind 10 → `"??"`; the checked-in artifact's winner returns `"??"` at kind 10 verbatim (synthesized_codegen.rs:800-826) and both specs' seed bodies sit in the golden fixture (tests/seed_body_golden.txt:34-35). A closure is structural, not fixed text: rendering one needs a NEW template family in the walk (slice-2-style spec surface) and a form-table reclassification — spec amendments, not a patch. **(2) BinOp(Lt) → `?` is winner-pinned** — `spec_binop_symbol` (codegen.morph:300) pins op 9 → `"?"` (rows 4, 9-13 likewise: Mod/Lt/Le/Gt/Ge/Concat), and its doc states the reservation verbatim: the '?' rows "are pinned deliberately: a synthesized replacement must reproduce today's bytes, fallback included. **Fixing the fallback is a separate later decision**" (seed fixture line 22). **(3) The Call-arm shapes (`__tuple__` / `List` / `None`) are glue in all three implementations** — no winner computes call text (`rust_expr_form` family 4 only classifies) — but they cannot discharge the blocker: sort's and binary_search's tuples CONTAIN shapes (1) and (2), and the walker's specced description (family 4: "the name, then '(' + args joined ', ' + ')'") states the un-special-cased template as the walker's contract, so a glue-only special case would falsify the standing specs while their classification stands. **Additional finding, same territory (driver level, recorded because it independently blocks binary_search's parse leg):** the precondition block is emitted as a free-standing `#[cfg(debug_assertions)] { ... }` block at module level (mod.rs:126-136) — rustc: "expected item after attributes", the file's FIRST parse error, before any test value is reached. The placement is specced, not incidental: `rust_file_text`'s doc (codegen.morph:1949) records it verbatim ("emitted BEFORE the signature, as a free-standing block at module level ... slice 3 recorded the placement surprise") and its equation pins the section order; the block text is winner `rust_precondition_block`. **Desync consequence of fixing unilaterally (why the phase stops):** patching `expr_to_rust` alone makes the original diverge from the composed/adapter output on sort + binary_search whole files — breaking the M4 gate corpus (138 specs, both files in it), M5-S1 equivalence Part B (138/138 three-way) and M5-S2 roundtrip (a) (4/4) — while three Codegen specs' stated contracts, the seed fixture, and the artifact keep implementing the old tables: the specs would lie about the emitter they specify. Patching all three implementations without the specs keeps byte-equality but breaks the M3 premise silently (specs state the emitter COMPLETE) and the next artifact regeneration from specs resurrects the broken bytes. Either way the artifact's winners for the amended tables must be re-synthesized — and per this phase's mandate, a fix requiring artifact regeneration stops here. **The required re-synthesis path (the honest form of blocker 2, in order):** (i) spec amendments in `examples/self_host/codegen.morph`, owner-visible because they change the emitter's contract — `spec_binop_symbol`: give the six '?' rows real symbols (Mod/Lt/Le/Gt/Ge, and an explicit Concat decision), discharging the spec's own recorded reservation; `rust_expr_form` + a new closure-template spec (slice-2 style, over pre-rendered param names + body): reclassify kind 10 from family 6 to the new family, and `rust_expr_fixed_text` loses the kind-10 row; the Call pseudo-functions stated as walker/driver rules (List literal → `vec![...]`; a tuple in test-INPUT position spreads positionally into the `rust_test_case` call — the driver owns placement — while a tuple in expect position renders as a Rust tuple; zero-arg constructors render bare, `None` not `None()`); the precondition block's placement amended in `rust_file_text` / `rust_precondition_block` (asserts move inside the function, or the free-standing block goes) so binary_search's file can parse at all. (ii) Re-synthesize the amended/new specs (seed bodies re-derive from the amended tables; the golden fixture updates through its own discipline), re-run the M3 gate + M4 gate corpus under the NEW behavior, regenerate the artifact and re-prove M5-S1 byte-identity + equivalence and M5-S2 roundtrip (a). (iii) Only then do this phase's deferred steps run: the build measurements (whole-file compile of the generated modules; the winner-function E0689 junk class reported separately, as before) and the Phase 52 behavioral roundtrip re-run. **What remains between here and checkbox 3, precisely:** blocker 2 as reclassified above (a spec-amendment + re-synthesis project, est. one slice for the amendments + one for re-synthesis/re-proof), and Phase 53's residue unchanged — the generation gap at opt 1 (no fully-passing sort/count_matching body in scanned pools) and the absence of an evaluation path for fn-typed test inputs (the evaluator has no function values). Steps 4-5 of this phase's brief (build measurements, behavioral re-run) were NOT run: with no production change they would reproduce Phase 52's table byte-for-byte in character, and their vehicle (the generated test module) is exactly what cannot be fixed unilaterally. **Blast radius:** none — no production, spec, fixture, or artifact change; full suite **376 passed / 0 failed / 17 ignored** (the Phase 53 baseline, re-verified after the temporary instruments were removed); `cargo test --features bootstrap-codegen` dispatch test passes; seed-body golden fixture untouched.

> **Phase 55 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/spec-amendments-test-values`) — blocker 2's proper fix, part 1: the Codegen SPEC AMENDMENTS landed with lockstep emitter changes and full re-measurement; the gate cascade + artifact regeneration are Phase 56. Checkbox 3 stays open.** The owner approved the amendments on 2026-11-21 ("amend the specs so they stop mandating broken output"), executing step (i) and the measurement half of step (ii) of the Phase 54 record. **Amendment inventory (before → after).** (1) `spec_binop_symbol`: the six rows pinned to `"?"` (Mod/Lt/Le/Gt/Ge/Concat) now carry their real Rust symbols — Mod `"%"`, Lt `"<"`, Le `"<="`, Gt `">"`, Ge `">="`, Concat `"+"` (sharing Add's symbol exactly as the IR table's Concat row does, `rust_binop_symbol` op 16; honest in this walker too because `expr_to_rust` applies no operand-ownership treatment anywhere — a StringLit renders as a bare quoted literal in every position, the recorded placement property — so the table states the operator's symbol and nothing more). The doc's reservation ("Fixing the fallback is a separate later decision") is discharged in the doc itself, dated 2026-11-21; the in-spec tests `mod-fallback`/`lt-fallback` are renamed `mod-symbol`/`lt-symbol` with the new expectations, plus a new `concat-symbol` test. (2) Closure rendering: `rust_expr_form` kind 10 (Lambda) moves from fallback family 6 to **new family 7** (closure template); `rust_expr_fixed_text` **loses its kind-10 row** (its pinned total drops 7 → 6; the kind-10 sweep case moves from pinned to spec-silent); new template spec **`rust_expr_lambda_text(params, body)`**: `output == "|" ++ params ++ "| " ++ body` — the slice-2 template shape over pre-rendered parts (params pre-joined names, body rendered by the walker itself), so sort's comparator renders `|a, b| (a < b)`. FloatLit (2) and UnaryOp (9) keep family 6 — deliberately untouched: they occur nowhere in test position (Phase 54 inventory) and the amendment's scope is the measured breakage. (3) Call pseudo-functions: the rules are **stated as specs**, not left as glue — new classification **`rust_expr_call_form(name)`** in the exact `rust_target_route` shape (String-equality guard rows + catch-all): `List` → 1 (vec literal), `__tuple__` → 2 (tuple), `None` → 3 (bare constructor), everything else → 0 (plain call template; `Some(x)` keeps the call form, correctly); new templates **`rust_expr_list_text(args)`** (`"vec![" ++ args ++ "]"`) and **`rust_expr_tuple_text(args)`** (`"(" ++ args ++ ")"`) for the value positions. The one irreducibly positional rule — a tuple in test-INPUT position spreads into the generated call — is driver glue by nature (it decides what text occupies the call's argument slot, not how a value renders) and is recorded in `rust_test_case`'s doc: spread iff the input is `Call("__tuple__", args)` with `args.len() == params.len()` (Phase 53's binding rule); a tuple in EXPECT position renders via `rust_expr_tuple_text`. Evidence for the spec placement: the classification + both templates are pure functions of data the walker already holds (the call's name, the pre-rendered args), statable in the module's established shapes; only the spread depends on the driver's parameter list. (4) Precondition placement: `rust_file_text`'s equation reorders to `header ++ signature ++ pre_block ++ body ++ "}\n" ++ test_module` — the block now sits at the head of the function body, immediately after the signature, where its asserts name the parameters in scope (verified form: `#[cfg(debug_assertions)] { ... }` as a statement inside a fn body parses and runs); Call arm dispatches on the name per `rust_expr_call_form`; new Lambda arm) and **`generate_rust`** (block emission moved after the signature push; test loop gains the tuple spread) — nothing else. `tests/m3_proof.rs`: mirrors for all four amended tables + the four new specs; > **Phase 56 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/gate-cascade-amendments`) — the GATE CASCADE after the Phase 55 amendments: every gate re-proven under the new behavior; the deferred build measurements land; blockers 1+2 both discharged; checkbox 3 stays open on the generation gap + the fn-typed evaluation path alone.** The cascade ran in dependency order, PASS standards unchanged everywhere, and every count the amendments legitimately changed was re-derived and re-recorded with its derivation (the instruments' own re-audit discipline). **Step 1 — M3 gate re-run: PASS.** Winners = **113 constraint seeds + the by-design seedless trio** (`rust_expr_call_form`, `rust_ident_empty`, `rust_target_route`; seed-kind tally from the gate run: equation 57, guard_table 39, composition_guard 7, pair_guard_table 4, expr_pin_guard 3, guarded_identity 1, multi_guard 1, mixed_pair_guard_table 1). The gate's proof expectations were transcribed from the proof assembly's own goldens (`(1 % 2)`, `("a" + "b")`, `vec![1, 2]`, `(1, x)`, `None`, `|a, b| (a < b)`, reordered full-driver bytes, the end-to-end test-value-shapes case). Raw artifact render **131,216 B** (Phase 42: 123,741 B), compiles. The gate record in `src/codegen/m3_gate.rs` is re-recorded with the derivation in its header. **Step 2 — M4 gate re-run: PASS.** Corpus re-derived per the harness's own instruction: **142 files** = codegen 116 + validator 7 + printer 7 + functional_tokenizer 8 + 4 standalone (the non-Codegen share is 26; the harness comment's older "18" was a Phase 46-era leftover, corrected in place). **Attempted 142, identical 142, divergent 0, errors 0 of every kind; pipeline runs exactly 142** (extract-once proven). `EXPECTED_CORPUS` and spec-count assertions re-recorded (112 → 116, 138 → 142). **Step 3 — artifact regeneration + adapter reconciliation.** `m5_s1_regen_artifact` (the documented command, original pipeline) regenerates `src/codegen/synthesized_codegen.rs`: **109,060 B** shipped (Phase 51: 103,183 B), 116 winner modules, header provenance re-recorded (116 winners; 113 seeds with the tally above; the seedless trio named; **58 of 116** generated test modules stripped — 55 + `rust_expr_call_form`/`rust_expr_list_text`/`rust_expr_tuple_text`; `rust_expr_lambda_text`'s doc declares no in-spec tests and its block carries none; the count is the transform's own segmentation of the raw render). The adapter's Phase 55 glue is reconciled to the regenerated composition: the Call arm dispatches through the artifact's `rust_expr_call_form` winner into `rust_expr_list_text`/`rust_expr_tuple_text` (form 3 stays the bare constructor, as in the assembly), the BinOp arm calls the artifact's amended `spec_binop_symbol` winner (glue fn `amended_spec_binop_symbol` deleted), the Lambda arm calls `rust_expr_lambda_text`; the S1 records in `src/codegen/m5_s1.rs` are re-derived to match (gate header "116 winners", corpus 2,559 / 142). **Step 4 — S1 re-proofs: PASS.** Byte identity: produced 109,060 B == checked-in 109,060 B (fresh gate-style extraction + render; regeneration idempotent). Equivalence three-way over the NEW corpora: **M3 2,559/2,559** (adapter == original == interpreter composition, every class perfect) and **M4 142/142** with exactly 142 pipeline runs. **Step 5 — S2 byte leg: PASS in both configurations** — the four standalone files extracted once each, **4/4 byte-identical** with `bootstrap-codegen` off and on (m5_s2.rs needed no count edits; it holds no corpus constants). **Step 6 — the deferred measurements (Phase 54 steps 4–5)**, new ignored instrument `phase56_build_measurements` (report at `target/phase56/build-measurements-report.md`): real-binary `-O 1` builds, 5 draws each. **Parse: 20/20** — no parse-class error in any draw of any file; the amendments' direct claim holds in full (generated test modules parse; binary_search's precondition placement parses). Per file: **sort** (6 tests) parse 5/5, whole compiles 5/5, function compiles 5/5 (no E0689 draw), test module compiles **0/5 — E0283 in every draw**: the first generated test passes an empty list whose element type nothing pins (the drawn winner's signature carries no trait bound; the closure `|a, b| (a < b)` cannot infer its parameter type from an unconstrained `fn(T, T) -> bool`) — a winner-signature/test-shape interaction in the drawn code, recorded as such; the rendered test text is exactly the spec's test. **binary_search** (7) parse 5/5, function compiles **0/5** — the crowned bodies are ill-typed generation-pool junk (E0369 `Vec<T>` arithmetic ×4 draws, the recorded E0689 class ×1), a winner-quality failure, not rendering. **count_matching** (3): compiles at every stage 5/5, test module executes, **1/3 passed every draw** — exactly Phase 53's measured pool ceiling. **is_sorted** (5): compiles at every stage 5/5, **4/5 passed every draw**. Where the modules run, the FAILURES are the honest signal working against crowns that are not correct implementations (Phase 53's generation gap), not a rendering regression; draws passing all in-spec tests: 0/20, as the gap predicts. **Behavioral roundtrip re-run** (both binaries × 5 draws of sort): builds 5/5, 5/5 distinct outputs both configurations; **whole-file compiles 4/5 (off) and 5/5 (on)** — against Phase 52's 0/5 in both; the single off failure is an E0689 junk draw, the recorded class; function compiles 4/5 (off), 5/5 (on); in-spec tests pass 0/5 in both. **Step 7 — checkbox 3: NOT ticked; the ledger, precisely.** Blocker 1 discharged (Phase 53: tuple inputs bind; the truth signal is real for first-order values). Blocker 2 discharged (Phase 55 + this cascade: files parse 20/20; test modules compile and execute wherever the crowned winner's own code compiles; the behavioral whole-file leg moved 0/5 → 4/5 + 5/5). What remains is exactly two measured things, neither a codegen-stage property: **the generation gap** (at opt 1 the pools hold no fully-correct body for sort or count_matching — crowns sit at the pools' measured ceilings — and binary_search's crowns are not even well-typed) and **the fn-typed evaluation path** (sort's and binary_search's tuple tests carry a lambda for the fn-typed parameter; the evaluator's `Value` has no function variant, so those specs' candidates still cannot be scored by their own tests). The bootstrap binary inherits both from the original compiler identically — which is what the roundtrip exists to prove. Blast radius: M2 probe + cross-validation one pass unchanged (both pass; cross-validation zero disagreements — golden 64/64 + pinned 539/539 character); the seed-body fixture is untouched (62 lines, as Phase 55 left it); the frozen emitter's functions are unmodified by this phase (the instruments, the adapter, and the regenerated artifact changed). **Suite: 378 passed / 0 failed / 18 ignored, identical under `--features bootstrap-codegen`** (ignored 17 → 18: the new measurement instrument). Remaining M5 work: S3 (held-out component, checkbox 1) and S4 (SC3 measurement + gate closing, checkbox 4); checkbox 3 additionally waits on the generation-gap / fn-typed-evaluation projects, which are engine work, not bootstrap work. Local branch only, not pushed.
> **Phase 57 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/generation-gap-audit`) — GENERATION-GAP AUDIT: why the opt-1 pools hold no correct body for the four standalone specs, what each candidate fix costs, and the campaign plan. Audit only — no production code changed; a temporary probe instrument (pool census + verdict distribution + rustc pool scan, 3 draws per spec through the real pipeline) was written, run, and removed, and its numbers are recorded here. Checkbox 3 stays open.**
> **(1) Generation inventory at opt 1 (code + probe).** The pipeline is `SynthesisEngine::run` (`src/synthesis/engine.rs:488`): seed → MCTS → GA (gated `opt_level >= 3`, engine.rs:520) → protected-seed readmission → scoring. `SynthesisConfig::from_opt_level` (engine.rs:413): opt 1 = population 20, 1,000 MCTS iterations, 5 s, `use_llm = false`; opt 2 = pop 50 / 5,000 iter / `use_llm = true`; opt 3 (the CLI default, `src/main.rs:44`, and the fallback for any other level) = pop 100 / 10,000 / `use_llm = true`; opt 4 = pop 500 / 100,000 / `use_llm = true`, min_score 0.9. `seed_population` (engine.rs:546) contributes, in order: ONE test-induced seed (a match over the test inputs with a typed wildcard hole, engine.rs:592ff); THREE template seeds whose builders are all bare `Hole`s (`build_divide_and_conquer` / `build_iterator_chain` / `build_simple_loop`, engine.rs:1893-1903 — the real template library in `src/synthesis/templates.rs` has NO production caller); constraint seeds (engine.rs:754ff); LLM seeds iff `use_llm`; then typed random bodies (`random_ir_tree_typed`, engine.rs:2271) filling to 20. The seed list is then sorted and passed through `dedup_by(ir_equivalent)` where `ir_equivalent` compares only the root variant's discriminant (engine.rs:1871) — measured consequence: the three identical template holes collapse to one, and every final pool below contains exactly ONE template candidate, always labelled divide_and_conquer. MCTS (engine.rs:869) runs two trees of 1,000 iterations — one rooted at a random pool member, one rooted at the test-induced seed — and harvests visited terminal nodes. GA/mutation/crossover contribute NOTHING at opt 1. Measured final pools (post-MCTS engine pool = the verify pool; 3 draws each): **sort 17/17/15** (distinct 14/14/12; per draw: random_seed 13/13/11, mcts_harvest 1, mcts_seed_harvest 1, TestInduced 1, Template 1); **binary_search 22/36/22** (distinct 22/36/22; random 12/14/12, mcts_harvest 4/16/4, mcts_seed_harvest 4, TestInduced 1, Template 1); **count_matching 21/20/22** (distinct 19/18/19; random 14/13/15, mcts_seed_harvest 4, mcts_harvest 1, TestInduced 1, Template 1); **is_sorted 22/19/17** (distinct 21/18/16; random 14/14/12, mcts_harvest 4/1/1, mcts_seed_harvest 2, TestInduced 1, Template 1). ConstraintInduced, Crossover, LLMGenerated: **0 in all 12 draws**. Opt ≥2 in the default build never reaches MCTS or GA at all: `seed_population` propagates `seed_from_llm`'s error (engine.rs:571) and `call_llm` errors "No LLM backend enabled" when neither `llm-local` nor `llm-remote` is compiled (Cargo.toml default features are z3-only; the llm-local arm is itself a stub error, and llm-remote posts to a localhost Ollama) — measured this phase: `synthesize(spec, 2)` and `synthesize(spec, 3)` both return `Err(LLM error: No LLM backend enabled)`. There is no opt ≥2 configuration that skips the LLM seed, so MCTS/GA cannot run LLM-free above opt 1 today.
> **(2) Why the pools miss — classification (a): the needed shapes are never proposed.** Shape census over all 231 distinct pool bodies from the probe (Debug-repr scan): **Index 0, Map 0, Filter 0, Fold 0, Sort 0, Lambda/Closure 0, Assign 0, Alloc 0, self-calls 0**. `Call` appears only in sort (2/draw) and binary_search (5/draw) bodies — the test-induced seed's converted expected outputs (`Some(..)`, list literals), never element access or recursion. For/While do appear in random bodies, but only as statement-position loops from `gen_typed_stmt` whose bodies are discarded expression statements — they cannot accumulate, index, or build output. The cause is the typed generator's documented menu (engine.rs:2231ff): fresh bodies are built only from literals, exactly-typed variables, BinOp/UnaryOp over scalar operands, literal collections, and If/Match/Let/Block/Return tails — no Call, no Index, no Len, no Concat, no higher-order nodes — because those shapes are what the Rust emitter and the conservative type verdict can stand behind. Per spec: **sort** — a correct body needs element permutation (recursion/iteration + construction, or the IR's own `Sort` node); pools contain identity (`list`), `vec![]`, and constant-conditional variants of both (Phase 56 crown files), which is exactly the menu's List repertoire for a generic element type. **count_matching** — a correct body must inspect elements (`Index`/iteration + comparison + counting); the menu offers only arithmetic over `threshold`, so crowns are constant-0 equivalents passing only the none-match test (1/3, Phase 53/56). **binary_search** — return type `Option<I64>` has NO typed literal menu (`typed_literal_fillings` returns None for Option), so roots fall to typed holes or legacy-menu fillings over the generic parameters (`needle + haystack`); crowns are verdict-Unknown, rustc-ill-typed. **is_sorted** — the menu cannot compare adjacent elements, so the best available body is constant `true`, which the test set rewards 4/5. Not (b): no correct-shaped body exists in any scanned pool to be scored away (Phase 53's compiled pool scans: 0 pass-all in 44 sort + 44 count_matching distinct bodies; this probe's census shows the shapes are absent, not outscored). Not (c): the IR vocabulary expresses all of it (`IRNode::Index/Fold/Sort/Call/Assign/Alloc/For`, engine.rs:122ff) — the generators' menus and the interpreter's subset exclude it, and the one library that contains the algorithms (`templates.rs`: divide-and-conquer sort, binary search, fold, linear scan) is uncalled and stale against current signatures (its sort recursion passes one argument to a two-parameter spec; its binary search returns a tuple-wrapped index and `Var("None")`).
> **(3) Constraint-seed applicability — no existing family admits any of the four, and for two of them no constraint family could.** The families rescue specs whose constraints ARE bodies: an `output == <expr>` equation, or Or-rows of scalar guard leaves + an output pin. Parsed postconditions (probe-printed): **sort** = `Predicate("is_permutation")`, a `Forall` over indices whose body calls the fn parameter as a predicate, `len(output) == len(list)` (an equation about lengths, not about `output`), and an `Implies` over `Predicate("is_sorted")` — no top-level `output ==` equation, no Or-rows. **count_matching** = a `Forall` one-way implication (`x > threshold` implies `output > 0`) + two bounds (`output >= 0`, `output <= len(list)`) — these constrain but do not determine a count; the counting construction appears nowhere in them. **binary_search** = a `Forall` over the output-as-index + an `Implies` into a `Forall`. **is_sorted** = an `Implies` + two `Forall`s over adjacent indices. Verdicts: sort and binary_search need an ALGORITHM (search/sort construction) — the honest seed source is the signature, not the constraints (template territory, item 2). is_sorted likewise (an adjacent-pair scan). count_matching is the one spec with a derivable construction: a future **forall-fold family** could mine the quantified predicate (`x > threshold`) from the `Forall` and build a fold/count body from it — a genuinely new family under the Phase 17/49 discipline, whose body (Fold node or loop+accumulator) would still sit outside the interpreter's evaluable subset and the typed generator's menu, so it must be judged by the compiled vehicle (item 5b) or not at all.
> **(4) Variation operators — five of six are empty, the sixth only deletes, and mutation is unreachable at opt 1 anyway.** `mutate` (engine.rs:1197) draws among six kinds: kind 0 `un_synthesize` is the only working one — and it is crude: `random_subtree` returns a clone of the WHOLE body (engine.rs:1861, "Simplified"), so the operator holes large structure rather than editing a chosen subtree. Kinds 1-5 — `inline_random_call`, `swap_equivalent`, `optimize_loop`, `add_parallel_hint`, `random_rewrite` — are empty function bodies (engine.rs:5368-5372, under the literal header "Stub helpers"). `mutate` and `crossover` are called only from `genetic_refine` (engine.rs:1119/1152), which `run` invokes only at opt ≥3 — a configuration the default build cannot reach (item 1). So the `Mutation` provenance in opt-1 pools is random seeds and MCTS harvest labels, never `mutate`. Even implemented, the stubs are local perturbations: no local rewrite of `list`, `true`, or `0` invents element iteration with accumulation. Mutation pays off only AFTER structural bodies exist to mutate from; it is not a prerequisite for closing the gap.
> **(5) Fn-typed evaluation — three options, measured.** Today (option c, status quo): sort's and binary_search's tests never bind (`bind_test_env` excludes any tuple element `value_of_expr` cannot map; a lambda lowers through `expr_to_ir` to a Hole), so `evaluate_candidate` (engine.rs:5169) scores every hole-free body by the FALLBACK tpr 1.0, and ranking among crowns falls to the complexity/quality tie-breakers. The verifier adds nothing for sort: probe verdicts over 3 draws were **Inconclusive for every hole-free candidate and Malformed for the 2 holed seeds, 0 Verified, 0 VerificationFailed**, with `constraint_score` overwritten by `verify_all` to **0.000 for every candidate** — all four postconditions come back Unknown (Forall/Predicate have no encoding, verifier.rs:883-887; even `len(output) == len(list)` is unencodable because list parameters are symbolic-opaque to the encoder). For count_matching the verifier CAN refute (1-7 VerificationFailed per draw, via the list-free bounds) and is_sorted's crowns are honestly measured by tests (tpr 0.800) — the blind spot is exactly the fn-typed pair. **(a) Function values in the interpreter (M):** add a `Value::Function` variant (params + body + captured env) — `Value` is `#[derive(PartialEq)]` over plain data (eval.rs:28) while `IRNode` has no `PartialEq`, so the derive breaks and equality must be hand-written (the Debug-fingerprint precedent in `readmit_protected_seeds`) or functions must compare never-equal; `value_of_expr`/`expr_to_ir` must learn to lower a Lambda to a closure value (today it becomes a Hole); `eval_flow`'s Lambda/Closure arms (currently NotEvaluable) must return the closure, and `eval_call` must apply a callee resolved from the environment, not only the `List`/`len` builtins. Other `Value` consumers are safe (m3_gate's matches carry catch-alls). Unlock: sort/binary_search tests bind and expression-subset bodies score truthfully everywhere including the MCTS reward. Limit, stated plainly: bodies using loops, Assign/Alloc, or Map/Filter/Fold/Sort nodes remain NotEvaluable and would score 0 — so (a) alone would rank the identity body (evaluable, 3/6) ABOVE a correct loop-based sort (unevaluable, 0/6); it must pair with subset expansion or with (b). **(b) Compiled-runner scoring at the final stage (M):** the Phase 53 instrument's vehicle, productized — after MCTS, render each finalist, compile function + a runner generated from the spec's tests, execute, and overwrite tpr before selection (the `verify_all` rescoring precedent). It judges real Rust, so it has no subset limit and needs no interpreter change; cost is one rustc invocation per finalist (~20 per draw — fine at the final stage, impossible inside the MCTS reward loop, which keeps the interpreter/FALLBACK signal during search; that divergence must be recorded as policy: the compiled vehicle is the behavioral ground truth where it runs). Precedents: Phase 53's runners, the tokenizer cross-validation's compiled runners. Its runner generation inherits the generated-test-module rendering questions — Phase 55 fixed tuple/lambda rendering; the remaining hole is item 6's E0283. **(c) Leave unbindable:** measured above — FALLBACK 1.0 actively masks junk as perfect on the two flagship specs; acceptable only under the null option (item 7) with the liability recorded. **Recommendation: (b) first, (a) only if interpreter-side truth inside MCTS is later required.**
> **(6) The E0283 and the ill-typed crowns — different owners.** **E0283 is signature/test-rendering side, not generation or selection:** the generated signature comes from the SPEC (`sort<T>` with no trait bound, `cmp: fn(T, T) -> bool`), identical for every draw, and Phase 56 measured E0283 in 5/5 draws with five different winner bodies; Phase 53's hand-written runner, calling `sort::<i64>` with a concretely typed closure, compiled the same class of winners 10/12. No pool body can avoid it and no crown choice changes it; the fix is a test-rendering annotation (turbofish/concrete element type from the test's own literals) — an emitter-contract amendment, owner territory by the Phase 54/55 precedent — or runner-side annotation under 5(b). **binary_search's ill-typed crowns are generation-side with a selection-side amplifier:** the root cause is item 2 (no Option construction in the typed menu; verdict-Unknown arithmetic over generic parameters). The amplifier, measured by this probe's rustc pool scan: **11-15 of 22-36 distinct pool bodies DO compile as functions in every draw**, but the compiling bodies the scan surfaces are holed placeholders (verdict Malformed; tpr 0.500 = FALLBACK × ½ hole factor; constraint 0.350; composite ≈ 0.507) while the crowned mcts_harvest winner (composite 0.900 via FALLBACK tpr 1.000) does not compile — and no stage of selection prices compilability at all (the completeness gate excludes the holed placeholders when hole-free candidates exist; the return-type gate treats the winner's Unknown verdict as neutral). For sort the same scan shows compilability is not the binding constraint (10-12 of 12-14 distinct bodies compile; 0 pass 6/6, Phase 53).
> **(7) Campaign plan — slices, sizes, decision points, and the null option.** Ordered: **G1 (M)** compiled-runner final scoring for unbindable specs (item 5b) — success signal: sort/binary_search crowns' recorded tpr equals their compiled pass ratio (identity earns 3/6, not FALLBACK 1.0); blast radius: the scoring stage only, MCTS reward unchanged by recorded policy. **G2 (M)** algorithm-template seeding: wire the template library into `seed_from_templates` after repairing it to current signatures/IR semantics (the divide-and-conquer sort must thread the comparator; the binary-search template's return shape must be fixed), and first measure whether the `Sort`/`Fold` IR nodes render and execute — success signal: pools contain a template-derived body passing all in-spec tests under the compiled vehicle for at least 2 of the 4 specs. **G3 (M)** the forall-fold constraint family for the count_matching shape (item 3), under the seed discipline (failing tests first, territory disjointness, fixture discipline) — success signal: count_matching crowns pass 3/3. **G4 (S/M)** fn-typed interpreter values (item 5a) — success signal: sort/binary_search `evaluable > 0` and expression-subset bodies score truthfully in MCTS; NOT a prerequisite if G1 lands. **G5 (S)** Option-typed generation discipline (typed fillings that construct `Some`/`None`; possibly a tightened return-type verdict) — success signal: binary_search crowns compile in ≥4/5 draws. **G6 (S)** the E0283 test-render annotation — success signal: sort's generated test module compiles 5/5. **G7 (M, optional strengthening)** implement the five mutation stubs and decouple GA reachability from the LLM seed — pays off only after G2/G3 supply viable bodies. **Prerequisites for checkbox 3, precisely:** a correct sort body in the pool (G2, or a sort-shaped G3-style construction), truthful crowning (G1 or G4), and a compiling test leg (G6, or runner-based judgment as Phase 53 already practiced). G5/G7 are strengthening, not prerequisites. **Decision points for the USER (language owner):** (i) G2's philosophy — whether signature-keyed algorithm templates are an acceptable meaning of "the compiler invents an implementation", versus deriving bodies from constraints only; (ii) G6 — another emitter-contract amendment, the Phase 55 class of decision; (iii) campaign scope itself — run G1-G3(+G6) toward checkbox 3, or take the null option. **Muse-decidable under standing delegation:** slice order and mechanics, G1's two-judge precedence recording, G3's family design under the seed discipline, G5's menu work, all instrumentation. **The null option, priced honestly:** skip the campaign; M5 proceeds to S3 (whose held-out component the Phase 50 mechanism lets us choose in a seedable equation/guard-table shape, so checkbox 1 can still be earned) and S4 (SC3 recorded measurement + gate closing) with checkbox 3 left open and this audit as its complete ledger. The cost: the flagship examples remain demo-grade — sort and binary_search crown plausible-looking junk under FALLBACK tpr 1.000 scores, a standing honesty liability against any claim that the language synthesizes its own examples. **Blast radius:** none — docs only; the temporary probe was removed and `src/main.rs` restored before this commit. **Suite: 378 passed / 0 failed / 18 ignored, identical under `--features bootstrap-codegen`** (re-verified after the probe's removal). Local branch only, not pushed.
> **Phase 58 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/g1-runner-scoring`) — G1 LANDED: compiled-runner final scoring for interpreter-unbindable specs (Phase 57 item 5b, the audit's first campaign slice). The user approved the full G1+G2+G3+G6 campaign (2026-11-21). Checkbox 3 stays open — its truthful-crowning prerequisite is now discharged; the remaining ones are G2 (a correct sort body in the pool) and G6 (the shipped test module's E0283).** **The stage** (`src/synthesis/runner.rs`, wired into `src/main.rs` between `verify_all` and `select_best` — the `verify_all` re-scoring precedent's shape): **trigger** — the spec HAS in-spec tests and the interpreter cannot bind all of them (`eval::bindable_test_count(spec) < tests.len()`; binding is a property of the test encodings alone, so the count is candidate-independent). Today the trigger fires for exactly sort and binary_search; when every test binds, the returned pool is byte-identical to the input (hard invariant — the M2/M3 instruments' winners are fully bindable and their measurements are unchanged, below). **Finalists** — top **k = 8** (`RUNNER_FINALISTS`) by the existing composite ranking, with the would-be winner (what `select_best` crowns on the un-rescored pool) guaranteed a slot, taking the last finalist's place if it ranked outside the top-k. **The runner** — each finalist is rendered through the production codegen (`codegen::generate`, the same output the build ships; the frozen emitter is untouched and unconsumed-but-unmodified), and a runner main is generated whose per-test invocations are **re-read from generate's own generated test module** (the one true test-value rendering: tuple spread, lambda and `List` forms included) and annotated with a turbofish of the concrete element type derived from the spec's own test literals — the Phase 53 instrument's harness pattern, which is what lets an empty-list test compile at all (the shipped module's own E0283 fix remains G6's). The invocations are count-checked against the spec's test count: any drift in generate's test-module shape disables the stage for that spec rather than mis-scoring it. Each test runs under `catch_unwind` (a panicking candidate fails that test); the runner prints one `TOTAL p/t` line. Budgets (Phase 53 precedent): 60 s compile, 10 s run. **Ranking semantics (explicit)** — a finalist's measured ratio replaces its `test_pass_ratio`, times the same per-hole factor `evaluate_candidate` applies; a finalist that fails to compile or whose runner times out scores **0** (compilability is priced by construction; a per-candidate timeout is a verdict about the candidate's own code, not an infrastructure failure). Non-finalists keep their FALLBACK score but are **capped at the worst finalist's measured ratio** — an unmeasured candidate may not claim a higher test score than the worst measured finalist, so no unscored fallback-1.0 can outrank a runner-scored finalist on the tpr dimension (8–11 non-finalists capped per sort draw; 13–72 per binary_search draw, where the cap is 0). **Degradation** — infrastructure failure (rustc unspawnable, temp dir unwritable, generate's test module unreadable in its expected shape) degrades the WHOLE spec to today's fallback behavior, pool unchanged; the stage never fails a build. **Policy (Phase 57's recorded two-judge precedence)** — where the runner runs, the compiled vehicle is the behavioral ground truth; the MCTS reward loop keeps the interpreter/FALLBACK signal during search (one rustc per candidate is impossible there). **Measured** (`src/phase58_measurement.rs`, ignored; report at `target/phase58/measurement-report.md`; 5 draws per spec through synthesize → verify_all → stage → select_best, crowns judged by the Phase 53 compiled vehicle; 2 control draws each for count_matching/is_sorted): **sort** — the crown's recorded tpr is **0.500 = its compiled 3/6 in 5/5 draws** (was FALLBACK 1.000); the crown CHANGED in 3/5 draws, in each case from a body passing 1/6 to one passing 3/6; finalist verdicts spread 0/6–3/6 within every draw. 3/6 is the pool's honest ceiling (the identity class) — no correct sort body exists until G2, stated as expected, not papered over. **binary_search** — the crown's recorded tpr is **0.000 = its compiled pass ratio in 5/5 draws** (was FALLBACK 1.000); the crown's identity is unchanged in 5/5, and a pool scan (2 draws) shows why no scoring stage could change it today: **0 hole-free bodies compile** (85 distinct / 68 hole-free and 23 distinct / 8 hole-free scanned); the only compiling bodies — 12 per draw — are holed placeholders that pass **0/7** under the runner and are barred from crowning by the completeness gate while hole-free candidates exist. Compiling binary_search crowns are therefore G5's/G2's deliverable, exactly where the Phase 57 plan put them (G5's success signal is binary_search crowns compiling ≥4/5 draws); G1's own success signal — both crowns' recorded tpr equals their compiled pass ratio — **holds for both specs**. **Controls** — count_matching and is_sorted: `NotTriggered` on every draw, every candidate's scores byte-identical, stage wall 11–29 µs. **Cost** — per affected build: sort ~1.1–1.2 s typical (2 of 5 draws measured 11.2 s — the stage's only 10 s quantum is the run timeout, i.e. one non-terminating finalist per such draw paying it in full; the 60 s compile timeout was never approached); binary_search ~0.6–0.7 s (fast compile failures); unaffected specs pay the trigger check only (~µs, above). **Phase 56 instrument re-run through the real binary** (which now includes the stage): sort parse 5/5, whole-file 5/5, function 5/5, test module 0/5 (E0283 — G6, unchanged); binary_search parse 5/5, function 0/5 (crowns still ill-typed pool junk — E0369/E0308/E0531 classes — now recorded at tpr 0.000 instead of masked at 1.000); count_matching compiles 4/5 (one E0531 `__tuple__` junk-draw crown — draw variance in a spec the stage provably does not touch), tests execute 1/3 in the other draws; is_sorted unchanged (5/5 compiles, 4/5 executed). **Blast radius:** suite **384 passed / 0 failed / 20 ignored in BOTH configurations** (378 baseline + 6 new runner unit tests; ignored +2 — the runner end-to-end miniature and this phase's instrument; the miniature asserts the vehicle end-to-end: identity 2/3 beats constant-empty 1/3 and crowns, from FALLBACK 1.0/1.0 before the stage). M2 probe + cross-validation unchanged (golden 64/64 + pinned 539/539, every agreement fraction full); M3 probe + cross-validation unchanged (seeds **113/116** with exactly the by-design seedless trio, Verified winners 116/116 in all 3 runs, golden **352/352** + pinned **2990/2990** per run); S2 roundtrip (a) re-run PASS in both configurations (4/4 byte-identical); seed-body fixture untouched; `src/codegen/` untouched. > **Phase 59 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/g2-template-seeding`) — G2 LANDED: algorithm-template seeding, structurally keyed. Checkbox 3 stays open — G2 connects real algorithm bodies to the pool for all three target specs, but each spec's crown is now blocked by a DIFFERENT, precisely located wall (below); none of the three walls is a seeding failure.** **What was there (Phase 57 answer 1, verified):** the engine's three template builders returned bare `Hole`s, `templates.rs` (v0.4) had no production caller and its shapes were stale (a divide-and-conquer passing 1 argument to a 2-param spec, a binary search returning a tuple-wrapped index, pseudo-calls — "first"/"rest"/"concat"/"transform"/"push"/"combine" — that render as undefined free functions, two-pointer detection keyed on the spec NAME). **Support matrix (measured first, temporary probe, hand-built bodies judged by every vehicle):** the IR **Sort** node renders `{ let mut sorted = list; sorted.sort_by(cmp); sorted }` but rustc rejects it **E0631** against the generated signature `cmp: fn(T, T) -> bool` — `sort_by` wants `FnMut(&T, &T) -> Ordering`; an adapter lambda fails **E0507** (the comparator is applied to values behind shared references), a clone-adapter fails **E0308**, and `std::cmp::Ordering` values are **unconstructible** in the rendered vocabulary (`rust_ident` sanitizes `::`/`.` to `_`, and generated signatures carry no trait bounds). A filter-quicksort Sort variant is worse (E0308 + E0277: `.iter().filter(..).collect()` yields `Vec<&T>`). **Index/For/Alloc/Assign bodies** (the scan forms) render compilable Rust and typecheck (selector verdict Matches); the **interpreter** evaluates Index/BinOp/If/Let/Match but **not** While/For/Loop/Alloc/Assign/Lambda/Map/Filter/Fold/Sort (NotEvaluable) and rejects spec/self calls; **Z3** encodes none of the HOF/loop/mutation nodes (Inconclusive for every template body — neutral, never a refutation); the selector's `infer_ir_type` returns None for HOF/loop nodes → verdict **Unknown** (neutral, not Mismatched). **Fold** renders but its closure binds `&i64` where the IR body uses the element by value → **E0308** (the IR has no Deref node). **Option does not block**: `Call("Some",[x])` renders `Some(x)` and `Var("None")` renders `None`. **Index typing does**: a computed index is `i64` and cannot index a Vec (only `usize`; the spec language has no usize type — For-loop variables over `0..len` infer usize and work, which is why the scan forms compile and the binary-search loop does not). **The library** (`src/synthesis/templates.rs`, rewritten): `TemplateKind::{SortPrimitive, AdjacentScan, BinarySearch}`, instantiated from the spec's actual parameter names/types with collision-safe local names; a template that cannot instantiate cleanly does not fire. **Keying rule (structural only, NEVER spec names — recorded per the campaign approval):** sort-primitive fires iff return type is `List<E>` ∧ some param is `List<E>` (same E) ∧ some param is `Function([E, E], Bool)`; binary-search iff return is `Option(_)` ∧ params[0] is `List<E>` ∧ params[1] == E ∧ some param is `Function([E, E], Int)`; adjacent-scan iff exactly one param, `List<E>` with E a concrete ordered scalar (Int/I8–I64/U8–U64/Float/Char/String — Bool, Generic and Named excluded) ∧ return Bool. At most one template per spec. **Corpus inventory (142 specs, 8 files): the firing set is exactly {sort → SortPrimitive, binary_search → BinarySearch, is_sorted → AdjacentScan}** (asserted in the instrument) — no M2/M3/tokenizer/example spec fires. The three bare-Hole engine builders and `has_list_type` are deleted; `seed_from_templates` consumes the library.
> **Dedup — the hazard bit, indirectly, and is fixed.** `ir_equivalent` compares only root-variant discriminants (Phase 57 answer 1). It does NOT collapse the new template seeds (≤1 per spec; the targets have no constraint seeds). But removing the bare Holes removed a side effect nobody had recorded: in seed order the Hole sat between the test-induced seed (a Match body) and the constraint seeds, acting as a **discriminant buffer** — the moment it left, `seed_population`'s `dedup_by(ir_equivalent)` began eating Match-rooted **guard_table** constraint seeds standing next to the Match-rooted test-induced seed, before the protected-seed snapshot is taken (readmission cannot restore what the snapshot never saw). Measured pre-fix: M2/M3 guard-table winners (type_tag_name, rust_type_name, …) collapsed to 0-agreement Mutation bodies. A failing-first regression test (`guard_table_seed_survives_seed_population_dedup`, printer/type_tag_name) proves the bite; the **minimal fix** changes only the dedup call site to true-duplicate identity by Debug fingerprint (`ir_identical`) — the identity `readmit_protected_seeds` already uses. `ir_equivalent` itself is untouched: `replace_subtree` keeps it as its documented approximation.
> **Measured** (`src/phase59_measurement.rs`, ignored; report at `target/phase59/measurement-report.md`; 5 draws per spec through the full pipeline, crowns and template bodies judged by the Phase 53/58 compiled vehicle; re-run after the dedup fix — every number identical): **sort** — template in pool **5/5**, its compiled verdict **0/6** in every draw (E0631, the comparator contract above: the wall is the generated SIGNATURE `fn(T, T) -> bool` vs the standard library's sort interface — a signature/emitter-contract matter, owner-class, G6 territory; no IR body expressible today sorts through it). Crown is the template **0/5**; crowns stay Mutation at recorded tpr **0.500** (compiled 3/6 each, **15/30** total) — **unchanged from Phase 58**. **binary_search** — template in pool **5/5**, verdict **0/7** (E0308 + E0277×2: the i64-index wall and the by-value comparator applied to an Index place — NOT the Option return, which renders cleanly; G5's recorded territory, now with the two exact blockers named). The crown **IS the template body in 5/5 draws** (one identical body, recorded tpr **0.000**, does not compile): at universal tpr 0.000 the tie-breakers land on the real algorithm — **crown identity changed vs Phase 58 (was mcts_harvest junk), crown quality unchanged (0.000, 0/7)**. **is_sorted** — template in pool **5/5**, its compiled verdict **5/5 in every draw** (the correct body the brief asked for exists and compiles), crown is the template **0/5**: the interpreter cannot evaluate For/Alloc/Assign bodies, so the template's interpreter tpr is 0.0 while constant-true crowns record **0.800** (compiled 4/5 each, **20/25** total) — a judging wall in the interpreter's BODY coverage, distinct from G1's test-BINDING trigger (is_sorted's tests bind; its runner never fires). A future slice that extends compiled judging to specs whose tests bind but whose candidate bodies the interpreter cannot evaluate would crown this template; that is a judging-policy decision, not taken here. **count_matching (control, G3's territory)** — no template fires (its shape is the forall-fold family), runner NotTriggered, crowns 0.333 (compiled 1/3), unchanged. **G3 record:** count_matching's shape DOES fit a Fold template, but the Fold node renders E0308 (above); the **Alloc/For/Index scan form** — the is_sorted construction with a counter — compiles and typechecks, and is G3's viable construction when Phase 60 builds it. **Blast radius:** verified against a pristine `1b2ca94` worktree baseline (a shared-target-dir staleness trap in this sandbox briefly made instrument runs execute baseline binaries — caught by test-count arithmetic and report diffs, and recorded here so the next phase distrusts any instrument run whose filtered-test count does not match the branch): M2 probe + cross-validation **winner rows byte-identical** to baseline (golden 64/64 + pinned 539/539 per run); M3 probe + cross-validation **winner rows byte-identical** (seeds **113/116**, Verified winners **116/116**, golden **352/352** + pinned **2990/2990** per run); tokenizer probe + cross-validation unchanged (char_equation classifiers executed **774/774**); S2 roundtrip (a) PASS in both configurations; seed-body fixture untouched (template seeds are a generation source, not constraint seeds — the fixture's discipline is untouched by construction); `src/codegen/` untouched. Suite **394 passed / 0 failed / 21 ignored in BOTH configurations** (384/20 at Phase 58; +6 net template tests, +4 engine tests incl. the dedup regression test, +1 ignored instrument). Local branch only, not pushed.

> **Phase 60 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/g3-forall-fold-seeds`) — G3 LANDED: the forall-fold constraint-seed family, and count_matching crowns a correct body at a measured tpr 1.000. Checkbox 3 stays open — its count_matching leg is closed (the Phase 57 campaign's G3 success signal: "count_matching crowns pass 3/3"); what remains is sort's leg (G6, next) and the fn-typed evaluation residue.** **Admission** (the tenth constraint-seed family, kind `forall_fold`, in `seed_from_constraints`, tried LAST and claiming only specs no earlier family seeded — territory disjointness by precedence, proven by a failing-first test): the return type is in the int family; exactly two parameters, exactly one of them a List whose element type is a concrete ordered scalar (the templates' scalar set); the spec's FIRST Forall postcondition (in spec order — a deviating first Forall blocks the family even when a later one matches; non-Forall postconditions are skipped) has exactly one quantified variable, declared at the element type, and a body of the form `Implies(antecedent, consequent)` where the consequent mentions `output` and the antecedent normalizes (from either the `Constraint::Order` or the parsed `Constraint::Expr(BinOp)` form — the parser produces the latter, measured) to a single comparison with one side exactly the quantified variable and the other exactly the scalar parameter (whose type equals the element type); and the spec's in-spec tests are non-empty and ALL bind in the interpreter (Phase 53's count — the seed's own judging story, below, is only honest where the tests bind). The iterated list is inferred as the unique List parameter of the quantified variable's type — the parser's `parse_forall` discards the collection expression, so the AST carries none (recorded). **Not admitted** (each pinned by a near-miss test): compound predicates (And/Or/Not), literal thresholds, arithmetic on either side of the comparison, predicate calls, Index-form element access (is_sorted's test-induced shape), a bare Forall without implication, output-silent consequents, `Exists`, generic elements, two-List-parameter specs, missing or unbindable tests. The family is structural, never nominal — a renamed spec with the same shape fires with its own names; a scalar spec NAMED count_matching does not fire. **Construction** — the Phase 59 scan form, NOT the Fold node (E0308, recorded there): `Block[Alloc{count, Int, 0}, For{i, 0, Len(list), Assign(count, count + If(pred, 1, 0))}, Return(count)]`, where `pred` is the mined comparison with its WRITTEN orientation preserved and the element side replaced by `Index(list, i)`; local names are collision-safe derivations of "count"/"i" against the spec's parameter names. The body is hole-free and typechecks against the spec's signature (selector verdict not Mismatched). One parser fact shaped the mining: `len(list)` in a postcondition parses as a Call, and the bounds postconditions (`output >= 0`, `output <= len(list)`) are not used by the family at all — the construction comes from the Forall alone, exactly as the Phase 57 audit said the stated constraints (one-way implication + bounds) do not determine a count but the quantified predicate does. **Judging — option (a) measured dead, option (b) landed.** The seed's interpreter tpr is 0.000 by construction (the interpreter evaluates no loop bodies; count_matching's tests bind, evaluable 3, passed 0 — while constant-0 holds a measured 0.333), and the verifier says nothing: the scan body verifies **Inconclusive, constraint_score 0.000** (measured) — option (a) is dead, as the brief predicted. Option (b): the G1 runner's trigger gains a second arm in `src/synthesis/runner.rs` — when every test binds but the pool holds a **ConstraintInduced candidate the interpreter cannot evaluate on ANY bindable test** (new `eval::valued_test_count(body, spec) == 0`: binds and evaluates are different facts — a wrong-but-evaluable body returns a value on every test and never triggers), the stage scores the pool with Phase 58's exact semantics (finalists' measured ratios replace tpr × hole factor; non-finalists capped at the worst finalist's measured ratio; infrastructure failure degrades the whole spec to fallback). Triggering seeds are **guaranteed finalist slots** (`choose_finalists` gains a guaranteed set: a guaranteed seed displaces the lowest-ranked unprotected finalist, never the would-be winner, never another guaranteed seed). **Scope, recorded as a decision:** the arm covers constraint-seed provenance ONLY. Template provenance is deliberately excluded — is_sorted's adjacent-scan template sits behind the same interpreter wall, and the brief reserves "specs whose tests bind but whose candidate bodies the interpreter cannot evaluate" for a separate decision (Phase 59 recorded the same reservation); arbitrary candidates are excluded for the same reason. The provenance set is a named constant in the trigger; admitting Template later is a one-line change when the owner decides. The is_sorted control (instrument Part C) pins the exclusion: stage NotTriggered, crowns unchanged in character (Mutation, tpr 0.800, compiled 4/5).
> **Measured** (`src/phase60_measurement.rs`, ignored; report at `target/phase60/measurement-report.md`): **firing inventory over all 142 specs (8 files): forall_fold fires for exactly {count_matching}** (asserted; the full constraint-seed census is printed in the report — no M2/M3/tokenizer/standalone spec gains the seed, and count_matching's census line is exactly `["forall_fold"]`). **Draws — 5 full-pipeline draws of count_matching: the seed is in the verified pool 5/5** (Z3 Inconclusive, pre-stage tpr 0.000 every draw); the runner stage scores every draw (8 finalists, 0–3 capped non-finalists, ~0.9–1.0 s per draw); **the seed's finalist verdict is 3/3 → rescored tpr 1.000 in 5/5 draws; the crown IS the seed's body in 5/5 draws** (recorded tpr 1.000; the crown compiles and passes 3/3 under the compiled vehicle). The Phase 59 instrument's count_matching control re-records the same crown (identical body hash in both its draws). The one rival that can also pass 3/3 — the test-induced seed, when its holes happen to fill correctly — is capped at 0.5 by its hole factor and barred by the completeness gate; it never threatened the crown in any draw. **Fixture:** the seed-body golden fixture gains NO line, per its own rules — the fixture tracks resolver-visible bodies (`SpecIndex::callee_body`, first-equation bodies) for the validator/printer/codegen/tokenizer modules; count_matching has no equation postcondition, hence no resolver-visible body, and the fixture's count assertion stands at 62 unchanged (the reasoning is recorded here because the brief asked for the fixture discipline to be followed exactly — following it means no entry). **Instruments re-recorded:** the Phase 58 instrument's count_matching control is retired (the stage triggers for it by design now; is_sorted remains its byte-identical no-op control, still passing) and the Phase 59 instrument's count_matching control asserts the new crown; both instruments pass in the new world. **Blast radius** (pristine `1636b3d` worktree baseline; the baseline needed the main target's z3-sys build cache transplanted — the metadata hashes match, so the copied outputs are accepted as fresh; instrument filtered-test counts verified per branch, 293 baseline vs 306 phase, per the Phase 59 staleness lesson): M2 cross-validation report **byte-identical**; M3 cross-validation report **byte-identical**; tokenizer cross-validation — the three char_equation classifier winner rows identical (EXACT, executed **774/774** every run, deterministic crowns), and every differing row confined to the five structurally unsynthesizable specs (peek/next_char/skip_whitespace/tokenize_one/tokenize_all, junk Mutation crowns at tpr 0.500 whose winners the report itself flags non-deterministic) — proven inherent, not blast radius, by a baseline-vs-baseline rerun wobbling in exactly the same rows; S2 roundtrip (a) PASS in both configurations; `src/codegen/` untouched (zero diff). ---

## 🚫 ANTI-PATTERNS (JANGAN DILAKUKAN)

| ❌ Anti-pattern | ✅ Correct approach |
|---|---|
| Langsung sintesis komponen besar (parser 1000+ lines) | Decompose jadi sub-fungsi ≤200 lines |
| Percaya hasil Z3 tanpa cross-validation | Selalu cross-validate dengan compiler original |
| Hapus kode Rust asli setelah generated code masuk | Keep both, gunakan feature flag untuk switch |
| Satu branch untuk semua komponen | Satu branch per komponen, merge bertahap |
| Skip benchmark karena "masih prototype" | Benchmark setiap komponen, tetapkan baseline |
| Gunakan generated compiler untuk generate compiler lain | Gunakan Rust compiler original sebagai bootstrap |
| Spesifikasi terlalu longgar → hasil synthesis valid tapi salah | Spesifikasi ketat dengan golden test suite |

---

> **Phase 61 (2026-11-21, branch `feat/g6-sort-leg`) — G6 LANDED (the E0283 test-render annotation, placed SPECCED) and the SortPrimitive template rebuilt in scan form; sort's shipped leg is closed, but checkbox 3 stays open — the crown now waits on ONE owner decision, priced below: a trait bound on the generated generic signature.** **Part 1 — the template.** `build_sort_primitive` (`src/synthesis/templates.rs`) no longer builds the IR Sort node (Phase 59: renders `sort_by(cmp)` → E0631). The new body is a bubble sort from the proven scan vocabulary: `Alloc` a working list bound to the moved `list` parameter, nested `For` loops over `0..len` / `1..len`, an `If` on a DIRECT fn-pointer call `cmp(clone(a[j]), clone(a[j-1]))` (element reads are clone-mediated `UnaryOp::Clone` on Index places), and a swap of `Let tmp` + two `Assign`s on Index places; locals are collision-safe (`fresh_name`). Keying is unchanged (the structural SortPrimitive rule), the provenance name is unchanged, the body is hole-free and typechecks against sort's signature (selector verdict Matches). **The probe (instrument Part A, through production codegen + rustc) is the phase's hard fact:** the generated signature is `pub fn sort<T>(list: Vec<T>, cmp: fn(T, T) -> bool) -> Vec<T>` with NO bounds on `T` — the parser has no bounds syntax (`GenericParam.bounds` is always empty) and `generate_rust` renders generic names only. Under that signature **no construction compiles**: (A) bubble sort with plain index reads → **E0507** ×4 (cannot move out of an index of `Vec<T>`); (B) the shipped clone-mediated construction → **E0599** (no `clone` on unbounded `T`); (C) insertion sort by push extraction → **E0507**; (D) the old Sort node → **E0631**. Assign-to-Index-place TARGETS render and assign lawfully — the wall is element READS (comparator arguments, swap sources), not the swap machinery. **The priced unlock:** construction B's generated text under a probe-only signature patch to `sort<T: Clone>` **compiles and passes 6/6** under the compiled vehicle; construction A under the same patch still dies E0507 (Clone is not Copy — plain reads stay illegal). Granting the bound is an owner-class signature/emitter-contract decision (it changes every generic signature's rendering contract, or adds bounds syntax to the language) and was NOT taken in this phase. **Finalist question, resolved as the brief prescribed:** pre-guarantee draws put the template in the pool 5/5 but among the runner's finalists **0/5** (composite ranking leaves it out), so the Phase 60 guaranteed-finalist mechanism is extended to Template provenance **within an already-triggered stage only** (`guaranteed_finalists` in `src/synthesis/runner.rs`, scoped per trigger arm: the UnbindableTests arm guarantees Template candidates; the ConstructedSeeds arm guarantees exactly its seeds, unchanged). The TRIGGER itself is untouched — is_sorted's stage still never fires (re-verified NotTriggered, crowns unchanged), its wall stays reserved for the owner decision Phase 60 recorded. One interaction found by measurement: the runner's `annotate_call` now skips calls whose text already carries `::<` (post-G6 the shipped module annotates its own calls; re-annotating would degrade the stage to InfraFailure for sort).
